## 1510090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、我を信じよ。滅びの日は近い。<line-break>その日に我を信じるもののみが<line-break>救われるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Only believe in me. The day of destruction draws near. On that day, only those who believe in me shall be saved.<end>"
KO: "오직 나를 믿어라. 멸망의 날은 가깝다. 그날 나를 믿는 자만이 구원받는다.<end>"

## 1510091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…なぜ、助ける？<line-break>僕が告死天使の長であることは<line-break>知っているのだろう？<line-break>…なぜ、助ける？<line-break>僕は、敵、だ。<end>"
EN: "Why save me? You know I'm the leader of the Angels of Death, don't you? Why save me? I'm... an enemy.<end>"
KO: "…왜 돕는 거지? 내가 고사천사의 우두머리라는 건 알고 있겠지? …왜 돕는 거지? 나는 적이다.<end>"

## 1510092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は、愚かだ。<line-break>…<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "You're foolish... <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "넌 어리석어. …<value:$28>.<end>"

## 1510093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君は君がやっていることに<line-break>気がついていない。<line-break>…君は、愚かだ。<end>"
EN: "You don't realize what you're doing. You are a fool.<end>"
KO: "자네는 자신이 무슨 일을 하는지 모르고 있어. …자네는 어리석다.<end>"

## 1510094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ありが…とう。<end>"
EN: "Thank... you.<end>"
KO: "…고마…워.<end>"

## 1510095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神は怒りたもう。<line-break>タルテュバよ、<line-break>この業はすべてお前に還るぞ。　　<line-break>お前は怪物のような姿になり果て、<line-break>死ぬことになる。<end>"
EN: "God is wroth. Tartuba, all your deeds shall return upon you. You will come to resemble a monster, and you will die.<end>"
KO: "신께서 노하셨다. 타르튜바여, 이 업보는 모두 네게 돌아갈 것이다. 너는 괴물 같은 모습이 되어 죽게 될 것이다.<end>"

## 1510096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を！<end>"
EN: "What?!<end>"
KO: "뭐라고!<end>"

## 1510097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、うぅ。　　　　　　　　<end>"
EN: "U-ugh...<end>"
KO: "으, 으으….<end>"

## 1510098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くそっ！<line-break>貴様、覚えてろ！<end>"
EN: "Damn you! You'll pay for this!<end>"
KO: "젠장! 네놈, 두고 보자!<end>"

## 1510099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "女よ。ひとつ聞く。<line-break>お前は今でも信じているか？<end>"
EN: "Woman, answer me one thing. Do you still believe?<end>"
KO: "여자여. 하나 묻겠다. 너는 지금도 믿고 있나?<end>"

## 1510100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ならば、神は<line-break>汝の願いをかなえるだろう。<line-break>　<line-break>少年よ、立て。<end>"
EN: "Then God shall grant your wish. Boy, arise.<end>"
KO: "그렇다면 신은 그대의 소원을 이루어 줄 것이다. 소년이여, 일어서라.<end>"

## 1510101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ママ！<end>"
EN: "Mama!<end>"
KO: "엄마!<end>"

## 1510102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奇跡だ！<end>"
EN: "It's a miracle!<end>"
KO: "기적이야!<end>"

## 1510103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴族に斬り殺された子供が甦った！<end>"
EN: "The child slain by a noble has come back to life!<end>"
KO: "귀족에게 베여 죽은 아이가 되살아났다!<end>"

## 1510104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、我を信じよ。滅びの日は近い。<line-break>その日に我を信じるもののみが<line-break>救われるのだ。<end>"

## 1510105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さては、お前らみんな仲間だな！　<line-break>　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I see it now―you are all in league together!<end>"
KO: "보아하니 너희 전부 한패구나!<end>"

## 1510106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えぇ！<line-break>何を言い出すんですか！？　　<end>"
EN: "What!? How can you say that!?<end>"
KO: "네!? 무슨 말을 하시는 겁니까!?<end>"

## 1510107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな茶番にだまされるものか！　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>このわしに恥をかかせて<line-break>ただで済むと思うなよ！<end>"
EN: "You think this farce can fool me!? You will not get away with humiliating me!<end>"
KO: "이런 연극에 속을 것 같으냐! 나에게 망신을 주고 무사할 줄 알지 마라!<end>"

## 1510108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってくださいよー。　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Wait, please!<end>"
KO: "기다려 주세요ー.<end>"

## 1510109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、なんじゃと！<line-break>いったいどこに！？<end>"
EN: "W-What did you say!? Where were they!?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐라고! 대체 어디에!?<end>"

## 1510110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だんな様が昨日お召しになった<line-break>服のポケットにございました。<end>"
EN: "It was in the pocket of what you wore yesterday, master.<end>"
KO: "주인님께서 어제 입으신 옷 주머니에 있었습니다.<end>"

## 1510111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec151-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "뭐라고!? 이 애송이, 아무 말이나 지껄이지 마라…!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "노래 한 곡 불러주겠나?<end>"

## 1520004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec152-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここには姉さんの墓がある。<line-break>僕の大事な大事な姉さんの…。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>姉さんはここで望まない死を遂げて、<line-break>ここに葬られた。<end>"
EN: "My sister is buried here. My dear, dear sister... She died here, though she did not want to die, and was laid to rest here.<end>"
KO: "여기에는 누나의 무덤이 있어. 내 소중하고 소중한 누나의…. 누나는 여기서 원치 않는 죽음을 맞고 이곳에 묻혔어.<end>"

## 1520005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec152-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いや、姉さんは死を望んでいた。<line-break>それも、僕が許せない理由で…。<line-break>　<line-break>もう姉さんは僕にほほえまない。<line-break>だけど僕以外の奴にもほほえまない…。<line-break>フフ、公平だろ？<end>"
EN: "No... She wanted to die. And for a reason I can't forgive... She'll never smile at me again. But she won't smile at anyone else, either... Heh. Fair, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "…아니, 누나는 죽음을 원했어. 그것도 내가 용서할 수 없는 이유로…. 이제 누나는 내게 미소 짓지 않아. 하지만 나 말고 다른 녀석에게도 미소 짓지 않지…. 후후, 공평하잖아?<end>"

## 1520006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec152-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だけど、無意味だ。<end>"
EN: "...But it's meaningless.<end>"
KO: "…하지만 무의미해.<end>"

## 1520008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec152-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "優しい歌を…ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Thank you... for that gentle song.<end>"
KO: "다정한 노래를… 고마워.<end>"

## 1530001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "天空神ノトゥーンよ。<line-break>アンギルダン将軍とともに出征した<line-break>私の恋人ラルフの安否を教えてください。<line-break>どうか、その大いなるお力をもって…<line-break>お示しください…。<line-break>どうか…。<end>"
EN: "O Notun, the Sky God. Please tell me what has become of my beloved Ralph, who went to war with General Angeerdan. By your great power, I beg you... show me. Please...<end>"
KO: "천공신 노툰이여. 앙길단 장군과 함께 출정한 제 연인 랄프의 안부를 알려 주소서. 부디 그 위대한 힘으로… 보여 주소서…. 부디….<end>"

## 1530002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "マータ、僕だよ、ラルフだよ。<end>"
EN: "Marta, it's me. It's Ralph.<end>"
KO: "마타, 나야, 랄프야.<end>"

## 1530003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラルフ！？<end>"
EN: "Ralph?!<end>"
KO: "랄프!?<end>"

## 1530004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴメンね、マータ。<line-break>僕は、君とは、もう、会えない。　　<line-break>かなしいけれど、僕は戦死した。<line-break>君は悲しむことだろう。<line-break>だけど、僕のことはあきらめてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry, Marta. I can't ever see you again. It grieves me to say it, but I was killed in battle. I know you'll mourn me, but please... let me go.<end>"
KO: "미안해, 마타. 난 이제 너와 만날 수 없어. 슬프지만 나는 전사했어. 너는 슬퍼하겠지. 하지만 나를 포기해 줘.<end>"

## 1530005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや！<line-break>そんな、そんなのイヤ！<line-break>ラルフ、そんな！<end>"
EN: "No! No, I won't accept that! Ralph, you can't...!<end>"
KO: "싫어! 그런 건, 그런 건 싫어! 랄프, 그러지 마!<end>"

## 1530006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こうして死んでもなお会いに行くことが<line-break>君をいっそう悲しませるのではないかと<line-break>僕は悩んでいた。<line-break>だけど、ゴメン、マータ。<line-break>君のことがずっと心に掛かっていた。<line-break>君に最後に会わずにいられなかった。<end>"
EN: "I was afraid that coming to see you, even after my death, would only cause you more pain. But I'm sorry, Marta. I couldn't stop worrying about you. I had to see you one last time.<end>"
KO: "이렇게 죽은 뒤에도 찾아가는 게 오히려 너를 더 슬프게 할까 고민했어. 하지만 미안해, 마타. 네가 계속 마음에 걸렸어. 마지막으로 널 보지 않을 수 없었어.<end>"

## 1530007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勝手な言いぐさだけど、<line-break>僕のことでいつまでも悲しまないで<line-break>早く元気になってくれると、うれしい。<line-break>それじゃ…<line-break>最後に会えてよかった。<line-break>マータ…。<end>"
EN: "I know it's selfish of me to say this, but it would make me happy if you didn't grieve over me forever and could get back to your old self soon. Well... I'm glad I got to see you one last time. Marta...<end>"
KO: "제멋대로인 말이지만, 나 때문에 언제까지나 슬퍼하지 말고 빨리 기운을 차려 줬으면 좋겠어. 그럼… 마지막으로 만나서 다행이야. 마타….<end>"

## 1530008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ラルフ…。<end>"
EN: "Ralph...<end>"
KO: "랄프….<end>"

## 1530009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…おせっかいだな。<line-break>死者の願いなど<line-break>無視するべきなのだ。<end>"
EN: "How meddlesome. The wishes of the dead ought to be ignored.<end>"
KO: "…쓸데없는 참견이군. 죽은 자의 소원 따위는 무시해야 하는 거야.<end>"

## 1530010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって、あの野ざらしの死体には<line-break>とっても強い願いが残ってたんだ。<line-break>放っておけないよ。<end>"
EN: "But such a powerful longing remained in that corpse lying out in the open. I couldn't just leave it.<end>"
KO: "그 들판에 버려진 시체에는 아주 강한 소원이 남아 있었거든. 그냥 둘 수가 없어.<end>"

## 1530011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…黙っていれば、あの女は<line-break>恋人を待つ自分に陶酔しながら、<line-break>ここで飽きるまでお祈りができたのだ。<line-break>幸福なままにな。<line-break>死者のために生きている人間の<line-break>幸福を壊すとは愚かなことだ。<end>"
EN: "Had you kept silent, that woman could have stayed here praying until she tired of it, intoxicated by the thought of herself waiting for her lover. She could have remained happy. It is foolish to destroy the happiness of the living who devote themselves to the dead.<end>"
KO: "…가만히 있었다면 그 여자는 연인을 기다리는 자신에게 취해 여기서 질릴 때까지 기도할 수 있었어. 행복한 채로. 죽은 자를 위해 사는 인간의 행복을 깨다니 어리석은 짓이야.<end>"

## 1530012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生きている人間の傷は時間がいやす。<line-break>けど、時間がすり抜ける死者の心の傷は<line-break>何もいやすことがないんだよ。<line-break>君もアズラゴーザの件で忙しいんだろ。<line-break>こんな所で油売ってないで、行きなよ。<line-break>…おっと、勇者さまがおいでだよ。<end>"
EN: "Time heals the wounds of the living. But the dead lie beyond time's reach, so nothing can heal their wounded hearts. You're busy with that Azuragoza business too, aren't you? Stop dawdling here and get going. Oh, look. The Hero has arrived.<end>"
KO: "살아 있는 인간의 상처는 시간이 치유하지. 하지만 시간이 스쳐 지나가는 죽은 자의 마음의 상처는 아무것도 낫게 하지 못해. 너도 아즈라고자 일로 바쁘잖아. 이런 데서 빈둥대지 말고 가. …어이쿠, 용자님이 오셨군.<end>"

## 1530013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いくら、君でも<line-break>神の前で事をかまえるほど<line-break>神を信じていないわけではないだろう？<end>"
EN: "Even you aren't so lacking in faith that you'd start a fight before a god, are you?<end>"
KO: "아무리 너라도 신 앞에서 일을 벌일 만큼 신을 믿지 않는 건 아니겠지?<end>"

## 1530014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あせらなくても、十分強くなったら、<line-break>相手してあげるよ。<line-break>じゃあね。<end>"
EN: "No need to rush. Once you're strong enough, I'll take you on. See you.<end>"
KO: "서두르지 않아도 충분히 강해지면 상대해 줄게. 그럼 안녕.<end>"

## 1530015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございます、エルファス様。<end>"
EN: "Thank you, Lord Elfas.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다, 엘파스 님.<end>"

## 1530016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、救世主様！<line-break>あれほどの食料をいただけるなんて！<end>"
EN: "Oh, Savior! To think you would give us so much food!<end>"
KO: "아아, 구세주님! 그렇게 많은 식량을 주시다니!<end>"

## 1530017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "信じ祈る者を、神は見捨てはしない。<line-break>裁きを受けたこの街で<line-break>なおも生き抜く者に許されること。<line-break>それは、ただ祈ることだ。<line-break>神を信じ、すがることだ。<end>"
EN: "God will not forsake those who believe and pray. For those who still endure in this city under judgment, only one thing is permitted: prayer. Believe in God and turn to God for salvation.<end>"
KO: "믿고 기도하는 자를 신은 버리지 않는다. 심판받은 이 거리에서 아직 살아가는 자에게 허락된 것은 오직 기도뿐. 신을 믿고 의지하는 것이다.<end>"

## 1530018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、真の裁きが降ろうとしている。<line-break>世界が滅びる日が迫っている。<line-break>　<line-break>祈るのだ。すべてを投げ捨て<line-break>神にすがるのだ。<end>"
EN: "True judgment is about to descend. The day the world ends draws near. Pray. Cast everything aside and seek refuge in God.<end>"
KO: "지금 진정한 심판이 내려지려 한다. 세계가 멸망할 날이 다가오고 있다. 기도하라. 모든 것을 버리고 신에게 매달려라.<end>"

## 1530019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神は、その祈りを<line-break>聞き届けてくださるだろう。<end>"
EN: "God will surely hear your prayers.<end>"
KO: "신께서 그 기도를 들어 주실 것이다.<end>"

## 1530020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くだない説教はやめないか！<line-break>さあ、みなも耳を貸すんじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Enough of this foolish sermon! Everyone, don't listen to him!<end>"
KO: "시시한 설교는 그만두지! 자, 모두 저 말에 귀 기울이지 마라.<end>"

## 1530021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょっと、あんた！<line-break>救世主様に何するんだい！<line-break>　<line-break>このお方はね、あたしたちに<line-break>食べ物を恵んでくださったんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, you! What do you think you're doing to the Savior? He gave us food!<end>"
KO: "이봐, 당신! 구세주님께 무슨 짓이야! 이분은 우리에게 먹을 것을 베풀어 주신 분이라고.<end>"

## 1530022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうさね。<line-break>このお方に指一本でも触れたら<line-break>ただじゃおかんわい。<end>"
EN: "That's right. Lay so much as a finger on him and you won't get away with it!<end>"
KO: "그렇지. 이분께 손가락 하나라도 댔다간 그냥 두지 않겠다.<end>"

## 1530023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙らないか！<line-break>破滅を説くような男の話を<line-break>信じてはダメだ！<end>"
EN: "Silence! Don't believe a man who preaches doom!<end>"
KO: "조용히 하지 못하겠나! 파멸을 떠드는 남자의 말을 믿어서는 안 된다!<end>"

## 1530024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この男は連行する！<end>"
EN: "We're taking this man into custody!<end>"
KO: "이 남자는 연행한다!<end>"

## 1530025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、来るんだ！<end>"
EN: "Come along!<end>"
KO: "자, 따라와!<end>"

## 1530026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだお前は？<line-break>邪魔をするな！<end>"
EN: "Who are you? Stay out of this!<end>"
KO: "넌 뭐냐? 방해하지 마!<end>"

## 1530027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…相変わらず血の気が多いな。<line-break>君は、騒ぎを見ると<line-break>放っておけない性分のようだ。<end>"
EN: "Still as hotheaded as ever, I see. Whenever there's trouble, you just can't stay out of it.<end>"
KO: "…여전히 혈기가 넘치는군. 너는 소란을 보면 그냥 지나치지 못하는 성격인가 봐.<end>"

## 1530028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の腕は買うけれど、<line-break>その性格はいただけない。<line-break>　<line-break>…操りにくいからね。<line-break>そういう人形は。<end>"
EN: "I value your skill, but not that temperament of yours. It makes a puppet like you difficult to control.<end>"
KO: "네 실력은 인정하지만 그 성격은 마음에 들지 않아. …다루기 힘들거든, 그런 인형은.<end>"

## 1530029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…相変わらず血の気が多いな。<line-break>騒ぎを見ると放っておけない<line-break>性分のようだ、君は。<line-break>僕が言ったことを<line-break>覚えているだろう？<end>"
EN: "Still as hotheaded as ever, I see. Whenever there's trouble, you just can't stay out of it. You remember what I told you, don't you?<end>"
KO: "…여전히 피가 뜨겁군. 소동을 보면 그냥 지나치지 못하는 성격인가 봐. 내가 한 말을 기억하고 있겠지?<end>"

## 1530030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみ…。<line-break>…君のために言ってるんだ。<end>"
EN: "It is foolish to rely on power. Power is only ever crushed by a greater power. I'm saying this for your own good.<end>"
KO: "힘에 매달리는 건 어리석은 짓이야. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐…. …너를 위해 하는 말이야.<end>"

## 1530031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余計なことをしてくれた。<line-break>放っておいてくれれば、<line-break>よかったのに…。<end>"
EN: "You interfered where you weren't wanted. You should have left me alone.<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 짓을 했군. 내버려 뒀으면 좋았을 텐데….<end>"

## 1530032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕が礼を言うとでも思ったのか？<end>"
EN: "Did you expect me to thank you?<end>"
KO: "내가 감사라도 할 줄 알았나?<end>"

## 1530033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか…？<line-break>それなら、礼を言う代わりに<line-break>ひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"
EN: "You don't know who I am? Then instead of thanking you, let me offer you a warning. It is foolish to rely on power. Power is only ever crushed by a greater power.<end>"
KO: "너, 나를 모르는 건가…? 그렇다면 감사 대신 충고 하나 하지. 힘에 매달리는 건 어리석은 일이다. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 짓눌릴 뿐이야.<end>"

## 1530034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は神が遣わしたる救世主<line-break>エルファス。<line-break>僕の言葉は、神の言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "I am Elfas, the Savior sent by God. My words are the words of God.<end>"
KO: "나는 신이 보낸 구세주 엘파스. 나의 말은 신의 말이다.<end>"

## 1530035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の名…、聞いておきたい。<end>"
EN: "I would like to know your name.<end>"
KO: "네 이름… 들어두고 싶어.<end>"

## 1530036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>君とは、また会いそうな気がするよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I have a feeling we'll meet again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 너와는 또 만날 것 같은 기분이 들어.<end>"

## 1530037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしらが生きていられるのは、<line-break>エルファス様のおかげじゃ。<line-break>ありがたや、ありがたや。<end>"
EN: "It's thanks to Lord Elfas that we're still alive. Bless him. Bless him.<end>"
KO: "우리가 살아 있는 건 엘파스 님 덕분이야. 감사하구나, 감사해.<end>"

## 1530038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのお方に手を上げるなんて。<line-break>まったく、ディンガルのやつらと来たら。<end>"
EN: "How dare they raise a hand against him. Those wretched Dyneskal...<end>"
KO: "그분에게 손을 올리다니. 정말 딩갈 녀석들이란.<end>"

## 1530039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神よ、感謝します。<end>"
EN: "God, I give thanks.<end>"
KO: "신이시여, 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1530040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと気が付いたか。<end>"
EN: "Finally awake, are you?<end>"
KO: "드디어 정신이 들었나.<end>"

## 1530041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら、僕が追い払った。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>やってくれるね、君も。<line-break>でも、どうせ邪魔をするなら<line-break>もっと他に方法があるだろう？<end>"
EN: "I drove them off. You really are something, you know. But if you were going to interfere, couldn't you have found a better way?<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들은 내가 쫓아냈다. 제법 방해하는군. 하지만 어차피 훼방 놓을 거면 다른 방법도 있었을 텐데?<end>"

## 1530042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと気が付いたか。<end>"
EN: "Finally awake, are you?<end>"
KO: "드디어 정신이 들었나.<end>"

## 1530043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら、僕が追い払った。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>少しは自分の力をわきまえてくれ。<line-break>次はもう、君を助けたりしないよ。<end>"
EN: "I drove them off. Try to understand the limits of your strength. I won't save you again.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들이라면 내가 쫓아냈어. 조금은 자기 힘을 알아 둬. 다음엔 더 이상 널 도와주지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 1530044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと気が付いたか。<end>"
EN: "Finally awake, are you?<end>"
KO: "드디어 정신이 들었나.<end>"

## 1530045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あいつらなら僕が片付けた。<line-break>君が気を失っている間にね。<line-break>　<line-break>君の無責任なお節介の礼に<line-break>ひとつ忠告しよう。<line-break>…自分の力を過信しないことだ。<end>"
EN: "I dealt with them while you were unconscious. In return for your reckless meddling, let me offer you a warning: do not place too much faith in your own strength.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석들이라면 내가 처리했어. 네가 정신을 잃은 동안에. 네 무책임한 참견에 대한 답례로 충고 하나 하지. 자신의 힘을 과신하지 마.<end>"

## 1530046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "力にすがるのは愚かなことだ。<line-break>力はただ、より強い力によって<line-break>叩き伏せられるのみだ。<end>"
EN: "It is foolish to rely on power. Power is only ever crushed by a greater power.<end>"
KO: "힘에 매달리는 건 어리석다. 힘은 그저 더 강한 힘에 의해 짓눌릴 뿐이다.<end>"

## 1530047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕を知らないのか？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>僕は神が遣わしたる<line-break>救世主エルファス。<line-break>僕の言葉は、神の言葉だ。<end>"
EN: "You don't know who I am? I am Elfas, the Savior sent by God. My words are the words of God.<end>"
KO: "너, 나를 모르는 거야? 나는 신께서 보내신 구세주 엘파스. 내 말은 곧 신의 말씀이다.<end>"

## 1530048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君の名…、聞いておきたい。<end>"
EN: "I would like to know your name.<end>"
KO: "네 이름… 들어두고 싶어.<end>"

## 1530049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>君とは、また会いそうな気がするよ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I have a feeling we'll meet again.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 너와는 또 만날 것 같은 기분이 들어.<end>"

## 1530050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "き、消えた！？<end>"
EN: "G-gone?!<end>"
KO: "사, 사라졌어!?<end>"

## 1530051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こっちだ。<end>"
EN: "Over here.<end>"
KO: "이쪽이야.<end>"

## 1530052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "く、くそぉ…。<end>"
EN: "D-damn it...<end>"
KO: "제, 제길….<end>"

## 1530053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわっ。<line-break>ま、待て…！<end>"
EN: "Agh! W-wait!<end>"
KO: "우왓. 자, 잠깐…!<end>"

## 1530054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こ、ここはいったん退こう。<end>"
EN: "L-let's retreat for now.<end>"
KO: "여, 여기서는 일단 물러나자.<end>"

## 1530055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス様！<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "Lord Elfas! Thank you!<end>"
KO: "엘파스 님! 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1530056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "救世主様！<end>"
EN: "Savior!<end>"
KO: "구세주님!<end>"

## 1530057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、我を信じよ。<line-break>滅びのは近い。<line-break>その日に我を信じる者のみが<line-break>救われるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Simply believe in me. Destruction draws near. On that day, only those who believe in me shall be saved.<end>"
KO: "그저 나를 믿어라. 멸망이 가까웠다. 그날 나를 믿는 자만이 구원받을 것이다.<end>"

## 1530058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わしらは生きていられるのは<line-break>エルファス様のおかげじゃ<line-break>ありがたや、ありがたや。<end>"
EN: "It's thanks to Lord Elfas that we're still alive. Bless him. Bless him.<end>"
KO: "우리가 살아 있는 건 엘파스 님 덕분이야. 감사하네, 감사해.<end>"

## 1530059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのお方に手を上げるなんて。<line-break>まったく、ディンガルのやつらと来たら。<end>"
EN: "How dare they raise a hand against him. Those wretched Dyneskal...<end>"
KO: "그분에게 손을 올리다니. 정말 딩갈 녀석들이란.<end>"

## 1530060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec153-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神よ、感謝します。<end>"
EN: "God, I give thanks.<end>"
KO: "신이시여, 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1540001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>美しいな…、<line-break>世界は…。<end>"
EN: "...The world is beautiful.<end>"
KO: "………………. 아름답구나…, 세계는….<end>"

## 1540002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "美しい？<line-break>美しいはずさ。<line-break>この世界は輝きに満ちている…。　　<line-break>特にその世界っていうものに、<line-break>好きな人が存在するとき、<line-break>人は世界を美しいと感じるようだね。<end>"
EN: "Beautiful? Of course it is. This world is filled with light... It seems people find the world especially beautiful when someone they love is in it.<end>"
KO: "아름답다고? 아름다울 수밖에. 이 세계는 빛으로 가득하니까…. 특히 그 세계에 좋아하는 사람이 존재할 때, 사람은 세계를 아름답다고 느끼는 모양이야.<end>"

## 1540003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉さん以外に好きな人間ができたの？<line-break>エルファス…？<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Is there someone you love besides your sister, Elfas?<end>"
KO: "누나 말고 좋아하는 사람이 생긴 거야? 엘파스…?<end>"

## 1540004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、でもね、それは裏切りだよ？<line-break>君の姉さんに対する…。<end>"
EN: "Heh. But you know, that's a betrayal. A betrayal of your sister...<end>"
KO: "후후, 하지만 그건 배신이야? 네 누나에 대한….<end>"

## 1540005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…先に裏切ったのは姉さんの方だ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>姉さんはネメアに心を奪われ、<line-break>僕が姉さんを救い出そうとしても<line-break>エンシャントを離れようとしなかった。<end>"
EN: "...She betrayed me first. Nemea stole her heart, and even when I tried to save her, she refused to leave Ancient.<end>"
KO: "…먼저 배신한 건 누나 쪽이야. 누나는 네메아에게 마음을 빼앗겨 내가 구하려 해도 엔샨트를 떠나려 하지 않았어.<end>"

## 1540006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、君はその報復に<line-break>姉さんを見殺しにした。<line-break>　<line-break>皇帝エリュマルクの嫉妬から<line-break>彼女を救わずに、放っておいた。<line-break>君の力があれば救えたのに…。<end>"
EN: "So you took your revenge by leaving your sister to die. You abandoned her to Emperor Elmark's jealousy instead of saving her, even though you had the power to do it...<end>"
KO: "그래서 넌 그 보복으로 누나를 죽게 내버려 뒀어. 황제 에류마르크의 질투에서 그녀를 구하지 않고 방치했지. 네 힘이면 구할 수 있었는데…<end>"

## 1540007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…姉さんは死を望んでいた。<end>"
EN: "...My sister wanted to die.<end>"
KO: "…누나는 죽음을 바라고 있었어.<end>"

## 1540008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死んだ人はかわいそうだね。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>死んだ人はいつまでもそのときの<line-break>気持ちを、心に持ち続けるのに…<line-break>生きてる人は変わってゆく。<end>"
EN: "The dead are so pitiful. They carry the feelings they had at the moment of death forever... while the living go on changing.<end>"
KO: "죽은 사람은 불쌍하지. 죽은 사람은 언제까지나 그때의 마음을 품고 있는데… 살아 있는 사람은 변해 가니까.<end>"

## 1540009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "死者の気持ちを平然と踏みにじる。<line-break>死者はどうやって<line-break>気持ちを訴えればいいんだろう？<end>"
EN: "They trample the feelings of the dead without a second thought. How are the dead supposed to make their feelings known?<end>"
KO: "죽은 자의 마음을 태연히 짓밟는군. 죽은 사람은 대체 어떻게 자기 마음을 호소해야 하지?<end>"

## 1540010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わからないよ、…シャリ。<line-break>…どうすれば、いいんだ？<end>"
EN: "...I don't know, Shalli. What am I supposed to do?<end>"
KO: "…모르겠어, 샤리…. 어떻게 해야 하지?<end>"

## 1540011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "公平にするのさ。<line-break>それで、すべてが終わるよ。<end>"
EN: "Make it fair. Then it will all be over.<end>"
KO: "공평하게 만드는 거야. 그러면 모든 게 끝나.<end>"

## 1540012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…シャリ、僕は…<line-break>僕は…、どうすれば…。<end>"
EN: "Shalli, I... What am I supposed to do...?<end>"
KO: "…샤리, 나는… 나는… 어떻게 해야….<end>"

## 1540013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "簡単さ。後悔してくれ。<line-break>後ろめたく思ってくれ。<line-break>　<line-break>人間は賢いからね。後悔さえあれば、<line-break>自分を守るために、憎悪や軽蔑が<line-break>正当な理由とともに浮かんでくるだろ？<end>"
EN: "It's simple. Regret what you did. Feel guilty about it. Humans are clever, you know. Once regret sets in, hatred and contempt will rise up to protect you, complete with perfectly good reasons. Won't they?<end>"
KO: "간단해. 후회해 줘. 죄책감을 가져 줘. 인간은 영리하니까. 후회만 있으면 자신을 지키기 위해 증오나 경멸이 정당한 이유와 함께 떠오르겠지?<end>"

## 1540014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁ、憎悪を通して世界を見て。<line-break>まだ、世界は美しいかい？<line-break><value:$28>のことを好きなの？<end>"
EN: "Now look at the world through your hatred. Is it still beautiful? Do you love <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "자, 증오를 통해 세상을 봐. 그래도 세상은 아름다워? <value:$28>을 좋아해?<end>"

## 1540015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "嫌いだろ？<end>"
EN: "You hate that person, don't you?<end>"
KO: "싫어하잖아?<end>"

## 1540016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いい子だ。<line-break>さて、実践の方に行こうか？<end>"
EN: "Good boy. Shall we put that into practice now?<end>"
KO: "착하네. 자, 이제 실전으로 가 볼까?<end>"

## 1540017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>を殺すんだ。<line-break>君の姉さんへの真心を示すために。<end>"
EN: "Kill <value:$28>. Show how true your devotion to your sister is.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>을 죽여. 네 누나를 향한 진심을 증명하기 위해서.<end>"

## 1540018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "意気地なしだなあ。<line-break>だから坊ちゃん育ちはダメなのさ。<end>"
EN: "What a coward. This is why sheltered little rich boys are no good.<end>"
KO: "겁쟁이네. 그래서 도련님처럼 자란 애들은 안 된다니까.<end>"

## 1540019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁい、お元気？　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>せっかく会えたんだけど、<line-break>僕は用があるから、<line-break>失礼するよ。<end>"
EN: "Hi there, <value:$28>. It was nice running into you, but I have something to do, so if you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "안녕, 잘 지내? <value:$28>. 모처럼 만났지만 난 볼일이 있어서 이만 갈게.<end>"

## 1540020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でもね、この子がね<line-break>君のこと食べちゃいたいくらい　　<line-break>好きだなんだってさ！<end>"
EN: "But you see, this one says he likes you so much he could just eat you up!<end>"
KO: "그런데 말이야, 이 아이가 널 먹어 버리고 싶을 만큼 좋아한대!<end>"

## 1540021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml

## 1540024

## 1540032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1540034

## 1540041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "後悔してる、エルファス？<line-break>それじゃ帰って反省しようか。<line-break>ゆっくり心の傷を開いてあげるよ。<end>"
EN: "Do you regret it, Elfas? Then let's go home so you can think about what you've done. I'll slowly open the wound in your heart.<end>"
KO: "후회하고 있어, 엘파스? 그럼 돌아가서 반성할까. 천천히 마음의 상처를 열어 줄게.<end>"

## 1540042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、エルファスは連れていくね。<line-break>彼にはまだやってもらいたいことが、<line-break>残っているんだ。アハハ！<end>"
EN: "Well then, I'll be taking Elfas with me. There are still things I need him to do. Ahaha!<end>"
KO: "그럼 엘파스는 데려갈게. 아직 그에게 해 달라고 할 일이 남아 있거든. 아하하!<end>"

## 1540043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁい、元気そうだね、<value:$28>。　<line-break>でも、ここまでくるとさすがに<line-break>目ざわりなんだよね。<line-break>　<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主は、<line-break>もうひとりいるから、<line-break>君にはそろそろ退場してもらおうかな。<end>"
EN: "Hi there, <value:$28>. You look well. But by now, you're really getting in my way. There's already another bearer of the Infinite Soul, so maybe it's time you left the stage.<end>"
KO: "하아이, 잘 지내는 것 같네, <value:$28>. 하지만 여기까지 오니 역시 눈에 거슬리네. 무한의 소울을 가진 자는 한 명 더 있으니까, 너는 슬슬 퇴장해 줘야겠어.<end>"

## 1540044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめてくれ、シャリ！<end>"
EN: "Stop, Shalli!<end>"
KO: "그만둬, 샤리!<end>"

## 1540045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アハハ。<line-break>聞こえないよ。エルファス。<line-break>君のそんな虚ろな願いなんて。<end>"
EN: "Ahaha. I can't hear you, Elfas. Not those hollow wishes of yours.<end>"
KO: "아하하. 안 들려, 엘파스. 그런 공허한 소원 따위.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん、理解に苦しむなぁ。<end>"

## 1540051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "어라어라? 혹시 나를 적으로 돌리겠다는 뜻인가?<end>"

## 1540052

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1540054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>が…。<line-break>僕にとって大事な存在だからだ。<end>"
EN: "Because <value:$28>... is important to me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>이… 내게 소중한 존재이기 때문이야.<end>"

## 1540055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいのかな？<line-break><value:$28>をかばうのって<line-break>裏切りじゃない？<line-break>君はこれまで多くの人の命を<line-break>奪ってきたんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>その中には誰かにとって、<line-break>大事な存在だった人もいたはずさ。<end>"
EN: "Is that really all right? Wouldn't protecting <value:$28> be a betrayal? You've taken many lives so far. Some of them must have been important to someone.<end>"
KO: "괜찮은 걸까? <value:$28>를 감싸는 건 배신 아니야? 넌 지금까지 많은 사람의 목숨을 빼앗아 왔어. 그중에는 누군가에게 소중한 사람이었던 자도 있었겠지.<end>"

## 1540056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何人もその手にかけたあげく、<line-break>自分の姉さんまで見殺しにした君が、<line-break>今さら大事な人を守りたいだなんて…。<line-break>虫のよすぎる話だとは思わないかい？<end>"
EN: "After putting so many people to death with your own hands, and even abandoning your own sister to die, you say you want to protect someone important now... Don't you think that's incredibly self-serving?<end>"
KO: "여럿을 제 손으로 죽이고 자기 누나까지 죽게 내버려 둔 네가 이제 와 소중한 사람을 지키고 싶다니…. 너무 제멋대로라고 생각하지 않아?<end>"

## 1540057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかってはいる。だけど…。<line-break>もう耐えきれないんだ。<end>"
EN: "I know. But... I can't endure it any longer.<end>"
KO: "알고 있어. 하지만…. 이제 더는 견딜 수 없어.<end>"

## 1540058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "理解してくれとは言わぬ。<line-break>だが<value:$28>を決して<line-break>死なせはしない。<end>"
EN: "I do not ask you to understand. But I will never let <value:$28> die.<end>"
KO: "이해해 달라고는 하지 않겠다. 하지만 <value:$28>를 절대 죽게 두지 않겠다.<end>"

## 1540059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん。まあいいさ。<line-break>壊れたオモチャに興味はないよ。<line-break>さようなら、エルファス。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. Fine. I have no interest in broken toys. Farewell, Elfas.<end>"
KO: "흐음. 뭐, 됐어. 망가진 장난감에는 관심 없어. 잘 있어, 엘파스.<end>"

## 1540060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう君には、僕がかなえてあげるだけの<line-break>願いはないみたいだからね。<end>"
EN: "It seems you no longer have any wish for me to grant.<end>"
KO: "이제 너에겐 내가 이루어줄 만한 소원이 없는 것 같으니까.<end>"

## 1540061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もはや僕にはどこに行くアテもない。<line-break>すまないが、しばらく一緒に<line-break>いさせてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I have nowhere left to go. Forgive me, but let me stay with you a while.<end>"
KO: "이제 내겐 갈 곳도 없어. 미안하지만 당분간 같이 있게 해 줘.<end>"

## 1540068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1540069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉さんは助けなかったのに<line-break><value:$28>は助けるの？<line-break>あれれ？　どうしてだろ？<end>"
EN: "You wouldn't save your sister, but you'll save <value:$28>? Well, well. I wonder why?<end>"
KO: "누나는 안 구해 줬으면서 <value:$28>은 구하는 거야? 어라? 왜 그럴까?<end>"

## 1540070

## 1540072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ。ずるいね、エルファス。<line-break>君は姉さんの代わりを…安らぎを<line-break><value:$28>に求めてるんだ。<end>"

KO: "그래. 치사하네, 엘파스. 넌 누나의 대신을… 안식을 <value:$28>에게서 찾고 있는 거야.<end>"

## 1540073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1540074

## 1540075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "絶望ってね、<line-break>ホントは<line-break>深い、深ーい願いなんだよ。<line-break><value:$28>ってさ、<line-break>可能性とか希望そのものだし、<line-break>彼が強く惹かれるのも当然かもね。<end>"
EN: "You know, despair is really a deep, deep wish. And <value:$28> is possibility and hope itself. Perhaps it's only natural that he should be drawn so strongly.<end>"
KO: "절망이라는 건 사실 아주 깊고 깊은 소망이야. <value:$28>는 가능성이나 희망 그 자체니까, 그가 강하게 끌리는 것도 당연할지 모르지.<end>"

## 1540076

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I know. But... I can't endure it any longer.<end>"
KO: "알고 있어. 하지만…. 이제 더는 견딜 수 없어.<end>"

## 1540077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "理解してくれとは言わぬ。<line-break>だが<value:$28>を決して<line-break>死なせはしない。<end>"
EN: "I do not ask you to understand. But I will never let <value:$28> die.<end>"
KO: "이해해 달라고는 하지 않겠다. 하지만 <value:$28>를 절대 죽게 두지 않겠다.<end>"

## 1540078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふーん。まあいいさ。<line-break>僕は願いをかなえるけど<line-break>強制はしない。<line-break>君の代わりを探すことにするよ。<line-break>さようなら、エルファス。<end>"

KO: "흐음. 뭐, 좋아. 나는 소원은 이루어 주지만 강요하진 않아. 네 대신을 찾지. 잘 가, 엘파스.<end>"

## 1540079

JP: "もう君には、僕がかなえてあげるだけの<line-break>願いはないみたいだからね。<end>"
EN: "It seems you no longer have any wish for me to grant.<end>"
KO: "이제 너에겐 내가 이루어줄 만한 소원이 없는 것 같으니까.<end>"

## 1540080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もはや僕にはどこに行くアテもない。<line-break>すまないが、しばらく一緒に<line-break>いさせてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I have nowhere left to go. Forgive me, but let me stay with you a while.<end>"
KO: "이제 내겐 갈 곳도 없어. 미안하지만 당분간 같이 있게 해 줘.<end>"

## 1540081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やーちゃった、やっちゃった！<line-break>うわー、人間ってひどいよね、<line-break>もの言わぬ者たちに対しては。<line-break>何も言えない者たちを<line-break>平気で踏みにじっちゃうよね、<line-break>姉さんを見捨てるエルファスみたいに。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you did it! You really did it! Humans are so cruel to those without a voice, aren't they? They trample all over those who can't speak for themselves without a second thought. Just like Elfas abandoning his sister.<end>"
KO: "해 버렸다, 해 버렸어! 우와~ 인간은 말 못 하는 존재들한테 정말 심하네. 아무 말도 못 하는 자들을 아무렇지도 않게 짓밟잖아. 누나를 버리는 엘파스처럼.<end>"

## 1540082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、エルファスは連れていくね。<line-break>彼にはまだやってもらいたいことが、<line-break>残っているんだ。アハハ！<end>"
EN: "Well then, I'll be taking Elfas with me. There are still things I need him to do. Ahaha!<end>"
KO: "그럼 엘파스는 데려갈게. 아직 그에게 해 달라고 할 일이 남아 있거든. 아하하!<end>"

## 1540083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やーちゃった、やっちゃった！<line-break>うわー、人間ってひどいよね、<line-break>もの言わぬ者たちに対しては。<line-break>何も言えない者たちを<line-break>平気で踏みにじっちゃうよね、<line-break>姉さんを見捨てるエルファスみたいに。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you did it! You really did it! Humans are so cruel to those without a voice, aren't they? They trample all over those who can't speak for themselves without a second thought. Just like Elfas abandoning his sister.<end>"
KO: "해 버렸다, 해 버렸어! 우와~ 인간은 말 못 하는 존재들한테 정말 심하네. 아무 말도 못 하는 자들을 아무렇지도 않게 짓밟잖아. 누나를 버리는 엘파스처럼.<end>"

## 1540084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "後悔してる、エルファス？<line-break>それじゃ帰って反省しようか。<line-break>ゆっくり心の傷を開いてあげるよ。<end>"
EN: "Do you regret it, Elfas? Then let's go home so you can think about what you've done. I'll slowly open the wound in your heart.<end>"
KO: "후회하고 있어, 엘파스? 그럼 돌아가서 반성할까. 천천히 마음의 상처를 열어 줄게.<end>"

## 1540094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめるんだ、シャリ。<line-break>できれば、お前を討ちたくはない…。<end>"
EN: "Stop, Shalli. I don't want to have to strike you down if I can help it...<end>"
KO: "그만둬, 샤리. 가능하면 너를 쓰러뜨리고 싶지는 않아….<end>"

## 1540095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフアハハ…！<line-break>可愛いこと言うね、エルファス。<line-break>そういう甘ちゃんなところは大好きさ。<line-break>でもね、君はもう負け犬なんだ。<line-break>神になる勇気をなくした君には、<line-break>もう石ころほどの価値もないんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh... Ahahaha! What a sweet thing to say, Elfas. I just love that softhearted side of you. But you're a loser now. You've lost your courage to become a god, so you're not even worth a pebble anymore.<end>"
KO: "후후후 하하하…! 귀여운 소리 하네, 엘파스. 그런 물러터진 점은 정말 좋아해. 하지만 넌 이제 패배자야. 신이 될 용기를 잃은 너에겐 돌멩이만큼의 가치도 없어.<end>"

## 1540096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっくっくっ。<value:$28>よ。<line-break>わしらがなにゆえに闇の巨人を呼び出したか、わかるか？<line-break>すべてはバロル様復活のためよ。<line-break>闇の巨人はバロル様が降臨するための<line-break>依り代に必要なのでな。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. Tell me, <value:$28>: do you know why we summoned the Dark Giant? It was all to bring Lord Balor back. The Dark Giant is needed as the vessel through which Lord Balor will descend.<end>"
KO: "크크크. <value:$28>여. 우리가 왜 어둠의 거인을 불러냈는지 아느냐? 모든 것은 발로르 님의 부활을 위해서다. 어둠의 거인은 발로르 님께서 강림하실 그릇에 필요하거든.<end>"

## 1540097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の巨人をこの世界に<line-break>呼び寄せることができたのも、<line-break>強大な闇の力の持ち主である<line-break>闇の王女がいればこそさ。<line-break>いくら僕やゾフォル爺さんでも<line-break>闇の巨人は生み出せないからね。<end>"
EN: "We were only able to summon the Dark Giant into this world because we had the Princess of Darkness, with all her immense dark power. Even old Zofor and I couldn't create the Dark Giant ourselves.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 거인을 이 세계로 불러올 수 있었던 것도 강대한 어둠의 힘을 지닌 어둠의 공주가 있었기 때문이야. 나나 조포르 영감이라 해도 어둠의 거인을 만들어 낼 수는 없거든.<end>"

## 1540098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして、彼女を闇に突き落とした<line-break>最大の功労者は君だよ。<line-break><value:$28>。アハハハ。<end>"
EN: "And no one did more to plunge her into darkness than you, <value:$28>. Ahahaha!<end>"
KO: "그리고 그녀를 어둠으로 밀어 넣은 가장 큰 공로자는 너야, <value:$28>. 아하하하.<end>"

## 1540099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁて、邪眼帝と呼ばれたバロルの<line-break>お手並み拝見といこうかな。<end>"
EN: "Now then, let's see what Balor, the Evil-Eyed Emperor, can do.<end>"
KO: "자, 사안제라 불리던 발로르의 솜씨를 구경해 볼까.<end>"

## 1540100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ…アハハ！　すごい！<line-break>予想以上だね、バロルの力は！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... Ahaha! Amazing! Balor's power exceeds even my expectations!<end>"
KO: "우후후… 아하하! 대단해! 예상 이상이야, 발로르의 힘은!<end>"

## 1540101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネ、ネメア…。<line-break>生きていたの…。<line-break> <line-break>ふふふ…でも、同じ事。<line-break>魔王バロルよ、<line-break>闇の者、ネメアを消し去りたまえ！<end>"
EN: "N-Nemea... You were alive... Hee hee... But it makes no difference. Balor, Demon King, erase Nemea, creature of darkness, from existence!<end>"
KO: "네, 네메아…. 살아 있었어…. 후후후… 하지만 마찬가지야. 마왕 발로르여, 어둠의 자 네메아를 없애 주소서!<end>"

## 1540102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！<line-break>破滅の槍の力を解放する気？<end>"
EN: "Damn! Are you going to unleash the power of the Spear of Ruin?<end>"
KO: "큭! 파멸의 창의 힘을 해방할 셈이야?<end>"

## 1540103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は残念だよ。ネメア。<line-break>次元の狭間に消えたと聞いていたのに、<line-break>また地上に舞い戻ってくるなんて。<end>"
EN: "I'm disappointed, Nemea. I heard you vanished into the Interdimensional Rift, yet here you are, returned to the world above.<end>"
KO: "난 유감이야, 네메아. 차원의 틈새로 사라졌다고 들었는데 또 지상으로 돌아오다니.<end>"

## 1540104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス。イズのことは…。<end>"
EN: "Elfas. About Iz...<end>"
KO: "엘파스. 이즈에 대해서는….<end>"

## 1540105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙っててくれないか、ネメア。お前が姉さんにした仕打ちを<line-break>僕は忘れたわけじゃない。<line-break>ただ、今は死者にではなく…、<line-break>生きている人への思いを<line-break>大切にしたいんだ。<end>"
EN: "Will you be quiet, Nemea? I haven't forgotten what you did to my sister. But now, rather than dwell on the dead... I want to cherish my feelings for the living.<end>"
KO: "조용히 해 줄래, 네메아. 네가 누나에게 한 짓을 내가 잊은 건 아니야. 다만 지금은 죽은 사람보다… 살아 있는 사람을 향한 마음을 소중히 하고 싶어.<end>"

## 1540106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ。いずれすべてが終われば、<line-break>語り合う機会もあるだろう。<line-break>　<line-break>今はただ鬼神となって、奴らの野望を<line-break>粉砕せねばならん。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. Once all this is over, perhaps we will have a chance to talk. For now, I must become a war god and shatter their ambitions.<end>"
KO: "훗. 언젠가 모든 게 끝나면 서로 이야기할 기회도 있겠지. 지금은 그저 귀신이 되어 놈들의 야망을 분쇄해야 한다.<end>"

## 1540108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間は、よきにつけ悪しきにつけ、<line-break>変わってゆくものです。<line-break>　<line-break>人の思いは、やがて世界を<line-break>新たな未来に導いていくでしょう。<line-break>これからは人間の時代が来るんですね。<end>"
EN: "For better or worse, humans change. In time, people's thoughts and feelings will lead the world toward a new future. The age of humanity is coming.<end>"
KO: "인간은 좋든 나쁘든 변해 가는 존재입니다. 사람들의 마음은 언젠가 세계를 새로운 미래로 이끌겠지요. 이제부터는 인간의 시대가 오는 거군요.<end>"

## 1540110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そんなこと知るもんか。<end>"
EN: "How should I know?<end>"
KO: "그런 걸 내가 알 게 뭐야.<end>"

## 1540111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "答えはね、ネメアは気づいているからさ。<line-break>あのとき、世界を救ったのが<line-break>自分たちだけじゃなかったって。<line-break>そしてあのときの悲劇のこともね。<line-break>それで彼は英雄視されるのを拒むのさ。<line-break>僕に言わせれば変なこだわりだけどね。<line-break>魔王バロルとネメアが戦ってるとき<line-break>実は、別の場所でも<line-break>世界を救うための戦いがあったんだ。<line-break>不死である魔王バロルを倒すために<line-break>イズの封印を破るって戦いがね。<line-break>　<line-break>ゼネテス、君、そして<line-break>忘れられた大いなるソウルの持ち主は、<line-break>イズが施した封印に挑んだ…。<end>"
EN: "The answer is that Nemea knows. He knows his group wasn't the only one to save the world that day, and he knows about the tragedy that occurred. That's why he refuses to be hailed as a hero. A strange thing to be so particular about, if you ask me. While Nemea fought Balor, Demon King, another battle to save the world was actually being fought elsewhere―a battle to break Iz's seal so that the immortal Balor could be defeated. Xenetes, you, and the forgotten bearer of the great Soul challenged the seal Iz had placed...<end>"
KO: "답은 말이지, 네메아가 알고 있기 때문이야. 그때 세계를 구한 게 자기들뿐만이 아니었다는 걸. 그리고 그때의 비극도 말이지. 그래서 그는 영웅으로 떠받들리는 걸 거부하는 거야. 내가 보기엔 이상한 고집이지만. 마왕 발로르와 네메아가 싸울 때 사실 다른 곳에서도 세계를 구하기 위한 싸움이 있었어. 불사인 마왕 발로르를 쓰러뜨리기 위해 이즈의 봉인을 깨는 싸움이었지. 제네테스, 너, 그리고 잊힌 위대한 소울의 소유자는 이즈가 걸어 둔 봉인에 도전했어….<end>"

## 1540112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルは苦しんでいたんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あらがえない運命と<line-break>見たくもない予知に<line-break>苛まれながら…。<line-break>そしていつの日か、<line-break>その苦しみから解放されることを<line-break>願っていたんだ…。<line-break>…僕は<value:$28>に救われた。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>もし、<value:$28>と<line-break>出会っていなければ…<line-break>　<line-break>今ここで倒されていたのは<line-break>僕だったのかもしれない…。<end>"
EN: "Zofor was suffering, tormented by a fate he could not resist and visions he never wished to see. All the while, he hoped that someday he would be freed from that suffering... <value:$28> saved me. If I had never met <value:$28>... perhaps I would have been the one lying defeated here.<end>"
KO: "조포르는 괴로워하고 있었어. 거스를 수 없는 운명과 보고 싶지 않은 예지에 시달리면서…. 그리고 언젠가 그 고통에서 해방되길 바랐지…. 나는 <value:$28>에게 구원받았어. 만약 <value:$28>를 만나지 못했다면… 지금 여기서 쓰러져 있던 건 나였을지도 몰라….<end>"

## 1540113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォルは苦しんでいたんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あらがえない運命と<line-break>見たくもない予知に<line-break>苛まれながら…。<line-break>そしていつの日か、<line-break>その苦しみから解放されることを<line-break>願っていたんだ…。<line-break>…僕は<value:$28>に救われた。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>もし、<value:$28>と<line-break>出会っていなければ…<line-break>　<line-break>今ここで倒されていたのは<line-break>僕だったのかもしれない…。<end>"
EN: "Zofor was suffering, tormented by a fate he could not resist and visions he never wished to see. All the while, he hoped that someday he would be freed from that suffering... <value:$28> saved me. If I had never met <value:$28>... perhaps I would have been the one lying defeated here.<end>"
KO: "조포르는 괴로워하고 있었어. 거스를 수 없는 운명과 보고 싶지 않은 예지에 시달리면서…. 그리고 언젠가 그 고통에서 해방되길 바랐지…. 나는 <value:$28>에게 구원받았어. 만약 <value:$28>를 만나지 못했다면… 지금 여기서 쓰러져 있던 건 나였을지도 몰라….<end>"

## 1540114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめるんだ、シャリ。<line-break>できれば、お前を討ちたくはない…。<end>"
EN: "Stop this, Shalli. I would rather not strike you down...<end>"
KO: "그만둬, 샤리. 가능하면 너를 쓰러뜨리고 싶지는 않아….<end>"

## 1540115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ…。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee...<end>"
KO: "우후후….<end>"

## 1540116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフアハハ…！<line-break>かわいいこと言うね、エルファス。<line-break>そういう甘ちゃんなところは大好きさ。<line-break>でも、君はもう舞台を降りちゃったし、<line-break>ちょっと惜しいけど、<line-break>代役を立てさせてもらうよ。<line-break>破壊の化身、魔王バロルをね。<end>"
EN: "Hee hee, ahaha...! How sweet of you, Elfas. I love that softhearted side of you. But you've already left the stage. It's a bit of a shame, but I'll have to cast a replacement: the Embodiment of Destruction, Balor, Demon King.<end>"
KO: "우후후 아하하…! 귀여운 말을 하는구나, 엘파스. 그런 순진한 점은 정말 좋아해. 하지만 넌 이미 무대에서 내려갔으니 조금 아쉽지만 대역을 세워야겠어. 파괴의 화신, 마왕 발로르를 말이야.<end>"

## 1540117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう誰にも止められないよ。<line-break>強大な闇の巨人が世界中を襲ってるのさ。<line-break>でも、これはほんの序の口<line-break>ここからが本番だ。<end>"
EN: "No one can stop it now. Mighty Dark Giants are attacking all over the world. But that's only the beginning. Now the real show starts.<end>"
KO: "이제 아무도 못 막아. 강대한 어둠의 거인이 세계 곳곳을 습격하고 있어. 하지만 이건 시작에 불과해. 이제부터가 본론이야.<end>"

## 1540118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゾフォル爺さん、頼んだよ。<end>"
EN: "Old Zofor, I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "조포르 영감, 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1540119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっくっくっ。<value:$28>よ。<line-break>わしらがなにゆえに闇の巨人を呼び出したか、わかるか？<line-break>すべてはバロル様復活のためよ。<line-break>闇の巨人はバロル様が降臨するための<line-break>依り代に必要なのでな。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. Tell me, <value:$28>: do you know why we summoned the Dark Giant? It was all to bring Lord Balor back. The Dark Giant is needed as the vessel through which Lord Balor will descend.<end>"
KO: "크크크. <value:$28>여. 우리가 왜 어둠의 거인을 불러냈는지 아느냐? 모든 것은 발로르 님의 부활을 위해서다. 어둠의 거인은 발로르 님께서 강림하실 그릇에 필요하거든.<end>"

## 1540120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の巨人をこれだけ<line-break>呼び寄せることができたから<line-break>バロルも復活できた。<line-break>それもすべて、<line-break>強大な闇の力の持ち主である<line-break>闇の王女がいればこそだけどね。<end>"
EN: "Summoning this many Giants of Darkness made it possible to revive Balor as well. Of course, none of it would have been possible without the Princess of Darkness and her tremendous dark power.<end>"
KO: "어둠의 거인을 이만큼 불러모을 수 있었기에 발로르도 부활할 수 있었어. 그것도 모두 강대한 어둠의 힘을 지닌 어둠의 왕녀가 있었기 때문이지만.<end>"

## 1540121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、<value:$28>。<line-break>彼女を闇に突き落とした<line-break>最大の功労者は君だよ。アハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Thank you, <value:$28>. No one did more than you to cast her into darkness. Ahahaha!<end>"
KO: "고마워, <value:$28>. 그녀를 어둠에 빠뜨린 최대 공로자는 너야. 아하하하!<end>"

## 1540122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さぁて、邪眼帝と呼ばれたバロルの<line-break>お手並み拝見といこうかな。<end>"
EN: "Now then, let's see what Balor, the Evil-Eyed Emperor, can do.<end>"
KO: "자, 사안제라 불리던 발로르의 솜씨를 구경해 볼까.<end>"

## 1540123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ…アハハ！　すごい！<line-break>予想以上だね、バロルの力は！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee... Ahaha! Amazing! Balor's power is greater than I expected!<end>"
KO: "우후후… 아하하! 대단해! 예상 이상이야, 발로르의 힘은!<end>"

## 1540124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、ネメア…。<line-break>破壊神の門たる<line-break>偽りの勇者様。<line-break>あなたに彼らと一緒にいる<line-break>資格はないわ。<line-break>神よ、汚らわしきネメアを消せ！<end>"
EN: "Why, Nemea... False Hero, gateway of the God of Destruction. You have no right to stand with them. God, erase that filthy Nemea!<end>"
KO: "어머, 네메아…. 파괴신의 문인 거짓 용사님. 당신에겐 그들과 함께 있을 자격이 없어. 신이여, 더러운 네메아를 없애 주소서!<end>"

## 1540125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ！<line-break>破滅の槍の力を解放する気なの？<end>"
EN: "Damn! Are you going to unleash the Spear of Ruin's power?<end>"
KO: "큭! 파멸의 창의 힘을 해방하려는 거야?<end>"

## 1540126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス…。<end>"
EN: "Elfas...<end>"
KO: "엘파스….<end>"

## 1540127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は残念だよ。ネメア。<line-break>次元の狭間に消えたと聞いていたのに、<line-break>また地上に舞い戻ってくるなんて。<end>"
EN: "I'm disappointed, Nemea. I heard you vanished into the Interdimensional Rift, yet here you are, returned to the world above.<end>"
KO: "난 유감이야, 네메아. 차원의 틈새로 사라졌다고 들었는데 또 지상으로 돌아오다니.<end>"

## 1540128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス。イズのことは…。<end>"
EN: "Elfas. About Iz...<end>"
KO: "엘파스. 이즈에 대해서는….<end>"

## 1540129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ネメア…。<line-break>姉さんのこと、<line-break>水に流したわけじゃない。<line-break>ただ、今は死者にではなく…、<line-break>生きている人への思いを<line-break>大切にしたいんだ。<end>"
EN: "Nemea... I haven't put what happened to my older sister behind me. But right now, the feelings I want to cherish aren't for the dead... They're for the living.<end>"
KO: "네메아…. 누나 일을 잊은 건 아니야. 다만 지금은 죽은 사람이 아니라… 살아 있는 사람을 향한 마음을 소중히 하고 싶어.<end>"

## 1540130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふっ…そうか。<end>"
EN: "Hmph... I see.<end>"
KO: "훗… 그런가.<end>"

## 1540132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間は、善きにつけ悪しきにつけ、<line-break>変わってゆくものです。<line-break>　<line-break>人の思いは、やがて世界を<line-break>新たな未来に導いていくでしょう。<line-break>これからは人間の時代が来るんですね。<end>"
EN: "For better or worse, humans change. In time, people's thoughts and feelings will lead the world toward a new future. The age of humanity is coming.<end>"
KO: "인간은 좋든 나쁘든 변해 가는 존재입니다. 사람의 마음은 언젠가 세계를 새로운 미래로 이끌겠지요. 이제부터는 인간의 시대가 오는 거군요.<end>"

## 1540133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は<value:$28>と進む道を選んだ。<line-break>もう、過去からも運命からも<line-break>逃げるつもりはない。<end>"
EN: "I chose to walk the same path as <value:$28>. I won't run from the past or from fate anymore.<end>"
KO: "나는 <value:$28>과 함께 가는 길을 선택했어. 이제 과거에서도 운명에서도 도망치지 않을 거야.<end>"

## 1540134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec154-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エルファス　ボリューム５　終端<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "엘파스 볼륨5 끝<end>"

## 1550026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec155-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはセヅネの根というものです。<line-break>これを持つと、ＶＩＴが下がるので<line-break>注意してください。<line-break><line-break>また、これはかなり危険なものなので、<line-break>捨てるなら元のところへ返してください。<line-break>それが冒険者の心得というものです。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　冒険者ギルド<end>"
EN: "This is Sezune Root. Be careful: carrying it lowers ＶＩＴ. It is also quite dangerous, so if you discard it, return it to where you found it. That is the adventurer's code. ―Adventurers' Guild<end>"
KO: "이것은 세즈네의 뿌리라는 물건입니다. 가지고 있으면 VIT가 내려가니 주의하십시오. 또한 상당히 위험한 물건이므로 버리려면 원래 있던 곳에 돌려놓으십시오. 그것이 모험가의 마음가짐입니다. 모험가 길드<end>"

## 1550027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec155-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはトヅカネの枝というものです。<line-break>これを持つと、ＡＧＩが下がるので<line-break>注意してください。<line-break><line-break>また、これはかなり危険なものなので、<line-break>捨てるなら元のところへ返してください。<line-break>それが冒険者の心得というものです。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　冒険者ギルド<end>"
EN: "This is a Tozukane Branch. Be careful: carrying it lowers ＡＧＩ. It is also quite dangerous, so if you discard it, return it to where you found it. That is the adventurer's code. ―Adventurers' Guild<end>"
KO: "이것은 토즈카네의 가지라는 물건입니다. 가지고 있으면 AGI가 내려가니 주의하십시오. 또한 상당히 위험한 물건이므로 버리려면 원래 있던 곳에 돌려놓으십시오. 그것이 모험가의 마음가짐입니다. 모험가 길드<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec155-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはキッカネの香草というものです。<line-break>これを持つと、ＩＮＴが下がるので<line-break>注意してください。<line-break><line-break>また、これはかなり危険なものなので、<line-break>捨てるなら元のところへ返してください。<line-break>それが冒険者の心得というものです。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　冒険者ギルド<end>"
EN: "This is Kikkane Herb. Be careful: carrying it lowers ＩＮＴ. It is also quite dangerous, so if you discard it, return it to where you found it. That is the adventurer's code. ―Adventurers' Guild<end>"
KO: "이것은 킷카네의 향초라는 물건입니다. 가지고 있으면 INT가 내려가니 주의하십시오. 또한 상당히 위험한 물건이므로 버리려면 원래 있던 곳에 돌려놓으십시오. 그것이 모험가의 마음가짐입니다. 모험가 길드<end>"

## 1550032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec155-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは千年石というものです。<line-break>これを持つと、ＭＩＮが下がるので<line-break>注意してください。<line-break><line-break>また、これはかなり危険なものなので、<line-break>捨てるなら元のところへ返してください。<line-break>それが冒険者の心得というものです。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　冒険者ギルド<end>"
EN: "This is a Millennium Stone. Be careful: carrying it lowers ＭＩＮ. It is also quite dangerous, so if you discard it, return it to where you found it. That is the adventurer's code. ―Adventurers' Guild<end>"
KO: "이것은 천년석이라는 물건입니다. 이걸 가지고 있으면 MIN이 내려가니 주의하십시오. 또 상당히 위험한 물건이므로 버릴 거라면 원래 있던 곳에 돌려놓으십시오. 그것이 모험가의 마음가짐입니다. 모험가 길드<end>"

## 1550033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec155-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これはヤンバイ草というものです。<line-break>これを持つと、幻覚状態になるので<line-break>注意してください。<line-break><line-break>また、これはかなり危険なものなので、<line-break>捨てるなら元のところへ返してください。<line-break>それが冒険者の心得というものです。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　　冒険者ギルド<end>"
EN: "This is Yanbai Herb. Be careful: carrying it inflicts Hallucination. It is also quite dangerous, so if you discard it, return it to where you found it. That is the adventurer's code. ―Adventurers' Guild<end>"
KO: "이것은 얀바이초라는 물건입니다. 가지고 있으면 환각 상태가 되니 주의하십시오. 또한 상당히 위험한 물건이므로 버리려면 원래 있던 곳에 돌려놓으십시오. 그것이 모험가의 마음가짐입니다. 모험가 길드<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやー、あんたに頼んで正解だったぜ。　　<line-break>まったく戦闘しないで済んだからな。<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬より多いが、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>完璧に護衛をこなしてくれたことのお礼だ。<end>"
EN: "Well, hiring you was the right choice. There wasn't a single fight. Here's a little more than we agreed on. Take it―it's my thanks for a flawless escort job.<end>"
KO: "이야, 너한테 부탁하길 잘했어. 전혀 싸우지 않고 끝났으니까. 약속한 보수보다 많지만 받아 줘. 완벽하게 호위해 준 답례야.<end>"

## 1560001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとかたどり着くことができたか。<line-break>あんたも冒険者としてはまだまだだな。<line-break>約束の報酬だ、ご苦労さん。<end>"
EN: "Well, we made it somehow. You've still got a long way to go as an adventurer. Here's the agreed reward. Thanks for your work.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착했군. 너도 모험가로서는 아직 멀었어. 약속한 보수다. 수고했다.<end>"

## 1560002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたなんかに頼むんじゃなかった。<line-break>護衛の意味がないじゃないか。<line-break>　<line-break>まっとうな仕事をしてないんだ、<line-break>報酬は払わないからな。<end>"
EN: "I never should've hired you. Some escort you were. You didn't do your job, so I'm not paying you.<end>"
KO: "당신한테 부탁하는 게 아니었어. 호위의 의미가 없잖아. 제대로 일하지 않았으니 보수는 안 줄 거야.<end>"

## 1560003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "到着期限が過ぎてしまったな。<line-break>悪いが、報酬は半額だ。<end>"
EN: "We've missed the arrival deadline. Sorry, but you only get half the reward.<end>"
KO: "도착 기한이 지나 버렸군. 미안하지만 보수는 반액이다.<end>"

## 1560004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お役目ご苦労さん。<line-break>あんたなら、いい冒険者になれそうだな。　<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬より多いが、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>完璧に護衛をこなしてくれたことのお礼だ。<end>"
EN: "Good work. You could make a fine adventurer. This is more than we agreed on, but take it. It's a little extra for doing a perfect job.<end>"
KO: "수고했어. 당신이라면 좋은 모험자가 되겠군. 약속한 보수보다 많지만 받아. 완벽하게 호위해 준 답례야.<end>"

## 1560005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お役目ご苦労さん。<line-break>あんたなら、いい傭兵になれそうだな。<line-break>約束の報酬だ、ご苦労さん。<end>"
EN: "Good work. You could make a fine mercenary. Here's the agreed reward. Thanks for your work.<end>"
KO: "수고했다. 너라면 좋은 용병이 될 것 같군. 약속한 보수다. 수고했어.<end>"

## 1560006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、俺よりも弱いくらいなんじゃないか？<line-break>そんなんで護衛なんかやるんじゃねえよ。<line-break>　<line-break>まっとうな仕事をしてないんだ、<line-break>報酬は払わないぞ。<end>"
EN: "Are you actually weaker than I am? If that's the best you can do, don't take escort work. You didn't do your job, so I'm not paying you.<end>"
KO: "너, 나보다도 약한 거 아니야? 그런 실력으로 호위 일을 하지 마. 제대로 일도 못 했으니 보수는 안 줄 거야.<end>"

## 1560007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとかたどり着いたが、約束の期限には<line-break>間に合わなかったな。<line-break>悪いが報酬は半額だ。<end>"
EN: "We made it somehow, but not by the promised deadline. Sorry, but you only get half the reward.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착은 했지만 약속한 기한에는 못 맞췄군. 미안하지만 보수는 절반이다.<end>"

## 1560008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>おかげさまで、無事に着くことができました。<line-break>　<line-break>お約束の報酬です。少し多めですが、<line-break>お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. Thanks to you, I arrived safe and sound. Here is the promised reward. It's a little extra, but please accept it.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 덕분에 무사히 도착했습니다. 약속드린 보수입니다. 조금 많지만 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1560009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>なんとか、たどり着くことができました。<line-break>お約束の報酬です。お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I managed to make it here somehow. Here is the promised reward. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "고맙습니다. 어떻게든 도착할 수 있었습니다. 약속드린 보수입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1560010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう…。<line-break>ああ…やっと、たどり着けたのですね。　<line-break>　<line-break>あ…あれ？<line-break>皮袋を落としてしまったようです。<line-break>残念ながら、報酬はお支払いできません。<end>"
EN: "Whew... Ah, I've finally made it. Wait... Where is it? It seems I've dropped my leather pouch. Unfortunately, I can't pay you.<end>"
KO: "후우…. 아아… 겨우 도착했군요. 아… 어라? 가죽 주머니를 떨어뜨린 모양입니다. 유감이지만 보수는 드릴 수 없겠네요.<end>"

## 1560011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、たどり着くことができましたが、<line-break>期限を過ぎてしまいました。<line-break>申しわけありませんが、報酬は半額です。<end>"
EN: "I managed to make it here, but we've missed the deadline. I'm sorry, but you will receive only half the reward.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착하긴 했지만 기한을 넘기고 말았습니다. 죄송하지만 보수는 절반입니다.<end>"

## 1560012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やった、もう着いたんだね、どうもありがとう。<line-break>おかげで楽しい旅ができたよ。<line-break>　<line-break>はい、約束のお金。<line-break>ちょっと多めだけど、僕の気持ちだから<line-break>とっといてね。<end>"
EN: "We made it already! Thanks a lot. You made this such a fun journey. Here, the money I promised. It's a little extra, but that's because I want you to have it.<end>"
KO: "됐다, 벌써 도착했네. 정말 고마워. 덕분에 즐거운 여행이었어. 자, 약속한 돈. 조금 더 얹었지만 내 마음이니까 받아 둬.<end>"

## 1560013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと、着いたね。どうもありがとう。　　　　<line-break>はい、約束のお金。<line-break>　<line-break>…前に護衛してくれた人よりもヘタだったな…。<line-break>い、いや…なんでもないよ。<end>"
EN: "We finally made it. Thanks a lot. Here, the money I promised. ...You weren't as good as the last escort... N-no, it's nothing.<end>"
KO: "드디어 도착했네. 고마워. 자, 약속한 돈이야. …전에 호위해 준 사람보다 서툴렀네… 아, 아니… 아무것도 아니야.<end>"

## 1560014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イタタ…。あっちこっち傷だらけだよ。　　<line-break>もう、君に護衛は頼まないからね。<line-break>　<line-break>こんなんじゃ、お金なんて払わないからね。<line-break>こっちがお金をもらいたいくらいだよ。<end>"
EN: "Ow... I'm covered in wounds. I'm never hiring you as an escort again. I'm not paying for this. If anything, you ought to be paying me.<end>"
KO: "아야야…. 여기저기 상처투성이잖아. 이제 너한테 호위는 안 맡길 거야. 이래서는 돈도 못 줘. 오히려 내가 돈을 받고 싶을 정도야.<end>"

## 1560015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと、着いたね。でも期限切れだね。<line-break>この場合、約束のお金は半額でいいんだよね。<end>"
EN: "We finally made it, but the deadline's passed. That means I only owe you half the money, right?<end>"
KO: "드디어 도착했네. 하지만 기한을 넘겼어. 이 경우 약속한 돈은 절반이면 되는 거지?<end>"

## 1560016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>これで無事に巡礼を続けられます。　<line-break>　<line-break>約束のお礼です。すこし多めですが、<line-break>私の気持ちです、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. Now I can continue my pilgrimage safely. Here is the promised payment. It's a little extra, as a token of my gratitude. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 이제 무사히 순례를 계속할 수 있겠습니다. 약속한 답례입니다. 조금 많지만 제 마음이니 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1560017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>これでなんとか巡礼を続けられます。<line-break>約束のお礼です、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. Now I can at least continue my pilgrimage. Here is the promised payment. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "정말 고맙습니다. 덕분에 순례를 계속할 수 있겠어요. 약속한 사례입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1560018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神さまはさまざまな試練を私に課すのですね。<line-break>…あなたに支払うお金は、風の彼方におられる<line-break>我らが神に献上させていただきます。<line-break>ついでにあなたの今後の運命も<line-break>お祈りして差し上げましょう。<end>"
EN: "God does see fit to visit many trials upon me. The money intended for you shall instead be offered to our God, who dwells beyond the wind. While I am at it, I shall pray for the fate that awaits you as well.<end>"
KO: "신께서는 제게 여러 시련을 내리시는군요…. 당신에게 드릴 돈은 바람 저편에 계신 우리 신께 바치겠습니다. 덤으로 당신의 앞날도 기도해 드리지요.<end>"

## 1560019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとかたどり着きましたが、約束の日には<line-break>間に合いませんでした。<line-break>お礼は半額だけお支払いします。<end>"
EN: "I managed to arrive, but not by the promised day. I will pay only half the agreed sum.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착했지만 약속한 날에는 늦었습니다. 사례는 절반만 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり護衛を頼んでよかったです。<line-break>約束の報酬金と心ばかりのお礼です。<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "I'm certainly glad I hired an escort. Here is the promised reward, along with a small token of my gratitude. Thank you very much.<end>"
KO: "역시 호위를 부탁하길 잘했어요. 약속한 보수와 작은 사례입니다. 정말 고맙습니다.<end>"

## 1560021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "護衛を頼まなければ、大変なめにあっていたかも<line-break>しれません。約束の報酬金です。<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "I might have been in terrible trouble without an escort. Here is the promised reward. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "호위를 부탁하지 않았다면 큰일을 겪었을지도 모르겠어요. 약속한 보수입니다. 고맙습니다.<end>"

## 1560022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これじゃ護衛の意味がないじゃないですか。　<line-break>もう、二度と護衛なんか頼みません。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたへの報酬金は、私のケガの治療代として<line-break>使わせていただきますので、あしからず。<end>"
EN: "What was the point of hiring an escort if this is the result? I will never hire one again. I shall use your reward to pay for the treatment of my injuries. I trust you understand.<end>"
KO: "이래서는 호위의 의미가 없잖아요. 이제 두 번 다시 호위를 부탁하지 않겠습니다. 당신에게 줄 보수는 제 부상 치료비로 쓰겠습니다. 양해하세요.<end>"

## 1560023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか到着しましたが、約束の期限には<line-break>間に合いませんでしたね。<line-break>約束の報酬金は半額しか払いません。<end>"
EN: "I managed to arrive, but not by the promised deadline. I will pay only half the agreed reward.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착했지만 약속한 기한에는 늦었네요. 약속한 보수는 절반만 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "……何事もなく無事に旅を終えられましたな。<line-break>神のご加護に感謝しなさい。<line-break>　<line-break>これは神があなたに与えられる報酬です。<line-break>少し多めなのは神のご慈悲です。<line-break>神に失礼なきよう、有意義に使われよ。<end>"
EN: "...The journey has ended safely and without incident. Give thanks for God's protection. This is the reward God bestows upon you. That it is a little more than promised is an act of divine mercy. Use it wisely, lest you give offense to God.<end>"
KO: "……아무 일 없이 무사히 여행을 마쳤군요. 신의 가호에 감사하십시오. 이것은 신께서 당신에게 내리시는 보상입니다. 조금 많은 것은 신의 자비입니다. 신께 실례가 되지 않도록 뜻있게 쓰십시오.<end>"

## 1560025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、旅を終えられましたな。多少の戦いは<line-break>神の試練です。神に感謝しなさい。<line-break>　<line-break>これは神があなたに与えられる報酬です。<line-break>神に失礼なきよう、有意義に使われよ。<end>"
EN: "The journey has ended somehow. Those few battles were trials sent by God. Give thanks. This is the reward God bestows upon you. Use it wisely, lest you give offense to God.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 여행을 마쳤군. 약간의 싸움은 신의 시련이다. 신께 감사하라. 이것은 신이 네게 주시는 보상이다. 신께 실례가 되지 않도록 뜻있게 쓰도록.<end>"

## 1560026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我を危地に落とそうとする汝は、闇の手の者か？<line-break>神のいかづちが落ちる前に改心するがよい。<line-break>　<line-break>報酬金だと！なんたる不届きものか！<line-break>神よ！この邪なるものに天罰を落とし給え！<end>"
EN: "Are you, who would cast me into peril, an agent of the dark hand? Repent before God's thunderbolt strikes you down. A reward, you say? What brazen impudence! O God, visit divine punishment upon this wicked creature!<end>"
KO: "나를 위험에 빠뜨리려 한 그대는 어둠의 수하인가? 신의 벼락이 내리기 전에 회개하라. 보수금이라고! 이 얼마나 불경한 자인가! 신이여! 이 사악한 자에게 천벌을 내리소서!<end>"

## 1560027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、旅を終えられましたな。<line-break>しかし、約束の期日に遅れるとは…。　<line-break>汝への報酬は半額だ。<line-break>人は、神のような永遠の命を持たぬ。<line-break>時間を大切に生きてゆかねばならぬぞ。<line-break>よいな、冒険者よ。<end>"
EN: "The journey has ended somehow. Yet to arrive after the appointed day... Your reward shall be halved. Mortals do not possess eternal life as God does. You must cherish the time allotted to you. Heed me, adventurer.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 여행을 끝냈군. 하지만 약속한 날에 늦다니…. 그대의 보수는 절반이다. 인간은 신처럼 영원한 생명을 가진 것이 아니니 시간을 소중히 여기며 살아야 한다. 알겠나, 모험가여.<end>"

## 1560028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげで、無事にたどり着けた。　　　　<line-break>礼を言うぞ。冒険者よ。<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬と、これは私からの心付けだ。<line-break>遠慮なく受け取り給え。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I arrived safely. You have my gratitude, adventurer. Here is the promised reward, along with a gratuity from me. Accept it without reservation.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 무사히 도착했다. 고맙다, 모험자여. 약속한 보수와 이건 내 성의다. 사양 말고 받아라.<end>"

## 1560029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、たどり着けたな。　<line-break>役目ご苦労だった。<line-break>約束の報酬だ。受け取り給え。<end>"
EN: "We made it somehow. You have discharged your duty well. Here is the promised reward. Accept it.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착했군. 임무 수고했다. 약속한 보수다. 받아라.<end>"

## 1560030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様、本官を陥れようとしたな。<line-break>今度出会ったら、その首ないものと思え。<line-break>　<line-break>誰が報酬など払うか！<line-break>三流冒険者め。<end>"
EN: "You dared plot against an officer! Cross my path again and consider your head forfeit. Pay you a reward? Not a chance, you third-rate adventurer!<end>"
KO: "네놈, 이 몸을 함정에 빠뜨리려 했겠다. 다음에 만나면 목이 붙어 있을 줄 알지 마라. 누가 보수 따위를 주나! 삼류 모험자 녀석.<end>"

## 1560031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "契約の期日から何日遅れたと思ってるんだ！？<line-break>無事にたどりつけても、遅れては意味がないぞ。<line-break>当然、報酬は半額にさせてもらうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Do you have any idea how many days late we are under the contract?! Arriving safely means nothing if we're late. Naturally, I will pay only half the reward.<end>"
KO: "계약 기한보다 며칠이나 늦은 줄 알아!? 무사히 도착해도 늦으면 의미가 없어. 당연히 보수는 반액만 지급하겠다.<end>"

## 1560032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげで、無事にたどり着けた。　　　　<line-break>礼を言うぞ。冒険者よ。<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬と、これは私からの心付けだ。<line-break>遠慮なく受け取り給え。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I arrived safely. You have my gratitude, adventurer. Here is the promised reward, along with a gratuity from me. Accept it without reservation.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 무사히 도착했다. 고맙다, 모험자여. 약속한 보수와 이건 내 성의다. 사양 말고 받아라.<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "어떻게든 도착했군. 임무 수고했다. 약속한 보수다. 받아라.<end>"

## 1560038

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

JP: "契約の期日から何日遅れたと思ってるんだ！？<line-break>無事にたどりつけても、遅れては意味がないぞ。<line-break>当然、報酬は半額にさせてもらうぞ。<end>"
EN: "Do you have any idea how many days late we are under the contract?! Arriving safely means nothing if we're late. Naturally, I will pay only half the reward.<end>"
KO: "계약 기한보다 며칠이나 늦은 줄 알아!? 무사히 도착해도 늦으면 의미가 없어. 당연히 보수는 반액만 지급하겠다.<end>"

## 1560040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、人間の護衛は久しぶりじゃが、<line-break>なかなかやるもんじゃのお。いや、ご苦労さん。<line-break>　<line-break>ほい、報酬金とこれはチップじゃ。<line-break>またそのうち、お前さんに頼みたいのお。<end>"
EN: "Well now, it's been a long time since I had a human escort, but you did a fine job. Good work. Here, your reward and a tip. I'd like to hire you again sometime.<end>"
KO: "호오, 인간 호위는 오랜만인데 제법 하는구먼. 수고했어. 자, 보수금이랑 이건 팁이야. 다음에도 자네한테 부탁하고 싶구먼.<end>"

## 1560041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、やっとついたかの。<line-break>ドワーフの護衛なら、もちっとましじゃがのお。<line-break>　<line-break>ほい、報酬金じゃ。<line-break>…今度はドワーフの護衛に頼もうかのお。<end>"
EN: "Good grief, we've finally arrived. A Dwarf escort would've done a better job. Here, your reward. ...Perhaps I'll hire a Dwarf next time.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런, 겨우 도착했군. 드워프 호위라면 좀 더 나았을 텐데. 자, 보수다. …다음엔 드워프에게 호위를 부탁할까.<end>"

## 1560042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、ひどいめにあわされてしまったの。<line-break>報酬金なんかやらんぞい。<line-break>人間に護衛なんぞ頼むもんじゃないわい。<end>"
EN: "Good grief, what an ordeal you put me through. You're not getting any reward. I should've known better than to hire a human escort.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런, 호되게 당하고 말았구먼. 보상금 따윈 못 주겠네. 인간에게 호위 같은 걸 부탁하는 게 아니었어.<end>"

## 1560043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、やっと到着か。<line-break>しかし、約束の期限には間に合わなんだの。<line-break>悪いが、報酬は半額じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Good grief, we've finally arrived. But we've missed the promised deadline. Sorry, but you only get half the reward.<end>"
KO: "이런, 이제야 도착했나. 하지만 약속 기한에는 맞추지 못했군. 미안하지만 보수는 절반이다.<end>"

## 1560044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげさまで、無事に着くことができました。<line-break>約束の報酬金と心ばかりのお礼です。<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I arrived safe and sound. Here is the promised reward, along with a small token of my gratitude. Thank you very much.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 무사히 도착할 수 있었습니다. 약속한 보수와 작은 감사의 뜻입니다. 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1560045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、たどり着くことができました。<line-break>約束の報酬金です。<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "I managed to make it here somehow. Here is the promised reward. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착할 수 있었습니다. 약속한 보수입니다. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1560046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう…。<line-break>ああ…やっと、たどり着けたのですね。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>こんな目に会わせて、さらにお金を巻き上げよう<line-break>なんて思っていませんよね。<line-break>報酬金はケガの治療代にさせていただきます。<end>"
EN: "Whew... Ah, I've finally made it. You don't expect to put me through all that and then wring money out of me too, do you? I'll be using your reward to pay for the treatment of my injuries.<end>"
KO: "후우…. 아아… 드디어 도착했군요. 이런 꼴을 당하게 해 놓고 돈까지 뜯어 갈 생각은 아니겠죠. 보수금은 제 치료비로 쓰겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう…。<line-break>ああ…やっと、たどり着けたのですね。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>約束の期日には間に合いませんでした。<line-break>残念ながら、報酬は半額にさせていただきます。<end>"
EN: "Whew... Ah, I've finally made it. We missed the promised deadline. Unfortunately, I will pay only half your reward.<end>"
KO: "후우… 아아… 겨우 도착했군요. 약속한 기한에는 못 맞췄습니다. 유감이지만 보수는 절반만 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無事到着ですな。大変お世話になりました。<line-break>お礼金とおチップはこちらでございます。<line-break>では、失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "We have arrived safely. I am greatly obliged to you. Here are your payment and tip. Now, if you will excuse me.<end>"
KO: "무사히 도착했군요. 정말 신세 많이 졌습니다. 사례금과 팁은 여기 있습니다. 그럼 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ようやく到着ですな。お世話になりました。<line-break>お礼はこちらでございます。<line-break>では、失礼いたします。<end>"
EN: "We have arrived at last. I am obliged to you. Here is your payment. Now, if you will excuse me.<end>"
KO: "드디어 도착했군요. 신세 많이 졌습니다. 사례는 여기 있습니다. 그럼 이만 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私をこんなめにあわせて……。<line-break>ご主人さまのお耳に入れますぞ。<line-break>せいぜい覚悟してらっしゃい！<end>"
EN: "After putting me through such an ordeal... My master shall hear of this. You had better prepare yourself!<end>"
KO: "나를 이런 꼴로 만들다니……. 주인님 귀에 들어가게 할 테다. 각오 단단히 해 둬!<end>"

## 1560051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>おかげで無事にたどりつけました。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お礼金とおチップはこちらでございます。<line-break>では、失礼いたします。<end>"

## 1560053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>おかげでなんとかたどりつけました。<line-break>　<line-break>お礼はこちらでございます。<line-break>では、失礼いたします。<end>"
