KO: "정말 감사합니다. 덕분에 어떻게든 도착했습니다. 답례는 여기 있습니다. 그럼 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶんひどいめにあわされました。<line-break>あなたには、もう二度と護衛を頼みません。　<line-break>　<line-break>あなたへの報酬金は、私のケガの治療代として<line-break>使わせていただきますので、あしからず。<end>"
EN: "You put me through a terrible ordeal. I will never hire you as an escort again. I shall use your reward to pay for the treatment of my injuries. I trust you understand.<end>"
KO: "정말 심한 꼴을 당했습니다. 당신에게는 이제 두 번 다시 호위를 부탁하지 않겠습니다. 당신에게 줄 보수는 제 부상 치료비로 쓰겠습니다. 양해하세요.<end>"

## 1560055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとかたどり着けました。<line-break>約束の期日には、間に合いませんでしたが…。<line-break>残念ながら、報酬は半額にさせていただきます。<end>"
EN: "I managed to make it here, though not by the promised date. Regrettably, I will pay only half your reward.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착했습니다. 약속한 날짜에는 맞추지 못했지만…. 유감이지만 보수는 반액으로 하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやっはっは、もう到着かの、ご苦労さん。<line-break>お礼はこれでよろしいか？<line-break>　<line-break>それと、ボーナスじゃな、ほれ。<line-break>いずれまた、頼むからの。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha! Here already, are we? Good work. Will this do for your payment? And here's a bonus. I'll be hiring you again someday.<end>"
KO: "이야하하, 벌써 도착했나. 수고했네. 답례는 이걸로 괜찮겠나? 그리고 보너스도 있지, 자. 또 부탁할 일이 있을 게야.<end>"

## 1560057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう、やっと着いたか。もうちっとしっかりと<line-break>働いてくれたら、お礼もはずんだのにのお。<end>"
EN: "Whew, we finally made it. If you'd worked a little harder, I'd have paid you more.<end>"
KO: "후우, 겨우 도착했구먼. 조금만 더 제대로 일했으면 사례도 더 후하게 했을 텐데.<end>"

## 1560058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はぁ、はぁ、なんでわしがこんなめに会わないと<line-break>いけないんじゃ？　お前はクビじゃ、クビ。<line-break>　<line-break>報酬なんぞやらん。ギルドの親父め…、<line-break>まったく、ひどい護衛をつけおってからに…。<end>"
EN: "Pant... pant... Why did I have to suffer through all that? You're fired, you hear me? Fired! You're not getting a reward. That old fool at the Guild... Saddling me with such a dreadful escort...<end>"
KO: "헉, 헉, 왜 내가 이런 꼴을 당해야 하는 게야? 넌 해고다, 해고! 보수 따윈 없다. 길드 영감탱이… 정말 형편없는 호위를 붙여 놨구먼….<end>"

## 1560059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったく、なんて護衛じゃ、期日に遅れるとは！<line-break>おかげで、市場に間に合わなくなるとはのぉ。<line-break>　<line-break>本来、報酬なんて払えたもんじゃないが、<line-break>ほれ、約束の半分の金額じゃ、受け取れ。<end>"
EN: "What a worthless escort, making us late! Now I'll miss the market because of you. I shouldn't pay you anything at all, but here―take half the sum we agreed on.<end>"
KO: "정말 무슨 호위가 이래, 기한에 늦다니! 덕분에 시장 시간도 놓치게 생겼잖아. 원래 보수 따윈 줄 수 없지만, 자, 약속한 절반이다. 받아.<end>"

## 1560060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうよ。<line-break>おかげさんで、無事にたどりつけたぜ。　　<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬とボーナスだ、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>また、そのうち頼むぜ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks. You got me here safe and sound. Take the promised reward and a bonus. I'll hire you again sometime.<end>"
KO: "고맙다. 덕분에 무사히 도착했어. 약속한 보수와 보너스다. 받아 둬. 또 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1560061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっとついたかい。<line-break>約束の報酬だ、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>ま、暇があったら酒場でも覗いていってくれや。<end>"
EN: "Finally here, eh? Take the reward I promised you. And if you've got time, stop by the tavern.<end>"
KO: "드디어 도착했군. 약속한 보수다, 받아. 뭐, 시간 나면 술집에도 한번 들러줘.<end>"

## 1560062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "てめえ、こんなていたらくで金稼ごうなんて<line-break>思っちゃいねぇだろうな。あばよ。<end>"
EN: "You'd better not think you can earn money after a sorry showing like that. So long.<end>"
KO: "네놈, 이 꼴로 돈을 벌 생각은 아니겠지. 잘 있어라.<end>"

## 1560063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。　　　　　<line-break>無事に到着できました。<line-break>　<line-break>約束のお礼です。すこし多めですが、<line-break>私の気持ちです、お納めください。<end>"

## 1560065

## 1560066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、もう着いたかよ。安心できる旅ってのは<line-break>ありがてぇな。ご苦労さん。<line-break>　<line-break>料金とボーナスだ、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>また、そのうち頼むかもしれねぇ、そんときも<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, we're here already? Can't beat a journey where you can relax. Good work. Here's your fee and a bonus. I might hire you again sometime, so I'll be counting on you then.<end>"
KO: "오, 벌써 도착했나. 안심되는 여행은 고맙군. 수고했어. 요금과 보너스다. 받아 줘. 또 언젠가 부탁할지도 모르니 그때도 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1560073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、やっと着いたかよ。<line-break>ご苦労さんな。ありがとよ。　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>護衛の料金だ、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>また、そのうち頼むかもしれねぇ、そんときも<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, we're finally here? Good work, and thanks. Here's your escort fee. I might hire you again sometime, so I'll be counting on you then.<end>"
KO: "오, 이제야 도착했군. 수고했다. 고맙다. 호위 비용이야, 받아 둬. 다음에 또 부탁할지도 모르니 그때도 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1560074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、いくら俺が頑丈な鉱山の男でも、<line-break>戦いでほっとかれたら、たまらねぇぜ。<line-break>あばよ！<end>"
EN: "Look, I may be a tough miner, but even I can't take being abandoned in a fight. So long!<end>"
KO: "아무리 내가 튼튼한 광산 사내라도 싸움 중에 내버려 두면 못 버틴다고. 잘 있어!<end>"

## 1560075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、やっと着いたかよ。<line-break>が、約束の期限には間に合わなかったようだな。<line-break>ま、しゃあないな、料金は半額だ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, we're finally here? But it looks like we missed the promised deadline. Well, nothing for it. You get half the fee.<end>"
KO: "오, 드디어 왔군. 하지만 약속한 기한에는 늦은 모양이네. 뭐 어쩔 수 없지, 비용은 절반이다.<end>"

## 1560076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。　　　　　　<line-break>護衛の料金とボーナスです。<line-break>　<line-break>…そうそう、世の中には、鍛冶屋でも<line-break>作れないような武器や防具があるそうですよ。<line-break>ま、噂なのでほんとかどうかは知りませんが。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. Here is your escort fee and a bonus. Oh, and apparently there are weapons and armor in this world that not even a blacksmith could make. It's only a rumor, mind you, so I can't say whether it's true.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 호위 비용과 보너스입니다. …참, 세상에는 대장장이도 만들 수 없는 무기나 방어구가 있다고 하더군요. 뭐, 소문이라 진짜인지는 모르겠지만요.<end>"

## 1560077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。　　　　　　<line-break>護衛の料金です。<line-break>　<line-break>…そうそう、あなたの武器のことですが、<line-break>そろそろ鍛えた方がいいかもしれませんね。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. Here is your escort fee. Oh, and about your weapon―you may want to have it strengthened soon.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 호위비입니다. …아, 그리고 당신 무기 말인데 이제 슬슬 단련하는 게 좋을지도 모르겠네요.<end>"

## 1560078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶんひどい目にあわされたもんだ。<line-break>あんた、ちゃんと武器を鍛えているのか？　　<line-break>　<line-break>ちゃんと護衛を果たしてくれなければ、報酬は<line-break>払えないね。<end>"
EN: "You put me through quite an ordeal. Have you been strengthening your weapon properly? If you can't do your job as an escort, I can't pay you.<end>"
KO: "정말 심한 꼴을 당했군. 당신, 무기 제대로 단련하고 있나? 호위를 제대로 못 해 주면 보수는 못 줘.<end>"

## 1560079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと到着しましたね。<line-break>でも、約束の期日には間に合いませんでした。<line-break>とりあえず、料金の半額だけお支払いします。<end>"
EN: "We've finally arrived, but we missed the promised date. For now, I will pay only half the fee.<end>"
KO: "드디어 도착했네요. 하지만 약속한 기한에는 맞추지 못했습니다. 일단 비용의 절반만 지급하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたのおかげで、無事到着か。<line-break>これが護衛の料金に、ボーナスだ。　　　　　　<line-break>どうもありがとう。<line-break>ところで、武器や防具を鍛える時は、<line-break>強度だけじゃなく特殊効果も鍛えておくと、<line-break>戦闘で有利になることがあるよ。覚えときな。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I arrived safely. Here's your escort fee and a bonus. Thank you. One more thing: when strengthening weapons or armor, improving not only their durability but also their special effects can give you an advantage in battle. Remember that.<end>"
KO: "네 덕에 무사히 도착했군. 이게 호위 요금에 보너스다. 고맙다. 그런데 무기나 방어구를 단련할 때는 강도뿐 아니라 특수효과도 단련해 두면 전투에서 유리할 때가 있어. 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 1560081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたのおかげでなんとか到着か。　　　　　<line-break>これが護衛の料金だ。<line-break>どうもありがとう。<line-break>ところで、武器や防具を選ぶ時は、精霊ＬＶ<line-break>にも注意してくれよ。このレベルが高いほど、<line-break>属性の影響が強くなるんだ、覚えておきな。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I managed to arrive somehow. Here's your escort fee. Thank you. One more thing: when choosing weapons or armor, pay attention to the Spirit ＬＶ as well. The higher that level, the stronger the elemental influence. Remember that.<end>"
KO: "네 덕분에 어떻게든 도착했군. 이게 호위 비용이다. 고맙다. 그리고 무기나 방어구를 고를 때는 정령 LV에도 주의해. 이 레벨이 높을수록 속성의 영향이 강해지니까 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 1560082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたのおかげで、ひどいめにあったな。<line-break>報酬は払わないよ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I had a terrible time. I'm not paying you.<end>"
KO: "당신 때문에 큰일을 겪었군. 보수는 안 줄 거야.<end>"

## 1560083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか到着したか。<line-break>しかし、約束の期限には間に合わなかったな。<line-break>悪いが報酬は半額だ。<end>"
EN: "We managed to arrive, but not by the promised deadline. Sorry, but you only get half the reward.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착했군. 하지만 약속한 기한에는 못 맞췄어. 미안하지만 보수는 절반이다.<end>"

## 1560084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>約束の報酬金と心ばかりのお礼です。<line-break>おかげさまで、無事に着くことができました。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. Here is the promised reward, along with a small token of my gratitude. Thanks to you, I arrived safe and sound.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 약속한 보수와 작은 감사의 뜻입니다. 덕분에 무사히 도착할 수 있었습니다.<end>"

## 1560085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、たどり着くことができました。<line-break>約束の報酬金です。<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "I managed to make it here somehow. Here is the promised reward. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착할 수 있었습니다. 약속한 보수입니다. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1560086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう…。<line-break>ああ…やっと、たどり着けたのですね。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>こんな目に会わせて、さらにお金を巻き上げよう<line-break>なんて思っていませんよね。<line-break>報酬金はケガの治療代にさせていただきます。<end>"
EN: "Whew... Ah, I've finally made it. You don't expect to put me through all that and then wring money out of me too, do you? I'll be using your reward to pay for the treatment of my injuries.<end>"
KO: "후우…. 아아… 드디어 도착했군요. 이런 꼴을 당하게 해 놓고 돈까지 뜯어 갈 생각은 아니겠죠. 보수금은 제 치료비로 쓰겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう…。<line-break>ああ…やっと、たどり着けたのですね。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>約束の期日には間に合いませんでした。<line-break>残念ながら、報酬は半額にさせていただきます。<end>"
EN: "Whew... Ah, I've finally made it. We missed the promised deadline. Unfortunately, I will pay only half your reward.<end>"
KO: "후우… 아아… 겨우 도착했군요. 약속한 기한에는 못 맞췄습니다. 유감이지만 보수는 절반만 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いずれまた、お願いするかもしれません。<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>護衛の料金とボーナスです、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "I may call upon you again someday. Thank you very much. Here is your escort fee and a bonus. Please accept them.<end>"
KO: "다시 부탁드릴 일이 있을지도 모르겠습니다. 정말 감사합니다. 호위 요금과 보너스입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1560089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとかたどり着けました。<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>護衛の料金です、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "I managed to make it here. Thank you very much. Here is your escort fee. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 도착했네요. 정말 감사합니다. 호위 비용입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1560090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これじゃ、護衛の意味がないじゃないですか。<line-break>もうあなたには頼みません。<line-break>料金も払いません。失礼します。<end>"

## 1560096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>僕、見かけによらず弱いんです。助かりました。<line-break>　<line-break>護衛の料金とボーナスです。<line-break>ほんとにありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. I may not look it, but I'm rather weak. You saved me. Here is your escort fee and a bonus. Thank you, truly.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 저, 보기보다 약하거든요. 살았어요. 호위비와 보너스입니다. 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1560097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>僕、こう見えても恐がりなんです。<line-break>護衛の料金です。ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. I may not look it, but I'm easily frightened. Here is your escort fee. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 저, 보기와 달리 겁이 많거든요. 호위 비용입니다. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1560098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちゃんと護衛してくださいよォ。<line-break>死ぬかと思っちゃったじゃないですか！<line-break>護衛の料金は僕の治療代にさせていただきます。<end>"
EN: "You're supposed to protect me! I thought I was going to die! I'll be using your escort fee to pay for my treatment.<end>"
KO: "제대로 호위해 주세요! 죽는 줄 알았잖아요! 호위 요금은 제 치료비로 쓰겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと到着しましたね。<line-break>約束の期限には遅れましたが…。<line-break>護衛の料金は、半額でいいですね。　　　　　　<line-break>…マスター、怒ってるだろうな、きっと。<line-break>マスターって、見かけによらず恐いんですよ。<line-break>怒ると、酒びんで殴るんです。…恐いなぁ。<end>"
EN: "We've finally arrived. We missed the promised deadline, though... Half the escort fee is enough, right? The master must be angry. The master is frightening despite appearances, you know. When angry, the master hits people with a wine bottle. That's frightening...<end>"
KO: "드디어 도착했네요. 약속한 기한에는 늦었지만…. 호위 요금은 반액이면 되죠. …마스터, 화났겠지. 분명. 마스터는 보기와 달리 무서워요. 화나면 술병으로 때린다고요. …무섭다.<end>"

## 1560100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。　　　　　<line-break>無事に到着できました。<line-break>　<line-break>約束のお礼です。すこし多めですが、<line-break>私の気持ちです、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. I arrived safely. Here is the promised payment. It's a little extra, as a token of my gratitude. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 무사히 도착했습니다. 약속한 보수입니다. 조금 많지만 제 마음이니 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1560101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>約束のお礼です、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Here is the promised payment. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 약속한 답례입니다, 받아주세요.<end>"

## 1560102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶんひどいめにあわされました。<line-break>ぜんぜん護衛になっていないじゃないですか。<line-break>お礼金は支払いません。失礼します！<end>"
EN: "You put me through a terrible ordeal. That was no escort at all. I will not pay you. Good day!<end>"
KO: "정말 호되게 당했잖아요. 이게 무슨 호위입니까. 사례금은 지급하지 않겠습니다. 실례하겠습니다!<end>"

## 1560103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>しかし、約束の期日には遅れてしまいましたね。<line-break>残念ながら、約束のお礼は半分です。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. However, we were late for the promised date. Regrettably, you will receive only half the agreed payment.<end>"
KO: "감사했습니다. 하지만 약속한 날짜에는 늦어버렸군요. 유감이지만 약속한 사례는 절반입니다.<end>"

## 1560104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ、なかなか楽しい旅であった。　　　　　<line-break>いや、ご苦労であったな。<line-break>これは、護衛の手間賃とボーナスだ。<line-break>これからは、ずっとそなたに護衛を依頼したい<line-break>ものだな。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. That was a most enjoyable journey. You have done well. Here is your escort fee and a bonus. I should like to entrust all my future journeys to you.<end>"
KO: "흠, 꽤 즐거운 여행이었군. 아니, 수고했다. 이건 호위 수고비와 보너스다. 앞으로도 계속 자네에게 호위를 맡기고 싶군.<end>"

## 1560105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ、着いたようだな。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>ご苦労だった。<line-break>護衛の手間賃だ。<line-break>この間の何とかいう護衛は一度も戦うことなく<line-break>旅させてくれたものだったが…。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. It seems we have arrived. Well done. Here is your escort fee. That other escort―whatever the name was―managed the whole journey without a single battle...<end>"
KO: "흠, 도착한 모양이군. 수고했다. 호위 수고비다. 지난번의 뭐라던 호위는 한 번도 싸우지 않고 여행하게 해 줬었는데….<end>"

## 1560106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ、ずいぶんなめにあわされたものだ。命が<line-break>惜しければ、二度と私の前に現れないことだな。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. You have put me through quite an ordeal. If you value your life, never show yourself before me again.<end>"
KO: "흠, 꽤 험한 꼴을 당했군. 목숨이 아깝다면 두 번 다시 내 앞에 나타나지 마라.<end>"

## 1560107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ、やっと着いたようだな。<line-break>約束の期日は過ぎてしまったが、まあ、よかろう。<line-break>護衛の手間賃は半額でよかったのだな。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. It seems we have finally arrived. The promised date has passed, but no matter. Half the escort fee was the agreement in such a case, was it not?<end>"
KO: "흠, 드디어 도착한 모양이군. 약속한 기한은 지났지만 뭐, 됐다. 호위 수고비는 반액이면 되는 거였지.<end>"

## 1560108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、おかげで何事もなくたどり着けたよ。<line-break>報酬だ、約束よりも若干多い。受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I arrived without incident. Here is your reward. It's slightly more than we agreed on. Take it.<end>"

## 1560109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、おかげでなんとかたどり着けたよ。<line-break>報酬だ、受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I managed to arrive somehow. Here is your reward. Take it.<end>"
KO: "자네 덕분에 어떻게든 도착했어. 보수다, 받아줘.<end>"

## 1560110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560113

## 1560114

## 1560115

## 1560116

## 1560124

## 1560125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやー、あんたに頼んで正解だったぜ。　　<line-break>まったく戦闘しないで済んだからな。<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬より多いが、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>完璧に護衛をこなしてくれたことのお礼だ。<end>"

## 1560129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml

## 1560130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560131

## 1560132

## 1560134

## 1560135

## 1560136

## 1560137

## 1560139

## 1560140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>おかげさまで、無事たどりつけました。<line-break>　<line-break>これは、護衛の報酬とボーナスです。<line-break>お受け取りください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. Thanks to you, I arrived safely. Here is your escort fee and a bonus. Please accept them.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 덕분에 무사히 도착했습니다. 이건 호위 보수와 보너스입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1560141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>おかげさまで、なんとかたどりつけました。<line-break>　<line-break>これは、護衛の報酬です。<line-break>お受け取りください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. Thanks to you, I managed to arrive somehow. Here is your escort fee. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 덕분에 무사히 도착했습니다. 이건 호위 보수입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1560142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、どうしてこんなに運がないのかしら…。<line-break>もっと強い人に守ってもらいたかったわ。<line-break>お支払いする気になりませんわ。失礼します。<end>"
EN: "Oh, why must my luck be so wretched...? I wish someone stronger had protected me. I have no intention of paying you. Good day.<end>"
KO: "아아, 왜 이렇게 운이 없는 걸까요…. 좀 더 강한 사람에게 지켜 달라고 했어야 했어요. 돈을 드릴 기분이 아니네요. 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1560143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっと到着しましたわ。<line-break>約束の期限には遅れましたわね。<line-break>護衛の報酬は半額だけ、お支払いします。<end>"
EN: "We have arrived at last. We missed the promised deadline. I shall pay only half the escort fee.<end>"

## 1560144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございましたですわ。<line-break>またそのうちお願いいたしますわ、ウフ。<line-break>　<line-break>これは、護衛していただいた料金ですわ。<line-break>それとボーナスですわね。<end>"
EN: "Thank you ever so much. I shall call upon you again sometime. Hee hee. Here is your escort fee, along with a bonus.<end>"
KO: "감사했어요. 다음에 또 부탁드릴게요, 우후. 이건 호위 요금이에요. 그리고 보너스고요.<end>"

## 1560145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございましたですわ。<line-break>今度はもう少しお手柔らかに、お願いしますわ。<line-break>これは、護衛していただいた料金ですわ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you ever so much. Do be a little gentler next time, won't you? Here is your escort fee.<end>"
KO: "감사했습니다. 다음에는 조금만 더 살살 부탁드릴게요. 이건 호위해 주신 대가예요.<end>"

## 1560146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml

## 1560148

## 1560149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1560151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>約束の期限には間に合いませんでしたが、<line-break>なんとか、無事たどり着くことができました。<line-break>護衛の報酬は半額でよろしいですね。<line-break>こちらです、お受け取りください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Although we missed the promised deadline, I did at least arrive safely. Half the escort fee will suffice, yes? Here it is. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "고맙습니다. 약속한 기한에는 늦었지만 어떻게든 무사히 도착했습니다. 호위 보수는 절반이면 되죠. 여기 있습니다, 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1560152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげで、看板娘に傷が付かずにすんだから、<line-break>酒場のマスターも大喜びよ、きっと。<line-break>どうもありがとね。<line-break>これは、護衛の報酬とボーナスね。<line-break>いつでも酒場に遊びに来てね。<end>"
EN: "The tavern master will be delighted that the star barmaid came through without a scratch, I'm sure. Thanks so much. Here's your escort fee and a bonus. Come visit the tavern anytime.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 간판 아가씨도 다치지 않았으니 술집 주인도 아주 기뻐할 거야. 정말 고마워. 이건 호위 보수와 보너스야. 언제든 술집에 놀러 와.<end>"

## 1560153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近も物騒になったものよね。やっぱり、護衛を<line-break>頼んでよかったわ。<line-break>護衛の報酬ね、どうもありがと。<end>"
EN: "Things have certainly become dangerous lately. I'm glad I hired an escort. Here's your fee. Thanks.<end>"
KO: "요즘은 참 흉흉해졌네. 역시 호위를 부탁하길 잘했어. 자, 호위 보수야. 고마워.<end>"

## 1560154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげで、ずいぶんひどいめにあったわよ！<line-break>もう二度とあんたには頼まないから！<end>"
EN: "You put me through a terrible ordeal! I'm never hiring you again!<end>"
KO: "덕분에 정말 호되게 당했잖아! 두 번 다시 너한테 부탁하지 않을 거야!<end>"

## 1560155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec156-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だいぶ遅れたけど、やっと着いたわね。<line-break>マスター、心配してなければいいけど…。<line-break>うちのマスターは心配性だから。<line-break>ああ、護衛の報酬ね、遅れた場合は半額だけ<line-break>払えばいいのよね。<end>"
EN: "We're very late, but we've finally arrived. I hope the master isn't worried... The master worries so much. Oh, your escort fee. Since we're late, I only have to pay half, right?<end>"
KO: "많이 늦었지만 드디어 도착했네. 마스터가 걱정하지 않았으면 좋겠는데…. 우리 마스터는 걱정이 많거든. 아, 호위 보수 말이지. 늦었을 때는 절반만 주면 되는 거지?<end>"

## 1570000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギルドから来てくれたのか？<line-break>助かった、近くの町まで連れていってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Did the Guild send you? Thank goodness. Take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "길드에서 와 준 건가? 살았다. 가까운 마을까지 데려다 줘.<end>"

## 1570001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやー、いいところに来てくれて助かったぜ。<line-break>いつかあんたと一緒に冒険してみたいもんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬より多いが、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>無事に助けてくれたことへのお礼だ。<end>"
EN: "You showed up just in time. I'd like to go adventuring with you someday. Here, take this. It's more than we agreed, but you earned it for getting me back safely.<end>"
KO: "이야, 좋은 때 와 줘서 살았어. 언젠가 당신과 함께 모험해 보고 싶군. 약속한 보수보다 많지만 받아 줘. 무사히 구해 준 데 대한 감사야.<end>"

## 1570002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう、危ないところだったな。<line-break>あんたが来てくれて助かったぜ。<line-break>約束の報酬だ、ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Whew, that was close. Good thing you came along. Here's the reward we agreed on. Thanks.<end>"
KO: "휴, 위험한 순간이었어. 네가 와 줘서 살았다. 약속한 보수야. 고마워.<end>"

## 1570003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんであんたみたいのが助っ人に来たんだ？<line-break>もっと強いやつが助けてくれりゃよかったのに。<line-break>　<line-break>まっとうな仕事をしてないんだ、<line-break>報酬は払わないからな。<end>"
EN: "Why did someone like you come to help? I wish someone stronger had come to rescue me. You didn't do the job properly, so I'm not paying you.<end>"
KO: "왜 당신 같은 사람이 구조하러 온 거야? 더 강한 녀석이 왔으면 좋았을 텐데. 제대로 일하지 않았으니 보수는 안 줄 거야.<end>"

## 1570004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、助かった…。ギルドからきてくれたのか。<line-break>近くの町まで連れていってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, thank goodness... Did the Guild send you? Take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "오오, 살았다…. 길드에서 와 준 건가. 가까운 마을까지 데려다줘.<end>"

## 1570005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたのおかげで助かったぜ。　　　　　<line-break>危うく命を落とすところだった。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬より多いが、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>無事に助けてくれたことへのお礼だ。<end>"
EN: "You saved my life. I nearly died back there. Here, take this. It's more than we agreed, but you earned it for getting me back safely.<end>"
KO: "네 덕분에 살았어. 하마터면 목숨을 잃을 뻔했지. 약속한 보수보다 많지만 받아 줘. 무사히 구해 준 답례야.<end>"

## 1570006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたの加勢がなけりゃ、危なかったぜ。<line-break>ほんとにいいところに現れてくれたな。<line-break>約束の報酬だ、ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "I'd have been in real trouble without your help. You couldn't have arrived at a better time. Here's the reward we agreed on. Thanks.<end>"
KO: "네 도움이 없었으면 위험했어. 정말 좋은 때 나타나 줬군. 약속한 보수다. 고마워.<end>"

## 1570007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんた、俺よりも弱いくらいなんじゃないか？<line-break>その程度の腕で救出なんかやるんじゃねえよ。<line-break>　<line-break>まっとうな仕事をしてないんだ、<line-break>報酬は払わないぞ。<end>"
EN: "Are you even as strong as I am? Don't take rescue jobs if that's the best you can do. You didn't do the job properly, so I'm not paying you.<end>"
KO: "당신, 나보다 약한 거 아냐? 그 정도 실력으로 구조 같은 걸 하지 마. 일을 제대로 안 했으니 보수는 못 줘.<end>"

## 1570008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは…。助けにきてくれたのですね。<line-break>近くの町まで連れていってください。<end>"
EN: "You... You came to rescue me. Please take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "당신은…. 구하러 와 주셨군요. 가까운 마을까지 데려다 주세요.<end>"

## 1570009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危ないところを助けていただき、<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。　　<line-break>　<line-break>お約束の報酬です。少し多めですが、<line-break>お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much for saving me from such danger. Here is the promised reward. It is a little more than we agreed, but please accept it.<end>"
KO: "위험한 곳에서 구해 주셔서 정말 감사합니다. 약속한 보수입니다. 조금 더 넣었으니 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのおかげで、なんとか戻ってくることが<line-break>できました。ありがとうございます。<line-break>お約束の報酬です。お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I managed to make it back. Thank you. Here is the promised reward. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "당신 덕분에 어떻게든 돌아올 수 있었습니다. 감사합니다. 약속한 보수입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生きた心地がしませんでした。<line-break>もっと修行して、高度な魔法を覚えなければ…。<line-break>　<line-break>あ…れ？…あれ？<line-break>か、皮袋を落としてしまったようです…。<line-break>残念ながら、報酬はお支払いできません。<end>"
EN: "I have never been so terrified. I must train harder and learn more advanced magic... Oh... What? Where is it? I-I seem to have dropped my purse... Regrettably, I cannot pay your reward.<end>"
KO: "살아 있는 기분이 아니었어요. 더 수련해서 고등 마법을 익혀야…. 어…라? 어라? 가, 가죽주머니를 떨어뜨린 것 같습니다…. 유감이지만 보수는 드릴 수 없겠네요.<end>"

## 1570012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、ギルドから来てくれたんだね！<line-break>助かったよ、近くの町まで連れてって。<end>"
EN: "Oh, the Guild sent you! What a relief. Take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "아, 길드에서 와 줬구나! 살았다. 가까운 마을까지 데려다 줘.<end>"

## 1570013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君のおかげで命拾いしたよ。もしも君をうたった<line-break>歌を聴いたら僕を思い出してね。<line-break>　<line-break>はい、約束のお金。ちょっと多めだけど、<line-break>とっといて。またどこかで会おうね。<line-break>絶対だよ。<end>"
EN: "You saved my life. If you ever hear a song about yourself, remember me. Here, the money we agreed on. It's a little extra, but keep it. We'll meet again somewhere. We will.<end>"
KO: "네 덕분에 살았어. 혹시 나중에 너를 노래한 노래를 들으면 나를 떠올려 줘. 자, 약속한 돈. 조금 더 넣었지만 받아 둬. 또 어디선가 만나자. 꼭이야.<end>"

## 1570014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげでなんとか助かったよ。　　　　　<line-break>どうもありがと。<line-break>　<line-break>はい、約束のお金。もうちょっと<line-break>修行を積めば、君はいい冒険者になれる、<line-break>…ような気がするよ。<end>"
EN: "I managed to survive, thanks to you. Much obliged. Here, the money we agreed on. With a little more training, you might make a good adventurer... I think.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 어떻게든 살았어. 정말 고마워. 자, 약속한 돈. 조금 더 수련하면 너는 좋은 모험자가 될… 것 같은 기분이 드네.<end>"

## 1570015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん、ひどい冒険だった。もっと修行してよ。<line-break>当分君を題材にした詩は作れそうにないなぁ。<line-break>　<line-break>こんなんじゃ、お金なんて払わないからね。<line-break>こっちがお金をもらいたいくらいだよ。<end>"
EN: "What an awful adventure. You need more training. I certainly won't be writing any poems about you for a while. I'm not paying for this. If anything, you should be paying me.<end>"
KO: "으음, 끔찍한 모험이었어. 좀 더 수련해 줘. 당분간은 널 소재로 시를 쓸 수 없겠네. 이래서는 돈도 안 줄 거야. 오히려 내가 돈을 받고 싶을 정도야.<end>"

## 1570016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、助けにきてくれたのですね。<line-break>どうか、近くの町まで連れていってください。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you have come to rescue me. Please take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "아, 구하러 와 주셨군요. 부디 가까운 마을까지 데려다주세요.<end>"

## 1570017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>これで無事に巡礼を続けられます。　<line-break>　<line-break>約束のお礼です。すこし多めですが、<line-break>私の気持ちです、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. Now I can safely continue my pilgrimage. Here is the promised payment. It is a little extra, as a token of my gratitude. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 이제 무사히 순례를 계속할 수 있겠습니다. 약속한 답례입니다. 조금 많지만 제 마음이니 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>これでなんとか巡礼を続けられます。<line-break>約束のお礼です、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. Now I can at least continue my pilgrimage. Here is the promised payment. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "정말 고맙습니다. 덕분에 순례를 계속할 수 있겠어요. 약속한 사례입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神さまはさまざまな試練を私に課すのですね。<line-break>あなたに支払うお金は、風の彼方におられる<line-break>我らが神に献上させていただきます。<line-break>さあ、あなたも一緒にお祈りしましょう。<line-break>我らが神ノトゥーンよ、罪深き大地の汚れを<line-break>そのまぶしき天上より吹き清められたまえ。<end>"
EN: "God does set many trials before me. I shall offer the money meant for you to our god beyond the winds. Come, let us pray together. O Notun, our god, from your radiant heaven, blow the taint from this sinful earth and cleanse it.<end>"
KO: "신께서는 제게 여러 시련을 내리시는군요. 당신에게 지불할 돈은 바람 저편에 계신 우리 신께 바치겠습니다. 자, 당신도 함께 기도합시다. 우리 신 노툰이여, 죄 깊은 대지의 더러움을 그 눈부신 천상에서 불어 씻어 주소서.<end>"

## 1570020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けに来てくれたのですね。<line-break>近くの町まで連れていってください。<end>"
EN: "You came to rescue me. Please take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "구하러 와 주셨군요. 가까운 마을까지 데려다주세요.<end>"

## 1570021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危ないところを助けていただき、<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。　　<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬金と心ばかりのお礼です。<line-break>どうか、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much for saving me from such danger. Here is the promised reward, along with a small token of my gratitude. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "위험한 순간에 구해 주셔서 정말 감사합니다. 약속한 보수금과 작은 성의입니다. 부디 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげで、なんとか難を逃れられました。<line-break>約束の報酬金です。<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I managed to escape danger. Here is the promised reward. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 어떻게든 위기를 벗어났습니다. 약속한 보수입니다. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1570023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶんとひどい目に会いました。<line-break>……もっと強い人だったらよかったのに。　<line-break>あなたへの報酬金は、私のケガの治療代として<line-break>使わせていただきますので、あしからず。<end>"
EN: "I have been through a dreadful ordeal. I only wish someone stronger had come... I shall be using your reward to pay for my injuries. I trust you understand.<end>"
KO: "정말 심한 일을 겪었습니다. ……좀 더 강한 분이었으면 좋았을 텐데요. 당신에게 드릴 보수는 제 상처 치료비로 쓰겠습니다. 양해하시죠.<end>"

## 1570024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、やはり神は私を見捨ててはいない。<line-break>近くの町まででいい、よろしく護衛を頼む。<end>"
EN: "Ah, so God has not forsaken me after all. I need only reach the nearest town. Escort me there.<end>"
KO: "오오, 역시 신은 나를 버리지 않으셨군. 가까운 마을까지만이라도 좋다. 호위를 부탁하네.<end>"

## 1570025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "……神の使いが、あなたについていたのです。<line-break>神のご加護に感謝しなさい。<line-break>これは神があなたに与えられる報酬です。<line-break>神に失礼なきよう、有意義に使われよ。<end>"
EN: "A messenger of God was at your side. Give thanks for divine protection. This is the reward God bestows upon you. Use it wisely, lest you offend God.<end>"
KO: "……신의 사자가 당신과 함께했습니다. 신의 가호에 감사하십시오. 이것은 신께서 당신에게 주시는 보상입니다. 신께 실례되지 않도록 뜻깊게 사용하십시오.<end>"

## 1570026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、きりぬけられましたな。　　　<line-break>多少の戦いは神の試練です。<line-break>神に感謝しなさい。<line-break>これは神があなたに与えられる報酬です。<line-break>神に失礼なきよう、有意義に使われよ。<end>"
EN: "Managed to make it through somehow. A little fighting is merely a trial from God. Give thanks. This is the reward God bestows upon you. Use it wisely, lest you offend God.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 위기를 넘겼군요. 어느 정도의 싸움은 신의 시련입니다. 신께 감사하십시오. 이것은 신께서 당신에게 내리신 보수입니다. 신께 실례되지 않도록 의미 있게 쓰십시오.<end>"

## 1570027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "我を危地に落とそうとする汝は、闇の手の者か？<line-break>神のいかづちが落ちる前に改心するがよい。<line-break>　<line-break>報酬金だと！なんたる不届きものか！<line-break>神よ！この邪なるものに天罰を落とし給え！<end>"
EN: "Are you an agent of darkness, seeking to cast me into peril? Repent before God's thunderbolt strikes you down. A reward, you say? What insolence! O God, visit divine punishment upon this wicked soul!<end>"
KO: "나를 위험에 빠뜨리려 한 그대는 어둠의 수하인가? 신의 벼락이 내리기 전에 회개하라. 보수금이라고! 이 얼마나 불경한 자인가! 신이여! 이 사악한 자에게 천벌을 내리소서!<end>"

## 1570028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、やっと救助が来たのか。<line-break>近くの町まで連れていってくれ。<line-break>頼むぞ。<end>"
EN: "At last, rescue has come. Take me to the nearest town. I am counting on you.<end>"
KO: "오오, 드디어 구조가 온 건가. 가까운 마을까지 데려다줘. 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1570029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげで、任務を遂行できる。　　　　　<line-break>礼を言うぞ。冒険者よ。<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬と、これは私からの心付けだ。<line-break>遠慮なく受け取り給え。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I can complete my mission. You have my gratitude, adventurer. Here is the promised reward, along with a gratuity from me. Accept it without reservation.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 임무를 수행할 수 있다. 고맙다, 모험자여. 약속한 보수와 이건 내 성의다. 사양 말고 받아라.<end>"

## 1570030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、助かったか。<line-break>約束の報酬だ。受け取り給え。<line-break>ここでよい、さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "It seems I survived. Here is the promised reward. Take it. This will do. Farewell.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 살았군. 약속한 보수다. 받아라. 여기면 됐다. 잘 있어.<end>"

## 1570031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様、三流冒険者め。<line-break>ロストール兵でも、まだましな働きをするぞ。<line-break>誰が報酬など払うか！<end>"
EN: "You third-rate adventurer! Even a Rostorl soldier would have done a better job. You shall receive no reward from me!<end>"
KO: "네놈, 삼류 모험자 같으니! 로스톨 병사도 이보단 잘하겠다. 누가 보수 같은 걸 주나!<end>"

## 1570032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "오오, 드디어 구조가 온 건가. 가까운 마을까지 데려다줘. 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1570033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前の働きは目覚しいものがあった。　<line-break>礼を言うぞ。冒険者よ。<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬と、これは私からの心付けだ。<line-break>遠慮なく受け取り給え。<end>"

## 1570037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前の働きは実にすばらしかった。　　　<line-break>礼を言うぞ。冒険者よ。<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬と、これは私からの心付けだ。<line-break>遠慮なく受け取り給え。<end>"

## 1570038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、助かったか…。<line-break>約束の報酬だ。受け取り給え。<line-break>ここでよい、さらばだ。<end>"
EN: "It seems I survived... Here is the promised reward. Take it. This will do. Farewell.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 살았군…. 약속한 보수다. 받아라. 여기면 됐다. 잘 있어.<end>"

## 1570039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様、三流冒険者め。誰が報酬など払うか！<line-break>監獄にぶちこまれたくなければ、<line-break>さっさと失せろ。<end>"
EN: "You third-rate adventurer! You shall receive no reward from me. Begone, unless you wish to be thrown into prison!<end>"
KO: "네놈, 삼류 모험가 같으니. 누가 보수 따위를 주겠나! 감옥에 처박히기 싫으면 당장 꺼져.<end>"

## 1570040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、助けに来てくれたか。<line-break>すまんの、若いの。<line-break>近くの町までよろしく頼むぞい。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you've come to help. Much obliged, youngster. Take me to the nearest town, if you would.<end>"
KO: "오오, 구하러 와 줬구나. 미안하네, 젊은이. 가까운 마을까지 잘 부탁하네.<end>"

## 1570041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間にもこんなに強いのがおるとはな。<line-break>お前さん、なかなかやるのお。<line-break>ほい、報酬金とこれはチップじゃ。<end>"
EN: "I never knew a human could be so strong. You're quite something. Here, your reward and a tip.<end>"
KO: "인간 중에도 이렇게 강한 자가 있구나. 자네 꽤 하는데. 자, 보수와 이건 팁이다.<end>"

## 1570042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、おかげで助かったわい。<line-break>恩に着るぞい、若いの。ありがとさん。<line-break>ほい、報酬金じゃ。<end>"
EN: "Well, you got me out alive. I'm in your debt, youngster. Thank you. Here, your reward.<end>"
KO: "이런 이런, 덕분에 살았네. 은혜 잊지 않겠네, 젊은이. 고맙네. 자, 보수일세.<end>"

## 1570043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、ひどいめにあわされてしまったの。<line-break>報酬金なんかやらんぞい。<line-break>わしの仲間がおったら、よかったのにのお。<end>"
EN: "Well, that was a wretched ordeal. You're getting no reward from me. If only one of my companions had been there.<end>"
KO: "원 참, 지독한 꼴을 당했구먼. 보수 같은 건 못 주겠네. 내 동료들이 있었으면 좋았을 텐데 말이야.<end>"

## 1570044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああっ、助けに来てくれたのですか！<line-break>お願いです、<line-break>近くの町まで連れていってください。<end>"
EN: "Oh! Have you come to rescue me? Please, take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "아, 구하러 와 주신 겁니까! 부탁드립니다. 가까운 마을까지 데려다 주세요.<end>"

## 1570045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危ないところを助けていただき、<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。　　<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬金と心ばかりのお礼です。<line-break>どうか、お受け取りください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much for saving me from such danger. Here is the promised reward, along with a small token of my gratitude. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "위험한 곳에서 구해 주셔서 정말 감사합니다. 약속한 보수와 작은 사례입니다. 부디 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのおかげで、なんとか戻ってくることが<line-break>できました。<line-break>　<line-break>これは約束の報酬金です。<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I managed to make it back. Here is the promised reward. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "당신 덕분에 어떻게든 돌아올 수 있었습니다. 이건 약속한 보수입니다. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1570047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生きた心地がしませんでした。<line-break>こんな目に会わせて、さらにお金を巻き上げよう<line-break>なんて思っていませんよね。<line-break>報酬金はケガの治療代にさせていただきます。<line-break>さよなら。<end>"
EN: "I have never been so terrified. Surely you do not expect to put me through such an ordeal and then wring money out of me as well. I shall use your reward to pay for my injuries. Goodbye.<end>"
KO: "살아 있는 기분이 아니었습니다. 이런 일을 겪게 해 놓고 돈까지 받아 가려는 건 아니겠죠. 보수는 부상 치료비로 쓰겠습니다. 안녕히.<end>"

## 1570048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ…、助けに来てくれたのですね。<line-break>近くの町までで結構ですので、<line-break>この恐ろしい場所から連れ帰ってください。<end>"
EN: "Ah... You have come to rescue me. The nearest town will be quite far enough. Please take me away from this dreadful place.<end>"
KO: "아아… 구하러 와주셨군요. 가까운 마을까지만이라도 좋으니 이 무서운 곳에서 데려가 주세요.<end>"

## 1570049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危ないところを助けていただき、<line-break>どのように感謝してよいやら……。　　<line-break>ありがとうございました。<line-break>こちらはお礼金とチップでございます。<line-break>本当にありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "How can I ever thank you for saving me from such danger...? Thank you. Here is your payment, together with a tip. Truly, thank you.<end>"
KO: "위험한 곳에서 구해 주셔서 어떻게 감사를 드려야 할지 모르겠습니다……. 감사합니다. 이것은 사례금과 팁입니다. 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1570050

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1570051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1570052

## 1570053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1570054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml

## 1570055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危うく死ぬところでしたよ。<line-break>もっと強い人が来てくれたらよかったのに……。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたへの報酬金は、私のケガの治療代として<line-break>使わせていただきますので、あしからず。<end>"

## 1570056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1570057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやっはっは、助かった助かった。　　<line-break>お礼はこれでよろしいか？<line-break>それと、ボーナスじゃな、ほれ。<line-break>はじめからあんたを護衛に雇っておけば<line-break>よかったのお。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha! Saved at last. Will this do for your reward? And here's a bonus. I should have hired you as my escort from the start.<end>"
KO: "하하하, 살았다 살았어. 사례는 이 정도면 되겠나? 그리고 보너스도 있지, 자. 처음부터 자네를 호위로 고용했으면 좋았을 걸 그랬구먼.<end>"

## 1570058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう、なんとか九死に一生を得たか。<line-break>いや、ご苦労さん。ほい、お駄賃。<end>"
EN: "Whew, I escaped death by the skin of my teeth. Fine work. Here, something for your trouble.<end>"
KO: "후우, 간신히 죽을 고비를 넘겼군. 수고했어. 자, 사례금.<end>"

## 1570059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う…、助けが来たと思ったら、<line-break>とんでもない素人冒険者とはのお…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>礼などやらんぞ。ギルドの親父め、<line-break>もうちっとましなのに依頼すればよいものを。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... I thought rescue had come, only to find some hopeless novice... You'll get no payment from me. That old fool at the Guild should have sent someone halfway competent.<end>"
KO: "으… 구원이 왔나 했더니 터무니없는 풋내기 모험자라니…. 사례 따윈 안 줄 거다. 길드 영감도 좀 더 나은 녀석에게 맡길 것이지.<end>"

## 1570060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けに来てくれたのか。<line-break>すまないが、近くの町まで頼む。<end>"
EN: "You came to rescue me? Sorry to trouble you, but take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "구하러 와 준 건가. 미안하지만 가까운 마을까지 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1570061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうよ。恩に着るぜ。<line-break>報酬とボーナスだ、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>いつでも、酒場に寄ってくれな。<end>"
EN: "Thanks. I owe you one. Here's your reward and a bonus. Drop by the tavern anytime.<end>"
KO: "고맙다. 은혜 잊지 않지. 보수와 보너스야, 받아. 언제든 술집에 들러 줘.<end>"

## 1570062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たすかったぜ。<line-break>約束の報酬だ、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>ありがとうよ、じゃあな。<end>"
EN: "You saved me. Here's the promised reward. Thanks. See you around.<end>"
KO: "살았어. 약속한 보수다, 받아 줘. 고맙다, 그럼 잘 가.<end>"

## 1570063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ひでぇめにあったぜ、まったく。<line-break>酒場に姿ぁ見せたら、ぶっ殺すからな。あばよ。<end>"
EN: "That was a bloody nightmare. Show your face at the tavern and I'll kill you. Get lost.<end>"
KO: "정말 호되게 당했군, 젠장. 술집에 얼굴 내밀면 가만 안 둘 거다. 잘 있어.<end>"

## 1570064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아, 구하러 와 주셨군요. 부디 가까운 마을까지 데려다주세요.<end>"

## 1570065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>このご恩は一生忘れません。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>約束のお礼です。すこし多めですが、<line-break>私の気持ちです、お納めください。<end>"

## 1570066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "감사합니다. 어떻게든 살았습니다. 약속한 사례입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "정말 호되게 당했어요. 사례금은 지급하지 않겠습니다. 실례할게요!<end>"

## 1570068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1570069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間にもこんなに強いのがおるとはな。<line-break>お前さん、なかなかやるのお。<line-break>ほい、報酬金とこれはチップじゃ。<end>"
EN: "I never knew a human could be so strong. You're quite something. Here, your reward and a tip.<end>"

## 1570070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1570071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれ、ひどいめにあわされてしまったの。<line-break>報酬金なんかやらんぞい。<line-break>わしの仲間がおったら、よかったのにのお。<end>"
EN: "Well, that was a wretched ordeal. You're getting no reward from me. If only one of my companions had been there.<end>"

## 1570072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、助けに来てくれたってか。<line-break>ありがてぇ、近くの町まで連れてってくれ。<end>"

## 1570073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、助かったぜい。死んじまうかと思ったが<line-break>またカカァのところに戻れるぜ。ありがとよ。<line-break>　<line-break>救出の料金とボーナスだ、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>またピンチになったら、あんたを待ってるぜ。<end>"
EN: "You saved me! I thought I was done for, but now I can get back to the missus. Thanks. Here's the rescue fee and a bonus. If I'm ever in trouble again, I'll be waiting for you.<end>"
KO: "오, 살았다. 죽는 줄 알았는데 다시 마누라한테 돌아갈 수 있겠군. 고맙다. 구조 비용이랑 보너스야, 받아 둬. 또 위기에 빠지면 너를 기다리고 있을게.<end>"

## 1570074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、助かったぜい。ありがとよ。これでまた　<line-break>うちのカカァの顔が拝めるってか。<line-break>　<line-break>救出の料金だ、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>またピンチになったら、あんたを待ってるぜ。<end>"
EN: "You saved me! Thanks. So I get to see the missus's face again after all. Here's the rescue fee. If I'm ever in trouble again, I'll be waiting for you.<end>"
KO: "오, 살았다. 고마워. 이걸로 또 우리 마누라 얼굴을 볼 수 있겠군. 구조 비용이다, 받아 줘. 또 위기에 빠지면 널 기다릴게.<end>"

## 1570075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、いくら俺が頑丈な鉱山の男でも、<line-break>戦いでほっとかれたらたまらねぇぜ。あばよ！<end>"
EN: "I may be a tough miner, but even I can't stand being abandoned in the middle of a fight. I'm off!<end>"
KO: "아무리 내가 튼튼한 광산 사내라도 전투에서 내버려두면 못 견딘다고. 잘 가!<end>"

## 1570076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、助けに来てくれたのですね。<line-break>どうか、近くの町まで連れて行ってください。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you have come to rescue me. Please take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "아, 구하러 와 주셨군요. 부디 가까운 마을까지 데려다주세요.<end>"

## 1570077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>助けてくれたお礼とボーナスです。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…それから、噂では世界には鍛冶屋でも作れない<line-break>ような強力な武器があるそうですよ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Here is your reward for rescuing me, along with a bonus. By the way, rumor has it that there are powerful weapons in this world beyond the skill of any smith to make.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 구해 주신 사례와 보너스입니다. …그리고 소문으로는 세상에 대장간에서도 만들 수 없는 강력한 무기가 있다더군요.<end>"

## 1570078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>助けてくれたお礼です。<line-break>　<line-break>…それから、あなたの武器、そろそろ鍛えた方が<line-break>いいかもしれませんね。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Here is your reward for rescuing me. By the way, it may be time to have your weapon improved.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 구해 주신 답례입니다. …그리고 당신 무기, 이제 슬슬 단련하는 편이 좋을지도 모르겠네요.<end>"

## 1570079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶんひどい目にあわされたもんだ。<line-break>あんた、ちゃんと武器を鍛えているのか？<line-break>　<line-break>助けに来てくれても、かばってくれなきゃ<line-break>意味がないじゃないか。<end>"
EN: "You put me through a dreadful ordeal. Do you even keep your weapon properly tempered? Coming to the rescue means nothing if you don't protect the person you're rescuing.<end>"
KO: "정말 심한 꼴을 당했군. 당신, 무기는 제대로 단련하고 있는 거야? 구하러 와도 날 지켜 주지 않으면 의미가 없잖아.<end>"

## 1570080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、助けに来てくれたのか。<line-break>近くの町まで連れて行ってくれ、頼む。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you've come to rescue me. Take me to the nearest town, please.<end>"
KO: "오오, 구하러 와 준 건가. 가까운 마을까지 데려다줘. 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1570081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたのおかげで、命拾いしたよ。　　　　　　<line-break>どうもありがとうな。<line-break>助けてくれたお礼とボーナスだ。<line-break>ところで、武器や防具を鍛える時は、強度だけ<line-break>じゃなく特殊効果も鍛えておくと、戦闘で<line-break>有利になることがあるから覚えておきなよ。<end>"
EN: "You saved my life. Thank you. Here's your reward and a bonus. One more thing: when tempering weapons or armor, don't just improve their strength. Enhancing their special effects can give you an advantage in battle. Remember that.<end>"
KO: "네 덕분에 목숨을 건졌어. 정말 고맙다. 구해 준 사례와 보너스야. 그런데 무기나 방어구를 단련할 때는 강도뿐 아니라 특수 효과도 강화해 두면 전투에서 유리할 때가 있으니 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 1570082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたのおかげで、なんとか助かったよ。　　　<line-break>どうもありがとうな。<line-break>これは助けてくれたお礼だ。<line-break>ところで、武器や防具を選ぶ時は、精霊レベル<line-break>にも注意してくれよ。このレベルが高いほど、<line-break>属性の影響が強くなるんだ、覚えておきな。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I made it out alive. Thank you. Here's your reward. One more thing: when choosing weapons or armor, pay attention to the spirit level. The higher it is, the stronger the elemental influence. Remember that.<end>"
KO: "네 덕분에 어떻게든 살았어. 정말 고맙다. 이건 구해 준 사례야. 그런데 무기나 방어구를 고를 때는 정령 레벨도 살펴봐. 이 레벨이 높을수록 속성의 영향이 강해지니까 기억해 둬.<end>"

## 1570083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1570085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危ないところを助けていただき、<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。　　<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬金と心ばかりのお礼です。<line-break>どうか、お受け取りください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much for saving me from such danger. Here is the promised reward, along with a small token of my gratitude. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "위험한 곳에서 구해 주셔서 정말 감사합니다. 약속한 보수와 작은 사례입니다. 부디 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのおかげで、なんとか戻ってくることが<line-break>できました。<line-break>　<line-break>これは約束の報酬金です。<line-break>ありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I managed to make it back. Here is the promised reward. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "당신 덕분에 어떻게든 돌아올 수 있었습니다. 이건 약속한 보수입니다. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1570087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "生きた心地がしませんでした。<line-break>こんな目に会わせて、さらにお金を巻き上げよう<line-break>なんて思っていませんよね。<line-break>報酬金はケガの治療代にさせていただきます。<line-break>さよなら。<end>"
EN: "I have never been so terrified. Surely you do not expect to put me through such an ordeal and then wring money out of me as well. I shall use your reward to pay for my injuries. Goodbye.<end>"
KO: "살아 있는 기분이 아니었습니다. 이런 일을 겪게 해 놓고 돈까지 받아 가려는 건 아니겠죠. 보수는 부상 치료비로 쓰겠습니다. 안녕히.<end>"

## 1570088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、助けに来てくれたのですね。<line-break>近くの町まで連れて行ってください。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you have come to rescue me. Please take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "아, 구하러 와 주셨군요. 가까운 마을까지 데려가 주세요.<end>"

## 1570089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげで、助かりました。<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。　　<line-break>　<line-break>お礼金とボーナスです。<line-break>ほんとうにありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I am safe. Thank you very much. Here is your payment and a bonus. Truly, thank you.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 살았습니다. 정말 감사합니다. 사례금과 보너스입니다. 정말 고맙습니다.<end>"

## 1570090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、助かりました。<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>　<line-break>お礼金です、受け取ってください。<line-break>ほんとうにありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "I managed to survive. Thank you very much. Here is your payment. Please accept it. Truly, thank you.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 살았습니다. 정말 감사합니다. 사례금입니다, 받아 주세요. 정말 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1570091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これじゃ、助けていただいたとは言えませんね。<line-break>お金も払いません。<line-break>失礼します。<end>"
EN: "I could hardly call that a rescue. I will not be paying you. Good day.<end>"
KO: "이래서는 도움을 받았다고 할 수 없겠네요. 돈도 드리지 않겠습니다. 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1570097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>僕、見かけによらず弱いんです。助かりました。<line-break>　<line-break>これはお礼とボーナスです。<line-break>ほんとうにありがとうございました。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. I may not look it, but I'm rather weak. You saved me. Here is your payment and a bonus. Truly, thank you.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 저, 보기보다 약하거든요. 살았습니다. 이건 사례금과 보너스입니다. 정말 감사했습니다.<end>"

## 1570098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。<line-break>僕、ケンカはからっきしだめなんです。<line-break>助けてくれたお礼です、受け取ってください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. I'm completely hopeless in a fight. Here is your reward for rescuing me. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 저는 싸움에는 영 소질이 없어서요. 도와주신 사례입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕に恨みでもあるんですか！？<line-break>死ぬかと思っちゃったじゃないですか！　　　　<line-break>救出のお礼なんてできませんからね！<line-break>誰が払うもんか。<line-break>僕のケガの治療代を払ってもらいたいくらいだ。<end>"
EN: "Do you have some grudge against me?! I thought I was going to die! You certainly won't get a rescue fee from me. Why should I pay? If anything, you should pay for my injuries.<end>"
KO: "저한테 원한이라도 있습니까!? 죽는 줄 알았잖아요! 구조 사례 같은 건 못 해 드립니다! 누가 주겠어요. 오히려 제 치료비를 받아야 할 판이에요.<end>"

## 1570100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、助けにきてくれたのですね。<line-break>どうか、近くの町まで連れていってください。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you have come to rescue me. Please take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "아, 구하러 와 주셨군요. 부디 가까운 마을까지 데려다주세요.<end>"

## 1570101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>このご恩は一生忘れません。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>約束のお礼です。すこし多めですが、<line-break>私の気持ちです、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I shall never forget this kindness. Here is the promised payment. It is a little extra, as a token of my gratitude. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 이 은혜는 평생 잊지 않겠습니다. 약속한 보답입니다. 조금 많지만 제 마음이니 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。<line-break>なんとか助かりました。<line-break>約束のお礼です、お納めください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I managed to survive. Here is the promised payment. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 어떻게든 살았습니다. 약속한 사례입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶんひどいめにあわされました。<line-break>お礼金は支払いません。失礼します！<end>"
EN: "You put me through a dreadful ordeal. I will not be paying you. Good day!<end>"
KO: "정말 호되게 당했어요. 사례금은 지급하지 않겠습니다. 실례할게요!<end>"

## 1570104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、助けに来たのだな？<line-break>近くの町までわしの護衛を頼むぞ。<end>"
EN: "Ah, so you have come to my rescue. Escort me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "오오, 구하러 와 준 게냐? 가까운 마을까지 나를 호위해 다오.<end>"

## 1570105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者とはいえ、なかなか見事な<line-break>戦いっぷりであった。礼を申すぞ。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>これは謝礼とボーナスだ。<line-break>そなたのような屈強の冒険者はわしの護衛に雇い<line-break>たいものだな。<end>"
EN: "For an adventurer, you acquitted yourself admirably in battle. You have my thanks. Here is your payment and a bonus. I should like to hire a stalwart adventurer such as you as my escort.<end>"
KO: "모험가치고는 제법 훌륭한 싸움이었다. 감사하지. 이건 사례와 보너스다. 그대 같은 강한 모험가라면 내 호위로 고용하고 싶군.<end>"

## 1570106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ、なんとか戻ってこれたか。<line-break>ご苦労だった。<line-break>これは謝礼だ、受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Hm. So I made it back after all. Well done. Here is your payment. Take it.<end>"
KO: "흠, 어떻게든 돌아왔나. 수고했다. 이건 사례다. 받아라.<end>"

## 1570107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふむ、ずいぶんなめにあわされたものだ。命が<line-break>惜しければ、二度と私の前に現れないことだな。<end>"
EN: "Hm. You have put me through quite an ordeal. If you value your life, never show yourself before me again.<end>"
KO: "흠, 꽤 험한 꼴을 당했군. 목숨이 아깝다면 두 번 다시 내 앞에 나타나지 마라.<end>"

## 1570108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "助けに来てくれたのか。<line-break>近くの町まで連れてってくれないか。<end>"
EN: "You came to rescue me? Would you take me to the nearest town?<end>"
KO: "구하러 와 준 건가. 가까운 마을까지 데려다주지 않겠나.<end>"

## 1570109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、よく助けに来てくれたね。<line-break>助けてくれた謝礼だ、君の契約よりも若干多いが<line-break>受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "You did well to come to my rescue. Here is your payment. It is slightly more than your contract stipulated, but please accept it.<end>"
KO: "잘도 구하러 와 줬군. 구해 준 사례다. 계약한 금액보다 조금 많지만 받아 줘.<end>"

## 1570110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、よく助けに来てくれたね。<line-break>助けてくれた謝礼だ、受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "You did well to come to my rescue. Here is your payment. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "잘 구하러 와 줬군. 구해 준 답례다. 받아 줘.<end>"

## 1570111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "君、僕にこんなケガをさせておいて、<line-break>謝礼をもらえるなんて考えてないだろうね。<line-break>体が痛むので失礼するよ。<end>"

## 1570117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげで、無事に戻ってこれたな。　　　　　　<line-break>ありがとさんよ。<line-break>　<line-break>助けてくれた謝礼だ、あんたが契約したのよりも<line-break>いくらか多いかもしれねえが、<line-break>遠慮なく受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, I made it back safely. Much obliged. Here's your payment for rescuing me. It may be a bit more than your contract called for, but take it.<end>"

## 1570118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんとか、戻ってこれたな。<line-break>ありがとさんよ。<line-break>助けてくれた謝礼だ。受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Well, I made it back somehow. Much obliged. Here's your payment for rescuing me. Take it.<end>"
KO: "어떻게든 돌아왔군. 고맙다. 구해 준 사례야. 받아 줘.<end>"

## 1570119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶんひどいめにあったな。<line-break>とても感謝する気にはなれんな。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>これからは、もっと修行して強くなってから<line-break>仕事を受けてくれよな。<line-break>じゃあな。<end>"

KO: "자네, 나를 이렇게 다치게 해 놓고 사례를 받을 생각은 아니겠지? 몸이 아프니 이만 가 보겠네.<end>"

## 1570124
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、助けか？<line-break>近くの町まで連れてってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, is that help? Take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "오, 구조인가? 가까운 마을까지 데려다 줘.<end>"

## 1570125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやっはっは、助かった助かった。　　<line-break>お礼はこれでよろしいか？<line-break>それと、ボーナスじゃな、ほれ。<line-break>はじめからあんたを護衛に雇っておけば<line-break>よかったのお。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha! Saved at last. Will this do for your reward? And here's a bonus. I should have hired you as my escort from the start.<end>"
KO: "하하하, 살았다 살았어. 사례는 이 정도면 되겠나? 그리고 보너스도 있지, 자. 처음부터 자네를 호위로 고용했으면 좋았을 걸 그랬구먼.<end>"

## 1570126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう、なんとか九死に一生を得たか。<line-break>いや、ご苦労さん。ほい、お駄賃。<end>"
EN: "Whew, I escaped death by the skin of my teeth. Fine work. Here, something for your trouble.<end>"
KO: "후우, 간신히 죽을 고비를 넘겼군. 수고했어. 자, 사례금.<end>"

## 1570127
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う…、助けが来たと思ったら、<line-break>とんでもない素人冒険者とはのお…。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>礼などやらんぞ。ギルドの親父め、<line-break>もうちっとましなのに依頼すればいいものを。<end>"
EN: "Ugh... I thought rescue had come, only to find some hopeless novice... You'll get no payment from me. That old fool at the Guild should have sent someone halfway competent.<end>"
KO: "으… 구원이 왔나 했더니 터무니없는 풋내기 모험자라니…. 사례 따윈 안 줄 거다. 길드 영감도 좀 더 나은 녀석에게 맡길 것이지.<end>"

## 1570128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ギルドから来てくれたのか？<line-break>助かった、近くの町まで連れていってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Did the Guild send you? Thank goodness. Take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "길드에서 와 준 건가? 살았다. 가까운 마을까지 데려다 줘.<end>"

## 1570129
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやー、いいところに来てくれて助かったぜ。<line-break>いつかあんたと一緒に冒険してみたいもんだ。<line-break>　<line-break>約束の報酬より多いが、受け取ってくれ。<line-break>無事に助けてくれたことへのお礼だ。<end>"
EN: "You showed up just in time. I'd like to go adventuring with you someday. Here, take this. It's more than we agreed, but you earned it for getting me back safely.<end>"
KO: "이야, 좋은 때 와 줘서 살았어. 언젠가 당신과 함께 모험해 보고 싶군. 약속한 보수보다 많지만 받아 줘. 무사히 구해 준 데 대한 감사야.<end>"

## 1570130
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ふう、危ないところだったな。<line-break>あんたが来てくれて助かったぜ。<line-break>約束の報酬だ、ありがとう。<end>"
EN: "Whew, that was close. Good thing you came along. Here's the reward we agreed on. Thanks.<end>"
KO: "휴, 위험한 순간이었어. 네가 와 줘서 살았다. 약속한 보수야. 고마워.<end>"

## 1570131
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんであんたみたいのが助っ人に来たんだ？<line-break>もっと強いやつが助けてくれりゃよかったのに。<line-break>　<line-break>まっとうな仕事をしてないんだ、<line-break>報酬は払わないからな。<end>"
EN: "Why did someone like you come to help? I wish someone stronger had come to rescue me. You didn't do the job properly, so I'm not paying you.<end>"
KO: "왜 당신 같은 사람이 구조하러 온 거야? 더 강한 녀석이 왔으면 좋았을 텐데. 제대로 일하지 않았으니 보수는 안 줄 거야.<end>"

## 1570132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、助けに来てくれたのですね。<line-break>どうか、近くの町まで連れて行ってください。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you have come to rescue me. Please take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "아, 구하러 와 주셨군요. 부디 가까운 마을까지 데려다주세요.<end>"

## 1570133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。あなたさまのおかげで<line-break>無事に戻ってくることができました。<line-break>　<line-break>助けていただいたお礼です。<line-break>契約の料金よりも多いですが、私の気持ちです。<line-break>どうか、お受け取りください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Thanks to you, I returned safely. Here is my payment for your help. It is more than the contracted fee, but please accept it as a token of my gratitude.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 당신 덕분에 무사히 돌아올 수 있었습니다. 도와주신 답례입니다. 계약한 금액보다 많지만 제 마음입니다. 부디 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございました。あなたさまのおかげで<line-break>なんとか戻ってくることができました。<line-break>　<line-break>これは、助けていただいたお礼です。<line-break>お受け取りください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. Thanks to you, I managed to make it back. Here is my payment for your help. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. 당신 덕분에 어떻게든 돌아올 수 있었습니다. 이건 구해주신 답례입니다. 받아주세요.<end>"

## 1570135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ずいぶんひどいめに<line-break>あわせてくださいましたわね。<line-break>お礼金はお支払いできませんわ。失礼します。<end>"
EN: "You have put me through quite an ordeal. I cannot possibly pay you. Good day.<end>"
KO: "정말 심한 꼴을 당하게 해 주셨군요. 사례금은 드릴 수 없겠네요. 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1570136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あっ、助けに来てくれたんだァ。<line-break>近くの町まで連れてってくれる？　お願いね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, you came to rescue me! Will you take me to the nearest town? Please?<end>"
KO: "앗, 구하러 와 줬구나. 가까운 마을까지 데려다줄래? 부탁해.<end>"

## 1570137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ、楽しかった。ちょっと恐かったけど、<line-break>いい詩が書けそう。<line-break>はい、これ助けてくれたお礼。<line-break>え？　ギルドの契約よりも多いって？<line-break>あたしの気持ちだからとっておいてよ。<line-break>ほんとにどうもありがとね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that was fun! A little frightening, but I think I can write a good poem about it. Here, this is for rescuing me. What? It's more than the Guild contract? It's a token of my gratitude, so keep it. Thank you so much.<end>"
KO: "아아, 즐거웠어. 조금 무섭긴 했지만 좋은 시가 나올 것 같아. 자, 구해 준 사례야. 뭐? 길드 계약보다 많다고? 내 마음이니까 받아 둬. 정말 고마워.<end>"

## 1570138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁ、危なかったねぇ。でも、いい詩には<line-break>危険がつきものだものね。<line-break>はい、助けてくれたお礼、どうもありがとね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that was close. But a good poem always needs a little danger. Here, this is for rescuing me. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "아아, 위험했네. 그래도 좋은 시에는 위험이 따르는 법이니까. 자, 구해 준 답례야. 정말 고마워.<end>"

## 1570139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イタイイタイ。あなたを信頼して大損したわ。<line-break>しばらく旅ができないじゃないの！　ふんっ。<end>"
EN: "Ow, ow! Trusting you was a costly mistake. Now I won't be able to travel for ages! Hmph.<end>"
KO: "아파, 아파. 당신을 믿었다가 큰 손해 봤어. 한동안 여행도 못 하잖아! 흥.<end>"

## 1570140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、助けに来てくれたのね。<line-break>どうか、近くの町まで連れて行ってください。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you came to rescue me. Please take me to the nearest town.<end>"
KO: "아, 구하러 와 주셨군요. 부디 가까운 마을까지 데려가 주세요.<end>"

## 1570141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうもありがとうございました。　　　　　　　<line-break>このご恩は一生忘れません。<line-break>　<line-break>助けていただいたお礼です。<line-break>契約の料金よりも多いですが、私の気持ちです。<line-break>どうか、お受け取りください。<end>"
EN: "Thank you very much. I shall never forget this kindness. Here is my payment for your help. It is more than the contracted fee, but please accept it as a token of my gratitude.<end>"
KO: "정말 감사합니다. 이 은혜는 평생 잊지 않겠습니다. 구해 주신 답례입니다. 계약 금액보다 많지만 제 마음이니 부디 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おかげさまで命拾いしました。<line-break>どうもありがとうございました。　　<line-break>　<line-break>これは、助けていただいたお礼です。<line-break>お受け取りください。<end>"
EN: "Thanks to you, my life was spared. Thank you very much. Here is my payment for your help. Please accept it.<end>"
KO: "덕분에 목숨을 건졌습니다. 정말 감사합니다. 이것은 구해 주신 보답입니다. 받아 주세요.<end>"

## 1570143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、どうしてこんなに運がないのかしら…。<line-break>もっと強い人に助けてもらいたかったわ。<line-break>お礼金はお支払いできませんわ。失礼します。<end>"
EN: "Oh, why must I be so unlucky...? I wish someone stronger had rescued me. I cannot possibly pay you. Good day.<end>"
KO: "아아, 왜 이렇게 운이 없는 걸까요…. 좀 더 강한 사람에게 도움받고 싶었어요. 사례금은 드릴 수 없겠네요. 실례하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1570144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あぁっ、助けにきてくださったのですね。<line-break>しかもいかにもナイト様という感じ！<line-break>近くの町まで連れてってくださるのね。<end>"
EN: "Oh! You've come to rescue me, and you look every inch a knight! You will take me to the nearest town, won't you?<end>"
KO: "아아, 구하러 와 주셨군요. 게다가 정말 기사님 같은 분이네요! 근처 마을까지 데려다주시는 거죠.<end>"

## 1570145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございましたですわ。<line-break>またそのうちお願いいたしますわ、ウフ。　　　<line-break>これは、助けていただいた料金ですわ。<line-break>それとボーナスですわね。…今晩お暇でしたら、<line-break>宿屋の二階にいらしてくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Thank you ever so much. Perhaps I shall call on you again sometime. Hee-hee. Here is your rescue fee, and a bonus as well. If you're free tonight, do come to the second floor of the inn.<end>"
KO: "고마웠습니다. 다음에도 또 부탁드릴게요, 우후. 이건 도와주신 비용이에요. 그리고 보너스도 있고요. …오늘 밤 시간 되시면 여관 2층으로 오세요.<end>"

## 1570146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとうございましたですわ。<line-break>今度はもう少しお手柔らかに、お願いしますわ。<line-break>これは、助けていただいた料金ですわ。<end>"
EN: "Thank you ever so much. Next time, do try to be a little gentler. Here is your rescue fee.<end>"
KO: "감사했습니다. 다음에는 조금만 더 살살 부탁드릴게요. 이건 도와주신 대가예요.<end>"

## 1570147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんなめにあわせられるとは思いませんでした。<line-break>今度出会ったら、魔法で焼き焦がしますわよ。<end>"
EN: "I never imagined you would put me through such an ordeal. If we meet again, I shall burn you to a cinder with my magic.<end>"
KO: "이런 꼴을 당할 줄은 몰랐네요. 다음에 만나면 마법으로 새까맣게 태워 버리겠어요.<end>"

## 1570152

## 1570153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危ないところを、どうもありがとう。<line-break>はい、助けてくれたお礼よ。<line-break>　<line-break>それと、私からのほんの気持ちよ、受け取って。<line-break>あたしは酒場にいるから、遊びに来てくれると<line-break>うれしいわ。<end>"
EN: "Thanks for saving me back there. Here, this is for rescuing me. And take this little something from me as well. I'm at the tavern, so I'd love it if you came by sometime.<end>"
KO: "위험한 데서 구해 줘서 정말 고마워. 자, 구해 준 답례야. 그리고 이건 내 작은 성의니까 받아. 난 술집에 있으니 놀러 와 주면 기쁠 거야.<end>"

## 1570154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "危ないところを、どうもありがとう。<line-break>あんたが戦う姿、カッコよかったわよ。<line-break>はい、助けてくれたお礼よ、受け取って。<end>"
EN: "Thanks for saving me back there. You looked impressive in battle. Here, this is for rescuing me. Take it.<end>"
KO: "위험한 데서 구해 줘서 고마워. 싸우는 모습 멋있더라. 자, 구해 준 사례야. 받아.<end>"

## 1570155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec157-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なにが救出に来た、よ。素人同然じゃない。<line-break>おかげで、ずいぶんひどいめにあったわよ！<end>"
EN: "Some rescue that was. You're practically an amateur. Thanks to you, I had a dreadful time!<end>"
