## 1580000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec158-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$33><equal>%0アイリーンお姉ちゃんはね、<line-break>とってもつよいけど、とってもやさしいんだ。<line-break>あたし、お姉ちゃんだーい好き。<end><if><value:$23><equal>%2あっ！　<value:$28>お兄ちゃんだぁ。<line-break>おかえりなさぁい。<end><select><value:$20>%1ここにはねえ、アイリーンっていう　　　　<line-break>つよーいお姉ちゃんがいたんだよぉ。<line-break>とってもつよくて、やさしかったんだぁ。<line-break>でもね、今はね、ここには、いないよぉ。<line-break>ロストールで、女はきしになれないって<line-break>たびにでちゃったんだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$33><equal>%0Irene's really strong, but she's really kind, too. I just love her!<end><if><value:$23><equal>%2Oh! It's you, <value:$28>! Welcome home!<end><select><value:$20>%1There used to be a really strong girl here named Irene. She was very strong and kind. But she isn't here anymore. She left on a journey because women aren't allowed to become knights in Rostorl.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$33><equal>%0아이린 언니는 정말 강하지만 정말 상냥해. 나 언니가 정말 좋아.<end><if><value:$23><equal>%2앗! <value:$28> 오빠다아. 어서 와아.<end><select><value:$20>%1여기에는 아이린이라는 아주 강한 언니가 있었어. 정말 강하고 상냥했어. 하지만 지금은 여기 없어. 로스톨에서는 여자가 기사가 될 수 없다면서 여행을 떠났거든.<end>"

## 1580001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec158-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$33><equal>%0<if><value:$23><not-equal>2ここの道場のオッシさんには<line-break>怪物退治やら、橋の修理やら、悪人退治やら、　<line-break>本当にお世話になってます。<line-break>騎士団は貴族のことだけで手一杯で<line-break>平民のことなんてほったらかしです。<line-break>オッシさんがいてくれないとどうなることやら。<end><if><value:$33><equal>%0あら、<value:$28>くんじゃない。<line-break>修行のほうは進んでる？<line-break>オッシさんをめざしてがんばんなさい。<end><if><value:$33><equal>%1<if><value:$23><not-equal>2ここの道場のオッシさんには<line-break>怪物退治やら、橋の修理やら、悪人退治やら、　<line-break>本当にお世話になってます。<line-break>騎士団は貴族のことだけで手一杯で<line-break>平民のことなんてほったらかしです。<line-break>オッシさんがいてくれないとどうなることやら。<end><if><value:$33><equal>%1あら、<value:$28>くん、<line-break>おかえりなさい。<line-break>元気そうでよかったわ。<end><if><value:$33><equal>%2<if><value:$23><not-equal>2スラムでは、たちの悪い貴族がモンスターを　<line-break>放し飼いにして人々を襲わせたりと、<line-break>やりたい放題をしているそうですよ。<line-break>もし、ここの道場のオッシさんがいなければ、<line-break>悪い貴族はここにも来ていたかもしれません。<line-break>うちの子が無事なのもオッシさんのおかげですよ。<end><if><value:$33><equal>%2あら、<value:$28>くん、<line-break>おかえりなさい。<line-break>聖杯探しは順調に進んでる？<end><if><value:$33><equal>%3<if><value:$23><not-equal>2アイリーンという名のほんとに活発な女の子、<line-break>年齢はあなたと同じくらいなんですけど、<line-break>どこかで会ったことはありませんか？<line-break>女の子ながらオッシ道場でも１、２を争う<line-break>腕の持ち主で、騎士になるといって旅に<line-break>出たきり何の連絡もないんです。<end><if><value:$33><equal>%3あら、<value:$28>くん、<line-break>おかえりなさい。<line-break>アイリーンちゃんは元気にしてる？<end><if><value:$33><equal>%4<if><value:$23><not-equal>2オッシさんは突然フラッとやってきて<line-break>あの家に道場なんか開くから、最初は<line-break>周りから煙たがられていたんですよ。<line-break>でも、あの人柄だからすぐにとけ込ん<line-break>じゃって。今ではここにいなくては<line-break>ならない人になっていますわ。<end><if><value:$33><equal>%4あら、<value:$28>くん、<line-break>すこしたくましくなったんじゃない？<line-break>オッシさんも喜ぶんじゃないかしら。<end><if><value:$33><equal>%5<if><value:$23><not-equal>2リベルダムはカルラ将軍に破壊されたとか…。<line-break>ロストールもそんなことになってしまったら、<line-break>私たち一家はどうすればいいのでしょうか？<end><if><value:$33><equal>%5リベルダムはカルラ将軍に破壊されたそうね。<line-break>ロストールもそんなことになってしまったら、<line-break>私たち一家はどうすればいいの？<end><if><value:$33><equal>%6<if><value:$23><not-equal>2ディンガルのカルラ将軍の部下に<line-break>アイリーンという女騎士がいるそうなのです。<line-break>もしかしてアイリーンちゃんなのでしょうか…。<end><if><value:$33><equal>%6ディンガルのカルラ将軍の部下に<line-break>アイリーンという女騎士がいるらしいのよ。<line-break><value:$28>くん、何か知ってないの？<end><if><value:$33><equal>%7あの、やさしかったアイリーンちゃんが、<line-break>どうしてカルラなんかの部下に…。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$33><equal>%0<if><value:$23><not-equal>2We owe Ossi so much. He runs the training hall here, and he does everything from hunting monsters and repairing bridges to dealing with criminals. The knights are so busy looking after the nobility that they leave the commoners to fend for themselves. I don't know what we'd do without Ossi.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%0Why, if it isn't <value:$28>. How is your training going? Keep at it and aim to follow in Ossi's footsteps.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%1<if><value:$23><not-equal>2We owe Ossi so much. He runs the training hall here, and he does everything from hunting monsters and repairing bridges to dealing with criminals. The knights are so busy looking after the nobility that they leave the commoners to fend for themselves. I don't know what we'd do without Ossi.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%1Oh, <value:$28>! Welcome home. I'm glad to see you looking well.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%2<if><value:$23><not-equal>2I hear some truly vicious nobles in the Slums are letting monsters run loose and making them attack people. They do whatever they please. If Ossi weren't here, those wicked nobles might have come here as well. It's thanks to him that my child is safe.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%2Oh, <value:$28>! Welcome home. Is the search for the Grail going well?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%3<if><value:$23><not-equal>2Have you met a very lively girl named Irene anywhere? She's about your age. Even though she's a girl, she's one of the two best fighters at Ossi's training hall. She set out saying she would become a knight, and we haven't heard a word from her since.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%3Oh, <value:$28>! Welcome home. Is Irene doing well?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%4<if><value:$23><not-equal>2Ossi wandered in out of nowhere and opened a training hall in that house, so the neighbors didn't take kindly to him at first. But being the sort of man he is, he soon fit right in. Now we couldn't do without him.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%4Why, <value:$28>, haven't you grown a little sturdier? I expect Ossi will be pleased.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%5<if><value:$23><not-equal>2They say General Karula destroyed Liberdam. If the same thing happened to Rostorl, what would become of our family?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%5I hear General Karula destroyed Liberdam. If the same thing happened to Rostorl, what would become of our family?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%6<if><value:$23><not-equal>2I hear there is a woman knight named Irene serving under General Karula of Dyneskal. Could it possibly be our Irene?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%6Apparently, a woman knight named Irene serves under General Karula of Dyneskal. <value:$28>, don't you know anything about it?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%7How could sweet, kind Irene have ended up serving someone like Karula...?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$33><equal>%0<if><value:$23><not-equal>2이 도장의 오시 씨에게는 괴물 퇴치, 다리 수리, 악인 퇴치 등 정말 신세를 많이 졌어요. 기사단은 귀족 일만으로 벅차 평민은 내버려 두죠. 오시 씨가 없으면 어쩔 뻔했는지.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%0어머, <value:$28> 군이네. 수련은 잘돼 가? 오시 씨를 목표로 힘내.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%1<if><value:$23><not-equal>2이 도장의 오시 씨에게는 괴물 퇴치, 다리 수리, 악인 퇴치 등 정말 신세를 많이 졌어요. 기사단은 귀족 일만으로 벅차 평민은 내버려 두죠. 오시 씨가 없으면 어쩔 뻔했는지.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%1어머, <value:$28> 군, 어서 와. 건강해 보여 다행이야.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%2<if><value:$23><not-equal>2슬럼에서는 못된 귀족이 몬스터를 풀어 사람들을 공격하게 하는 등 제멋대로라더군요. 오시 씨가 없었다면 나쁜 귀족들이 이곳에도 왔을지 몰라요. 우리 아이가 무사한 것도 오시 씨 덕분이죠.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%2어머, <value:$28> 군, 어서 와. 성배 찾기는 순조로워?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%3<if><value:$23><not-equal>2아이린이라는 아주 활발한 여자아이, 당신과 비슷한 나이인데 어디서 만난 적 없나요? 여자지만 오시 도장에서도 손꼽히는 실력자고 기사가 되겠다며 여행을 떠난 뒤 연락이 없어요.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%3어머, <value:$28> 군, 어서 와. 아이린은 잘 지내?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%4<if><value:$23><not-equal>2오시 씨는 갑자기 나타나 저 집에 도장을 열어서 처음엔 주변에서 꺼렸어요. 하지만 인품이 좋아 금세 어울렸고 이제는 이곳에 없어서는 안 될 사람이죠.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%4어머, <value:$28> 군, 좀 듬직해진 것 같은데? 오시 씨도 기뻐하겠네.<end><if><value:$33><equal>%5<if><value:$23><not-equal>2리벨덤이 카를라 장군에게 파괴됐다니…. 로스톨도 그렇게 되면 우리 가족은 어떻게 해야 할까요?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%5리벨덤이 카를라 장군에게 파괴됐다더라. 로스톨도 그렇게 되면 우리 가족은 어쩌지?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%6<if><value:$23><not-equal>2딩갈의 카를라 장군 부하 중 아이린이라는 여기사도 있다더군요. 혹시 그 아이린일까요….<end><if><value:$33><equal>%6딩갈의 카를라 장군 부하 중 아이린이라는 여기사도 있다더라. <value:$28> 군, 뭔가 아는 거 없어?<end><if><value:$33><equal>%7그 착하던 아이린이 왜 카를라 같은 사람의 부하가….<end>"

## 1580002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec158-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9オッシ先生は酒癖は悪いが<line-break>面倒見がよく、実は手先も小器用だったりして<line-break>本当に頼りになるお方だ。<line-break>右腕を痛めていらっしゃるようだが、<line-break>それさえなければ、リベルダムの闘技場でも<line-break>チャンプになれる実力の持ち主なのだ。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29力を求め強くなることの先に何があるのか、<line-break>何のために強くなるのか、そんなことを<line-break>自問自答しながら修行に励んでいるのだ。<line-break>これもオッシ先生の教えのたまものだよ。<line-break>先生は強いだけでなくて考え方も<line-break>しっかりしたお方なのだ。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49君は数多くの経験を積み、その強さは<line-break>オッシ先生を超えているかもしれない。<line-break>だが、オッシ先生の崇高さには及ばないな。<end>君には強さという力がある。<line-break>それを何に使うべきなのか、<line-break>よく考えてみたまえ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9Master Ossi may be a troublesome drunk, but he looks after people, and he's surprisingly handy as well. He's a truly dependable man. He seems to have injured his right arm, but if not for that, he would have the skill to become champion of the arena in Liberdam.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29As I train, I keep asking myself what lies beyond the pursuit of power and why I seek strength at all. I owe that to Master Ossi's teaching. He isn't merely strong; he is a man of sound principles.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49You have gained a wealth of experience, and your strength may even surpass Master Ossi's. But you still fall short of his nobility.<end>You have strength. Think carefully about what you ought to use it for.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9오시 선생님은 술버릇은 나쁘지만 남을 잘 챙기고 손재주도 의외로 좋아서 정말 믿음직한 분이다. 오른팔이 좋지 않으신 것 같지만 그것만 아니면 리벨덤 투기장에서도 챔피언이 될 실력자지.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29힘을 추구하고 강해진 끝에 무엇이 있는지, 무엇을 위해 강해지는지 스스로 되물으며 수련하고 있다. 이것도 오시 선생님의 가르침 덕분이지. 선생님은 강하기만 한 게 아니라 생각도 올바른 분이다.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49너는 많은 경험을 쌓아 그 강함은 오시 선생님을 넘어섰을지도 모른다. 하지만 오시 선생님의 숭고함에는 미치지 못하겠지.<end>너에게는 강함이라는 힘이 있다. 그걸 무엇에 써야 하는지 잘 생각해 봐.<end>"

## 1580003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec158-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$23><equal>2<if><value:$33><equal>%0おう、<value:$28>じゃないっすか。<line-break>お互いがんばって修行して<line-break>オッシ先生をめざすっすぅ！<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49オッシ先生は本当にスバラシっ！<line-break>俺、先生を尊敬してるっす。<line-break>どこまでもついていくっす！<line-break>オッシ先生っ、<line-break>アイリーンが抜けた穴は<line-break>俺に任せてくださいっすぅ！<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100<if><value:$23><equal>2おや、<value:$28>じゃないっすか。　　<line-break>最近、少し名前を聞くようになったっす。<line-break>なんだか、かっこいいっすよ。<line-break>でも、俺も負けてないっす。<line-break>オッシ先生のもとで修行に励んで<line-break>いまにロストールで一番の剣士になるっすよ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100あなた最近少し有名になってきた冒険者の　　　<line-break><value:$28>さんじゃないっすか。<line-break>その若さで有名人なんてかっこいいっす。<line-break>でも、俺も負けてないっす。<line-break>オッシ先生のもとで修行に励んで<line-break>いまにロストールで一番の剣士になるっすよ。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130厳しい修行に耐えて、だいぶ強くなったっす。<line-break>でもロストールで一番には、まだまだっす。<line-break>俺も<value:$2B>って呼ばれたいっす！<end>うひょ、<value:$28>さんじゃないっすか！<line-break>大陸で一番の冒険者になっても、こうして<line-break>会いに来てくれるなんて感激っす！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$23><equal>2<if><value:$33><equal>%0Hey, isn't that <value:$28>? Let's both train hard and try to be like Master Ossi!<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49Master Ossi is amazing! I really look up to him. I'd follow him anywhere! Master Ossi, leave it to me to fill the gap Irene left behind!<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100<if><value:$23><equal>2Hey, isn't that <value:$28>? I've been hearing your name around lately. That's pretty impressive. But I'm not about to lose! I'll keep training under Master Ossi, and before long I'll be the finest swordsman in Rostorl!<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100Aren't you <value:$28>, the adventurer who's been making a name lately? Being famous at your age is pretty impressive. But I'm not about to lose! I'll keep training under Master Ossi, and before long I'll be the finest swordsman in Rostorl!<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130I've endured some grueling training, and I've gotten a lot stronger. But I'm still a long way from being the best in Rostorl. I want people to call me <value:$2B>, too!<end>Whoa, <value:$28>! Even after becoming the greatest adventurer on the continent, you still came to see me. I'm thrilled!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$23><equal>2<if><value:$33><equal>%0오, <value:$28> 아니십니까. 서로 열심히 수련해서 오시 선생님을 목표로 합시다아!<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49오시 선생님은 정말 대단하심다! 저, 선생님을 존경함다. 어디까지든 따라가겠슴다! 오시 선생님, 아이린이 빠진 자리는 저한테 맡겨 주십쇼오!<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100<if><value:$23><equal>2어, <value:$28> 아니십니까. 요즘 이름이 조금씩 들리더군요. 뭔가 멋짐다. 하지만 저도 안 질 겁니다. 오시 선생님 밑에서 열심히 수련해서 언젠가 로스톨 제일의 검사로 거듭나겠슴다.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100당신 요즘 조금 유명해진 모험자 <value:$28> 씨 아닙니까. 그 나이에 유명인이라니 멋짐다. 하지만 저도 안 질 겁니다. 오시 선생님 밑에서 열심히 수련해서 언젠가 로스톨 제일의 검사로 거듭나겠슴다.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130혹독한 수련을 견뎌서 꽤 강해졌슴다. 그래도 로스톨 제일이 되려면 아직 멀었죠. 저도 <value:$2B>라고 불리고 싶슴다!<end>우효, <value:$28> 씨 아닙니까! 대륙 최고의 모험자가 되고도 이렇게 만나러 와 주시다니 감격임다!<end>"

## 1580004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec158-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49へっ、貴族の奴らは、俺ら貧乏人を　　　　<line-break>人間だとは思っちゃあいないのさ。<line-break>特に七竜家の１つリューガ家のタルテュバはな。<line-break>タルテュバの奴、何かむかつくことがあると<line-break>ごろつきを連れてきちゃあ、気晴らしに<line-break>スラムのみんなに乱暴を働きやがる。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100貴族の奴ら、運よく貴族の子として生まれた　<line-break>だけの能なし野郎のくせにいばりやがって。<line-break>貴族なんてのは国が滅べばただの人なんだよ。<line-break>七竜士が世界を救った英雄なんてウソっぱちだ。<line-break>くだらん貴族制度を導入して、自分の子孫を<line-break>ぶくぶくと太らしているだけじゃないか。<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130あんた、<value:$28>だろ？<line-break>ただの平民から、今じゃ大陸で３本の指に入る<line-break>冒険者なんだってな。尊敬するぜ。<end>あんたを見てると、貴族制度を愚痴っている<line-break>自分が恥ずかしくなってよ。<line-break>俺も生きる努力ってのをしてみるぜ。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49Hah. The nobles don't even think of poor folk like us as human, especially Tartuba of House Ryuga, one of the Seven Dragon Houses. Whenever something puts him in a foul mood, he brings a gang of thugs down here and takes it out on everyone in the Slums.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100Those nobles are useless bastards who just happened to be born into noble families, yet they strut around as if they're better than us. If their country fell, they'd be ordinary people like anyone else. That tale about the Seven Dragon Knights being heroes who saved the world is a pack of lies. They brought in this rotten aristocracy just to fatten up their own descendants.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130You're <value:$28>, right? They say you started as an ordinary commoner, and now you're one of the three greatest adventurers on the continent. You have my respect.<end>Looking at you makes me ashamed of sitting around complaining about the aristocracy. I'm going to make an effort to do something with my life, too.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><less-equal>%49흥, 귀족 놈들은 우리 가난뱅이를 사람으로도 안 봐. 특히 칠룡가 중 하나인 류가 가문의 타르튜바는 더하지. 타르튜바는 기분 나쁜 일만 생기면 건달들을 데려와 화풀이로 슬럼 사람들을 두들겨 패.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%100귀족 놈들, 운 좋게 귀족 자식으로 태어난 무능한 놈들이 잘난 척하기는. 귀족도 나라가 망하면 그냥 사람이야. 칠룡사가 세계를 구한 영웅이라는 것도 거짓말이야. 쓸데없는 귀족 제도를 만들어 자기 후손만 배불리고 있는 것뿐이잖아.<end><if><value:$2A><less-equal>%130당신, <value:$28>지? 평민 출신으로 이제 대륙에서 세 손가락 안에 드는 모험자가 됐다면서. 존경해.<end>당신을 보고 있으면 귀족 제도를 불평만 하던 내가 부끄러워져. 나도 살아가기 위해 노력해 볼게.<end>"

## 1580005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec158-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$33>そりゃ、ロストールは身分がすべてさ。<line-break>だからってウジウジしてんのはゴメンだよ。<line-break>それよりゃ、笑って、働いて、遊ぶ方がいい。　<line-break>それでも気に入らなけりゃ、<line-break>でっかいことしてひっくり返すのさ。<line-break>…って、全部ゼネさんの受け売りなんだけどさ。<end>そりゃ、ロストールは身分がすべてさ。<line-break>だからってウジウジしてんのはゴメンだよ。<line-break>それよりゃ、笑って、働いて、遊ぶ方がいい。　<line-break>それでも気に入らなけりゃ、<line-break>でっかいことしてひっくり返すのさ。<line-break>…って、全部ゼネさんの受け売りなんだけどさ。<end>あんた冒険者ならゼネテスって知っているだろ。<line-break>一見だらしない酒好きの男だけど、あたしらに<line-break>とっちゃ頼もしい味方だよ。いい男だしねぇ。<line-break>世間じゃ勇者ネメアが皇帝になったなんて<line-break>騒いでいるけど勇者がなんだっていうんだい。<line-break>ゼネさんのほうがいい男に決まってるよ。<end>確かに生活は貧しいけどつらくはないさね。　　<line-break>人生は泣いて笑って楽しむものだって気づいた<line-break>からねぇ。子供たちも立派に育ってくれたし。<line-break>上の２人はディンガルとリベルダムで船員として<line-break>働いているよ。末っ子はロストールで一番の<line-break>剣士になるってオッシ道場で修行中だよ。<end>このロストールを救ってくれたのが、<line-break>我らのヒーロー、ゼネさんだよ。<line-break>それにくらべてタルテュバは男のクズだね。<end>リベルダムで働いている息子かい？<line-break>もちろん無事にリベルダムを脱出したさね。<line-break>海と船がある限り仕事はなくならないとさ。<end>カルラが攻めてきたって、あたしゃ恐くないね。<line-break>もちろん、ゼネさんが必ず守ってくれるって<line-break>信じているからさ。<end>エルファス様の話を聞いていると、<line-break>あの人が救世主って呼ばれている理由が<line-break>わかるような気がするよ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$33>Sure, status is everything in Rostorl. But that doesn't mean I'm going to mope about it. I'd rather laugh, work, and enjoy myself. And if I still can't stand it, I'll do something big and turn the whole thing on its head. Though I'm only repeating what Xene always says.<end>Sure, status is everything in Rostorl. But that doesn't mean I'm going to mope about it. I'd rather laugh, work, and enjoy myself. And if I still can't stand it, I'll do something big and turn the whole thing on its head. Though I'm only repeating what Xene always says.<end>You're an adventurer, so you know Xenetes, don't you? At first glance he looks sloppy and drinks too much, but to us he's a dependable ally. And a fine-looking man, too. Everyone's making a fuss about the Hero Nemea becoming emperor, but who cares if he's a hero? Xene's the better man, no question.<end>We may be poor, but life isn't miserable. I've learned that life is about crying, laughing, and enjoying yourself. My children have all grown up well, too. The two eldest work as sailors in Dyneskal and Liberdam. The youngest is training at Ossi's hall, determined to become the finest swordsman in Rostorl.<end>It was our hero Xene who saved Rostorl. Tartuba, on the other hand, is scum.<end>My son who works in Liberdam? Of course he escaped safely. He says that as long as there are seas and ships, there'll always be work.<end>I'm not afraid of Karula attacking. Of course, that's because I trust Xene to protect us.<end>When I listen to Elfas speak, I think I understand why people call him the Savior.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$33>그래, 로스톨은 신분이 전부야. 그렇다고 우물쭈물하는 건 사양이지. 그보다 웃고, 일하고, 노는 편이 좋아. 그래도 마음에 안 들면 큰일을 벌여 뒤집어엎는 거야. …라고 전부 제네 씨에게 들은 말이지만.<end>그래, 로스톨은 신분이 전부야. 그렇다고 우물쭈물하는 건 사양이지. 그보다 웃고, 일하고, 노는 편이 좋아. 그래도 마음에 안 들면 큰일을 벌여 뒤집어엎는 거야. …라고 전부 제네 씨에게 들은 말이지만.<end>너 모험자라면 제네테스 알지? 언뜻 보면 칠칠치 못한 술꾼이지만 우리한테는 든든한 편이야. 좋은 남자이기도 하고. 세상은 용사 네메아가 황제가 됐다고 떠들지만 용사가 뭐 어쨌다고. 제네 씨가 더 좋은 남자인 게 당연하지.<end>확실히 가난하지만 괴롭진 않아. 인생은 울고 웃으며 즐기는 거란 걸 깨달았거든. 아이들도 훌륭하게 자랐고. 위의 둘은 딩갈과 리벨덤에서 선원으로 일하고 있어. 막내는 로스톨 제일의 검사가 되겠다며 오시 도장에서 수련 중이야.<end>이 로스톨을 구해 준 게 우리의 영웅 제네 씨야. 그에 비하면 타르튜바는 남자 쓰레기지.<end>리벨덤에서 일하는 아들이냐고? 물론 무사히 리벨덤을 탈출했지. 바다와 배가 있는 한 일은 사라지지 않는대.<end>카를라가 쳐들어와도 난 무섭지 않아. 물론 제네 씨가 반드시 지켜 줄 거라고 믿으니까.<end>엘파스 님 이야기를 듣고 있으면 그 사람이 구세주라 불리는 이유를 알 것 같아.<end>"

## 1580006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec158-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$20>%2エンシャントのギルドにいる謎の冒険者に<line-break>のせられて、ついつい何度も挑戦してたら<line-break>所持金がなくなっちまった…トホホ。<end>楽して稼ごうなんて考えちゃだめだね。<line-break>エンシャントのギルドにある魔法の箱…<line-break>あれは悪魔の箱だよ。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$20>%2The Mysterious Adventurer at the Guild in Ancient egged me on, and before I knew it, I'd tried so many times that I'd lost every coin I had. What a disaster.<end>You should never expect to make easy money. That magic box at the Guild in Ancient... it's a devil's box.<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$20>%2엔샨트 길드의 수수께끼 모험가 말에 넘어가 몇 번이나 도전하다가 가진 돈이 다 떨어졌어… 흑흑.<end>편하게 돈 벌 생각은 하면 안 되겠어. 엔샨트 길드에 있는 마법의 상자… 그건 악마의 상자야.<end>"

## 1580007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec158-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<select><value:$33>♪おれっち、ボルダン～。<line-break>♪力がすべて～<line-break>♪戦いが大好きだ～！<line-break>♪でーもー、おれっちは～<line-break>♪うまい料理と～<line-break>♪酒も～大好きだ～！<end>♪ボルダンはな～強くなけりゃ～、<line-break>♪一人前と認められないんだ～よ～。<line-break>♪だから戦士になりたがる～のさ～…、ひっく。<end>♪燃えさ～かる命の炎～<line-break>♪ボルダ～ンの炎は輝き激し～い<line-break>♪命みじか～し戦え～激しく～。<end>♪ボ、ボ、ボ、ボ、ボルダ～ン<line-break>♪格闘大好きボルダ～ン<line-break>♪ドワーフ戦士と戦ってみ～た～い～。<end>♪アンギルダンは赤鎧～<line-break>♪ディンガル軍は黒鎧～<line-break>♪おれっちボルダン肉鎧～、つよいぞー。<end>♪破滅の槍はネメアがもって～<line-break>♪王者レーグは剛刃に閃刃～<line-break>♪将軍カルラはカマつかい～。<end>♪おれっちボ～ルダ～ン<line-break>♪強い女が大！　大！　大！　大好きだ～<line-break>♪愛しのカルラと戦いた～い。<end>♪ゼネテス、ネメアが強くとも～<line-break>♪王者レーグにゃかなうまい～<line-break>♪おいらもレーグにゃかなわない～。<end>"
EN: "<select><value:$33>♪I am a Boldan! ♪Strength is everything! ♪Oh, how I love to fight! ♪But I also love ♪good food ♪and plenty of drink!<end>♪A Boldan is no real man unless he is strong! ♪That is why we all want to be warriors... Hic!<end>♪The flames of life burn bright! ♪Boldan's flame blazes fierce and brilliant! ♪Life is short, so fight with all your might!<end>♪B-B-B-B-Boldan! ♪We Boldans love hand-to-hand fighting! ♪I would love to fight a Dwarf warrior!<end>♪Angeerdan wears red armor! ♪The Dyneskal Army wears black! ♪But I am a Boldan in armor of flesh! I am strong!<end>♪Nemea bears the Spear of Ruin! ♪Champion Reig has the Mighty Blade and Flash Blade! ♪General Karula fights with a sickle!<end>♪I am a Boldan! ♪I love, love, love, love strong women! ♪I want to fight my darling Karula!<end>♪Xenetes and Nemea may be strong, ♪but they can't match Champion Reig! ♪And I can't match Reig either!<end>"
KO: "<select><value:$33>♪나는 볼단~. ♪힘이 전부~ ♪싸움이 정말 좋다~! ♪하지만 나는~ ♪맛있는 요리와~ ♪술도~ 정말 좋다~!<end>♪볼단은 말이지~ 강하지 않으면~ ♪한 사람 몫으로 인정받지 못해~. ♪그래서 전사가 되려고 하지~… 딸꾹.<end>♪불타오르는 생명의 불꽃~ ♪볼단의 불꽃은 밝고 거세게 빛나네~ ♪인생은 짧아~ 싸워라~ 격렬하게~.<end>♪보, 보, 보, 보, 볼단~ ♪격투 정말 좋아 볼단~ ♪드워프 전사와 싸워 보고 싶다아~.<end>♪앙길단은 붉은 갑옷~ ♪딩갈군은 검은 갑옷~ ♪나는 볼단 살가죽 갑옷~, 강하다~.<end>♪파멸의 창은 네메아가 들고~ ♪왕자 레그는 강인한 칼날에 번뜩이는 칼날~ ♪장군 카를라는 낫을 쓰네~.<end>♪나는 보~올다~안 ♪강한 여자가 정말! 정말! 정말! 정말 좋다~ ♪사랑하는 카를라와 싸우고 싶다~.<end>♪제네테스, 네메아가 강해도~ ♪왕자 레그에겐 못 당하지~ ♪나도 레그에겐 못 당해~.<end>"

## 1580008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec158-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9うい～、最近の若いもんは自分の身の程も　　　<line-break>知らずに危険なことばかりしおる。<line-break>目立てばええっちゅうもんじゃないじゃろが。<line-break>自分のレベルに合った場所で地道に宝を<line-break>探すのが一番なんじゃ、ひっく…。<line-break>冒険初心者には飢えた者の迷宮がええんじゃい。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29なんじゃいなんじゃい、ちょっと強くなった<line-break>くらいで城塞都市跡を探検するんじゃないわい。<line-break>最近の冒険者は命を粗末にしすぎるぞ、ひっく。<line-break>探検するならぁ、ゼグナ鉱山に夢幻の湖…<line-break>まだまだあるじゃろが、うい～。<line-break>あんたも命知らずの冒険者なんかい…。<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49ライネル、グレト、エシック…みんなすまねえ…<line-break>俺が…俺がしぶきの群島に連れていかなきゃ<line-break>あんな化け物と戦わなきゃ…ぐずっ…。<end>あんた、強いじゃろ！<line-break>お願いじゃ、しぶきの群島の巨大な化け物を…<line-break>仲間の仇をとってくれ…お願いじゃ…<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9Ugh... Young people these days don't know their own limits. They're always doing something dangerous. Getting noticed isn't everything, you know. The best way is to search steadily for treasure somewhere suited to your level. Hic... Beginners ought to start with the Labyrinth of the Hungry.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29What's this? Just because you've gotten a little stronger doesn't mean you should go exploring the Fortress City Ruins! Adventurers these days are far too careless with their lives. If you want somewhere to explore, there's Zegna Mine, the Lake of Illusions... plenty of other places. Ugh... Are you another one of those foolhardy adventurers?<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49Linell, Greto, Ethic... forgive me, all of you. If I hadn't taken you to the Spray Archipelago... you never would have had to fight that monster... Sniff...<end>You're strong, aren't you? Please... kill the giant monster in the Spray Archipelago and avenge my companions. I'm begging you.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2C><less-equal>%9으이~, 요즘 젊은 것들은 제 분수도 모르고 위험한 짓만 해. 눈에 띄기만 하면 되는 게 아니라고. 자기 레벨에 맞는 곳에서 착실히 보물을 찾는 게 최고야, 딸꾹… 초보 모험자라면 굶주린 자의 미궁이 좋지.<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%29뭐야 뭐야, 조금 강해졌다고 성채도시 터를 탐험하는 게 아니야. 요즘 모험자들은 목숨을 너무 가볍게 여긴다니까, 딸꾹. 탐험할 거면 제그나 광산이나 몽환의 호수… 아직 많잖아, 으이~. 자네도 목숨 아까운 줄 모르는 모험자인가…<end><if><value:$2C><less-equal>%49라이넬, 그레트, 에식… 다들 미안해… 내가… 내가 물보라 군도로 데려가지 않았더라면 저런 괴물과 싸우지 않았을 텐데… 훌쩍…<end>자네, 강하지! 부탁이야, 물보라 군도의 거대한 괴물을… 동료들의 원수를 갚아 줘… 부탁이야…<end>"

## 1580011

## 1590001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Oh! Perfect timing. Hey, <value:$28>, do you know about the Adventurers' Guild?<end>"

## 1590002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "町にはたいてい、<line-break>冒険者ギルドっていうのがあってね、　<line-break>　<line-break>登録した冒険者に、<line-break>お仕事を探してくれたり、<line-break>その他にも色々助けてくれるんだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "Most towns have an Adventurers' Guild. They find work for registered adventurers and help them out with all sorts of other things, too.<end>"
KO: "마을에는 보통 모험가 길드라는 게 있어서 등록한 모험가에게 일을 찾아주거나 그 밖에도 여러 도움을 줘.<end>"

## 1590003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタたちも今日からは、　　　<line-break>自分たちの力で生きていかなきゃ！<line-break>ね、登録しにいこう？<end>"
EN: "Starting today, we'll have to make our own way in the world! Come on, let's go register.<end>"
KO: "릴비 일행도 오늘부터는 자기 힘으로 살아가야 해! 자, 등록하러 가자?<end>"

## 1590004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、ルルアンタ<line-break>冒険者ギルドでまってるね。<end>"
EN: "All right, then. Lulluantha will be waiting at the Adventurers' Guild.<end>"
KO: "그럼 루루안타, 모험자 길드에서 기다릴게.<end>"

## 1590005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、<value:$28>、<line-break>やっと来たぁー。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>冒険者登録するよぉ。<line-break>はやくカウンターに！<line-break>ほら、はやく、はやく！<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>! You finally made it! We're registering as adventurers. Hurry, get to the counter! Come on, hurry, hurry!<end>"
KO: "아~ <value:$28>, 이제야 왔네~. 모험자 등록할 거야. 빨리 카운터로! 어서, 어서!<end>"

## 1590006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すみませ～ん。<line-break>冒険者の登録をしたいんですけどぉ。<end>"
EN: "Excuse me! We'd like to register as adventurers.<end>"
KO: "실례합니다~. 모험자 등록을 하고 싶은데요.<end>"

## 1590007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほう、こりゃまた、かわいらしい冒険者だな。<line-break>おチビちゃんたちで本当にだいじょうぶかい？<end>"
EN: "Well, aren't you two the cutest little adventurers. Are you sure a couple of little things like you can handle it?<end>"
KO: "오, 또 귀여운 모험자들이군. 꼬맹이들끼리 정말 괜찮겠어?<end>"

## 1590008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おチビちゃんじゃないよぉ。<line-break>あたしはルルアンタ。<line-break>　<line-break>外見だけで判断するなんて、<line-break>おじさんもまだまだね。<end>"
EN: "I'm not a little thing! I'm Lulluantha. You've still got a lot to learn if you judge people by their looks, mister.<end>"
KO: "꼬맹이 아니거든~. 나는 루루안타야. 겉모습만 보고 판단하다니, 아저씨도 아직 멀었네.<end>"

## 1590009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ははははは。これは失礼。<line-break>はい、それじゃ、<line-break>ここに名前を書いてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Ha ha ha ha! My apologies. All right, write your names here.<end>"
KO: "하하하하. 이거 실례했군. 자, 그럼 여기에 이름을 써 줘.<end>"

## 1590010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "犯罪登録なし。幻術計振れておらず。<line-break>…はい、あなたたちは今日から<line-break>冒険者として登録されました。<line-break>がんばってくれよ。<line-break>とにかく、仕事をこなすのが<line-break>冒険者の基本だからな。<end>"
EN: "No criminal records. No response from the illusion gauge. Very well. As of today, you're registered adventurers. Good luck. Remember, completing jobs is the foundation of an adventurer's trade.<end>"
KO: "범죄 등록 없음. 환술계 흔들림 없음. …좋아, 너희는 오늘부터 모험자로 등록됐다. 힘내라. 어쨌든 일을 해내는 게 모험자의 기본이니까.<end>"

## 1590011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冒険者になれて、おめでとう。<line-break>お仕事がんばらなきゃ！　ね！<end>"
EN: "Congratulations! We're adventurers now. We'll have to work hard, won't we?<end>"
KO: "모험자가 된 걸 축하해. 일 열심히 해야지! 응!<end>"

## 1590012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、ここからは自由行動！<line-break>町を出るときには、おちあうからね！<end>"
EN: "Okay, we're free to do whatever we want now! We'll meet up when it's time to leave town.<end>"
KO: "자, 여기서부터는 자유행동! 마을을 떠날 때 다시 모이는 거야!<end>"

## 1590013
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ～て、ルルアンタたちも<line-break>冒険者に仲間入りできたけどぉ、<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>は、なんか<line-break>急いでやらなきゃいけない<line-break>仕事とかあるの？<end>"
EN: "Well, now that we're adventurers too, is there any urgent work you have to take care of, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "자, 루루안타들도 모험자의 동료가 됐는데, <value:$28>은 서둘러 해야 할 일 같은 게 있어?<end>"

## 1590014
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28>って<line-break>なんか急ぎの用事とかある？　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あ、ギルドに行って<line-break>冒険者の登録はしないといけないけど<line-break>そのほかに、ある？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>, do you have anything urgent to do? I mean, we still have to go register at the Guild, but is there anything else?<end>"
KO: "있잖아, <value:$28>은 급한 일 같은 거 있어? 아, 길드에 가서 모험자 등록은 해야 하지만 그 밖에 또 있어?<end>"

## 1590015
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあさぁ、<line-break>賢者の森に行ってみようよ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>さっき、ギルドの人に聞いたんだけど、<line-break>エンシャントの森の奥には、<line-break>賢者の森っていうのがあるんだって。<end>"
EN: "Then let's go visit Sage's Forest! Someone at the Guild told me there's a place called Sage's Forest deep in the woods of Ancient.<end>"
KO: "그럼 말이야, 현자의 숲에 가 보자. 아까 길드 사람한테 들었는데 엔샨트 숲 안쪽에 현자의 숲이라는 곳이 있대.<end>"

## 1590016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森には、森の賢者さんが住んでる<line-break>猫屋敷っていうのがあるんだけど、<line-break>誰でも入れるわけじゃないんだって。<line-break>ね。おもしろそうでしょ？<line-break>行ってみようよぉ。<end>"
EN: "There's a place in Sage's Forest called Cat Manor, where the Sage of the Forest lives, but apparently not just anyone can get inside. Sounds interesting, doesn't it? Let's go!<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲에는 숲의 현자님이 사는 고양이 저택이란 곳이 있는데 아무나 들어갈 수 있는 건 아니래. 어때, 재미있을 것 같지? 가 보자아.<end>"

## 1590017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わぁ、うれしいな　　　　<line-break><value:$28>！<line-break>　<line-break>それじゃあ、明日は、<line-break>賢者の森に出発だね！<end>"
EN: "Oh, I'm so happy, <value:$28>! Then tomorrow, we're off to Sage's Forest!<end>"
KO: "와, 기쁘다, <value:$28>! 그럼 내일은 현자의 숲으로 출발이네!<end>"

## 1590018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いやぁん！　助けて！<line-break>放してよぉ！　このひとさらい！<end>"
EN: "No! Help me! Let go of me, you kidnapper!<end>"
KO: "싫어어! 살려 줘! 놔줘! 이 납치범!<end>"

## 1590019
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったく手間をとらせやがって！<end>"
EN: "Damn it, you've been nothing but trouble!<end>"
KO: "정말 손이 많이 가게 하는군!<end>"

## 1590020
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誘拐の現場を見られちまった以上、<line-break>生かしておくわけにはいかねぇんでな。<line-break>おい、森の方で始末しちまおう。<end>"
EN: "Now that the kidnapping's been witnessed, we can't let the witness live. Hey, let's take care of this in the forest.<end>"
KO: "유괴 현장을 들킨 이상 살려 둘 수는 없어. 이봐, 숲 쪽에서 처리해 버리자.<end>"

## 1590021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー！　<value:$28>！<line-break>この人たちは…モガモガ。<end>"
EN: "Ah! <value:$28>! These people are... Mmph!<end>"
KO: "아! <value:$28>! 이 사람들은… 우물우물.<end>"

## 1590022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "余計なことは言うんじゃねえ！<end>"
EN: "Keep your mouth shut!<end>"
KO: "쓸데없는 소리는 하지 마!<end>"

## 1590023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見られちまった以上、<line-break>こいつも始末するしかねぇってか！<end>"
EN: "Now that we've been seen, I guess we'll have to finish this one off too!<end>"
KO: "들켜 버린 이상 이 녀석도 처리할 수밖에 없다는 건가!<end>"

## 1590024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あとは頼んだ、俺はこいつを<line-break>賢者の森で処分する！<end>"
EN: "I'll leave the rest to you. I'm taking this one to Sage's Forest to dispose of!<end>"
KO: "나머지는 부탁한다. 난 이 녀석을 현자의 숲에서 처리하겠다!<end>"

## 1590025
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>ー！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>!<end>"

## 1590026
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、お前の相手は俺たちだ。<line-break>恨むんなら、自分の不幸を恨めや！<end>"
EN: "Not so fast. You're dealing with us. If you want to blame something, blame your rotten luck!<end>"
KO: "이봐, 네 상대는 우리다. 원망하려면 네 불운을 원망해라!<end>"

## 1590027
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、覚えてろ！<end>"
EN: "Damn you! You'll pay for this!<end>"
KO: "큭, 두고 보자!<end>"

## 1590028
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とんだヘマだぜ。<line-break>ここまで連れてきて逃げられちまうとはな。<end>"
EN: "What a mess. We brought the brat all this way, only to let the brat escape.<end>"
KO: "큰 실수를 했군. 여기까지 데려와 놓고 도망치게 하다니.<end>"

## 1590029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だが、リルビーのガキひとり、こんな森の中で<line-break>迷ったら、モンスターに食われておしまいさ。<end>"
EN: "Still, one little Lilubee lost in a forest like this will end up as monster food.<end>"
KO: "하지만 릴비 꼬맹이 혼자 이런 숲에서 길을 잃으면 몬스터한테 먹혀 끝장이야.<end>"

## 1590030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "念には念をいれとかねえとな。<line-break>俺たちの一団が誘拐事件の黒幕だなんて<line-break>ばらされたら、いっかんの終わりだぜ。<line-break>…誰だ！<end>"
EN: "Can't be too careful. If word gets out that our band is behind this kidnapping, we're finished. Who's there?!<end>"
KO: "확실히 해 둬야지. 우리 일행이 유괴 사건의 배후라는 게 들통나면 끝장이야. …누구냐!<end>"

## 1590031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんだ、貴様！<line-break>こんな所に１人で来るとは怪しい奴だな。<end>"
EN: "Who the hell are you? Coming to a place like this alone... You look suspicious.<end>"
KO: "뭐냐, 네놈! 이런 곳에 혼자 오다니 수상한 녀석이군.<end>"

## 1590032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様、リルビーのガキの連れか？　　　　<line-break>あのガキは、もういねぇよ。<line-break>　<line-break>今ごろ、この森の中を、泣きながら<line-break>さまよい歩いているぜ。それとも、<line-break>もう、モンスターに食われちまったかな？<end>"
EN: "Are you with that Lilubee brat? The brat's gone. Probably wandering through the forest in tears right now. Or maybe a monster's already eaten the brat.<end>"
KO: "네놈, 릴비 꼬맹이 일행이냐? 그 꼬맹이는 이제 없어. 지금쯤 이 숲을 울면서 헤매고 있겠지. 아니면 이미 몬스터한테 먹혔으려나?<end>"

## 1590033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おっと、秘密を知られちまった以上、<line-break>生かして返すわけにはいかねぇな。<end>"
EN: "Well, now that you know our secret, we can't let you leave alive.<end>"
KO: "이런, 비밀을 알아버린 이상 살아서 돌려보낼 수는 없겠군.<end>"

## 1590034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのリルビ－のガキと一緒に<line-break>地獄に送ってやるぜ！<end>"
EN: "I'll send you to hell with that Lilubee brat!<end>"
KO: "저 릴비 꼬맹이와 함께 지옥으로 보내 주마!<end>"

## 1590035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当に知らねぇよ。<line-break>森の奥の方に行っちまったんだ。<end>"
EN: "I really don't know! The brat went deeper into the forest.<end>"
KO: "정말 모른다니까. 숲 안쪽으로 가 버렸어.<end>"

## 1590036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "覚えてやがれ！<end>"
EN: "You'll pay for this!<end>"
KO: "두고 보자!<end>"

## 1590037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、あのガキは<line-break>生きちゃいねぇよ！<end>"

KO: "그 꼬마는 벌써 살아 있지 않아!<end>"

## 1590038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわ～ん。<line-break>こわかったよぉ！<end>"
EN: "Waaah! I was so scared!<end>"
KO: "우와앙. 무서웠어어!<end>"

## 1590039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほら、大丈夫ですから、　　　　　<line-break>もう泣かないで。<line-break>　<line-break>ここは私の家です。さっきの連中も<line-break>ここまでは追って来られませんよ。<end>"
EN: "There, you're safe now, so please don't cry. This is my home. Those people from before can't follow you here.<end>"
KO: "자, 괜찮으니까 이제 울지 마세요. 여긴 제 집이에요. 아까 그 사람들도 여기까진 쫓아오지 못해요.<end>"

## 1590040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おや？　あなたは…<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者…。<end>"
EN: "Oh? You are... the Bearer of the Infinite Soul...<end>"
KO: "어라? 당신은… 무한의 소울을 지닌 자….<end>"

## 1590042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違うよ、<value:$28>！　<end>"
EN: "No, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "아니야, <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1590043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この人が、ルルアンタのこと、<line-break>助けてくれたんだよぉ。<end>"
EN: "This is the person who saved Lulluantha!<end>"
KO: "이 사람이 루루안타를 도와줬어.<end>"

## 1590044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタの命の恩人なんだよ、　<line-break>その人。もう、剣を降ろしてよぉ。<line-break>　<line-break>ごめんなさい。<line-break>この人、ルルアンタのお友達なの。<end>"
EN: "This person saved Lulluantha's life. Put your sword down already! I'm sorry. This is Lulluantha's friend.<end>"
KO: "그 사람은 루루안타의 생명의 은인이야. 이제 검을 내려 줘. 미안해. 이 사람은 루루안타의 친구야.<end>"

## 1590045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいんですよ、<line-break>間違いはよくあることですから。　<line-break>それにしても、このような時分に<line-break>この猫屋敷に、２人もお客さんが<line-break>見えるとはね。<end>"
EN: "It's quite all right. Mistakes happen. Still, what an unusual time for Cat Manor to receive not one guest, but two.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아요, 실수는 흔한 일이니까요. 그나저나 이런 때에 이 고양이 저택에 손님이 두 분이나 오시다니.<end>"

## 1590046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "What? Then you're the Sage of the Forest?<end>"
KO: "어? 그럼 당신이 숲의 현자님이야?<end>"

## 1590047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ、そんな風に私のことを　　<line-break>呼ぶ人もいるようですね。<line-break>　<line-break>それに、世間ではこんな風にも<line-break>言われているようですよ。<line-break><end>"
EN: "Heh heh. It seems some people do call me that. And there is something else people say.<end>"
KO: "후후, 저를 그렇게 부르는 사람도 있는 모양이네요. 게다가 세상에서는 이렇게도 부르는 모양이에요.<end>"

## 1590048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "賢者の森の猫屋敷に<line-break>入ることができるのは<line-break>運命に選ばれた者だけだ、ともね。<end>"
EN: "They say only those chosen by fate can enter Cat Manor in Sage's Forest.<end>"
KO: "현자의 숲 고양이 저택에 들어갈 수 있는 건 운명에 선택받은 자뿐이라는 말도 있었지.<end>"

## 1590049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うんめいに、選ばれた、もの？<end>"
EN: "Chosen... by fate?<end>"
KO: "운명에 선택받은 자?<end>"

## 1590050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ウフフ。お疲れのようですから、<line-break>今日は泊まっていかれてはいかがです？<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. You both seem tired. Why not stay here tonight?<end>"
KO: "후후. 피곤해 보이시는데 오늘은 묵고 가시는 게 어떠세요?<end>"

## 1590051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ホントにいいんですか？<end>"
EN: "Are you sure that's okay?<end>"
KO: "정말 괜찮겠어요?<end>"

## 1590052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "構いませんとも。<line-break>大したおもてなしもできませんがね。<end>"
EN: "Of course. I fear I cannot offer much in the way of hospitality, though.<end>"
KO: "물론입니다. 대단한 대접은 못 해 드리지만요.<end>"

## 1590053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わーい！　それじゃあ、<line-break>よろしくお願いします。<end>"
EN: "Hooray! Thank you for having us!<end>"
KO: "와아! 그럼 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1590054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうぞ。歓迎しますよ。<end>"
EN: "Please, make yourselves at home.<end>"
KO: "어서 오세요. 환영합니다.<end>"

## 1590055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<line-break>そんなことがあったんですか…。<end>"
EN: "I see. So that is what happened...<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 그런 일이 있었습니까….<end>"

## 1590056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>かわいそう…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... That's awful.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 불쌍해….<end>"

## 1590057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の円卓騎士、<line-break>告げるものアスティアと、　　<line-break>　<line-break>開け放つものヴァシュタール。<line-break>それに、無限のソウルを持つもの。<line-break>運命は、時として皮肉ですね…。<end>"
EN: "Astia the Herald and Vashtar the Opener, Knights of the Round Table of the God of Destruction... and the Bearer of the Infinite Soul. Fate can be ironic at times...<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 원탁기사, 알리는 자 아스티아와 열어젖히는 자 바슈타르. 그리고 무한의 소울을 지닌 자. 운명은 때로 참 얄궂군요….<end>"

## 1590058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大丈夫だよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>お母さんはきっと生きてるよぉ。<end>"
EN: "It'll be all right, <value:$28>. I'm sure your mother is still alive.<end>"
KO: "괜찮아, <value:$28>. 엄마는 분명 살아 있을 거야.<end>"

## 1590059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へっ、どうだかな！<end>"
EN: "Hah! I wouldn't count on it!<end>"
KO: "흥, 글쎄다!<end>"

## 1590060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、ブサイクな猫がしゃべった！<end>"
EN: "Whoa! That ugly cat just talked!<end>"
KO: "와, 못생긴 고양이가 말했어!<end>"

## 1590061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺は猫じゃない、ネモだ。　　　　　<end>"
EN: "I'm not a cat. I'm Nemo.<end>"
KO: "난 고양이가 아니야, 네모다.<end>"

## 1590062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アスティアや、ヴァシュタールと同じ<line-break>破壊神の円卓騎士、ネモ！<end>"
EN: "Nemo, a Knight of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction, just like Astia and Vashtar!<end>"
KO: "아스티아와 바슈타르와 같은 파괴신의 원탁기사, 네모!<end>"

## 1590063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、心配はいりません。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>昔、勇者ネメアに倒されてからは<line-break>猫の姿に封印されて、<line-break>今はなんの力も残っていませんから。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. There is no cause for alarm. Ever since the Hero Nemea defeated him long ago, he has been sealed in the form of a cat. He has no power left now.<end>"
KO: "후후, 걱정하지 마세요. 옛날 용자 네메아에게 쓰러진 뒤로는 고양이 모습에 봉인되어 지금은 아무 힘도 남지 않았으니까요.<end>"

## 1590064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、さすがは破壊神の円卓騎士　<line-break>ですよね。猫の姿になってなお<line-break>人語を操るんですから。<end>"
EN: "Still, he is a Knight of the Round Table serving the God of Destruction. Even in the form of a cat, he can speak the human tongue.<end>"
KO: "그래도 역시 파괴신의 원탁기사답네요. 고양이 모습이 되고도 사람 말을 하니까요.<end>"

## 1590065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "にしても、アスティアも<line-break>丸くなっちまったもんだぜ。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>頭も鈍ったみたいだしな。<line-break>同じ円卓騎士でも、ヴァシュタールと<line-break>アスティアでは実力の差があり過ぎる。<end>"
EN: "Still, Astia's gone soft. Seems a little slow in the head now, too. They may both be Knights of the Round Table, but there's a world of difference between Astia and Vashtar.<end>"
KO: "그나저나 아스티아도 많이 유해졌군. 머리도 둔해진 것 같고. 같은 원탁기사라도 바슈타르와 아스티아는 실력 차이가 너무 커.<end>"

## 1590066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どう考えたって、死んだぜ。　　　　　<line-break>アスティアはよ。<end>"
EN: "Any way you look at it, Astia's dead.<end>"
KO: "아무리 생각해도 죽었어. 아스티아는.<end>"

## 1590067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "黙りなさい、このバカネコ。<end>"
EN: "Be quiet, you stupid cat.<end>"
KO: "닥쳐, 이 바보 고양이.<end>"

## 1590068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだよぉ、このバカネコ！<end>"
EN: "Yeah, you stupid cat!<end>"
KO: "그래, 이 바보 고양이!<end>"

## 1590069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ぐ…ガキの分際で。<line-break>おもしれぇ、相手になってや…<end>"
EN: "Why, you little brat... Fine! I'll take you on―<end>"
KO: "큭… 꼬맹이 주제에. 재밌군, 상대해 주마…<end>"

## 1590070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何を言ってるんです、<line-break>猫の分際で。<end>"
EN: "What are you talking about? You're just a cat.<end>"
KO: "무슨 말을 하는 거죠, 고양이 주제에.<end>"

## 1590071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すいませんね、<value:$28>。<line-break>何せ、気の利かないバカネコでね。<end>"
EN: "Sorry, <value:$28>. This stupid cat has no tact at all.<end>"
KO: "미안하군요, <value:$28>. 워낙 눈치 없는 바보 고양이라서요.<end>"

## 1590072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アスティアのことは<line-break>私の口からはなんとも…。　　<line-break>　<line-break>もう遅いですから、<line-break>今日はこれくらいにして、<line-break>続きは明日にしましょう。<end>"
EN: "I'm afraid I can't tell you anything about Astia. It's already late, so let's stop here for tonight and continue tomorrow.<end>"
KO: "아스티아에 대해서는 제 입으로는 뭐라…. 시간이 늦었으니 오늘은 이쯤 하고, 나머지는 내일 이야기하죠.<end>"

## 1590073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の方も、<line-break>あなたにお話がありますから。<end>"
EN: "There is something I wish to discuss with you as well.<end>"
KO: "저도 당신께 드릴 말씀이 있습니다.<end>"

## 1590074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、<line-break>今日は一緒に寝てあげるよぉ。<end>"
EN: "I'll sleep beside you tonight, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 오늘은 같이 자 줄게에.<end>"

## 1590075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですね。<line-break>それでは、おやすみなさい。<end>"
EN: "Very well. Good night, then.<end>"
KO: "그렇군요. 그럼 안녕히 주무세요.<end>"

## 1590076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おやすみなさい。<end>"
EN: "Good night.<end>"
KO: "안녕히 주무세요.<end>"

## 1590077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさん、<line-break>おはようございます！！<end>"
EN: "Good morning, Orphaus!<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨, 좋은 아침입니다!!<end>"

## 1590078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おはよう、　　　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>、ルルアンタ。<line-break>　<line-break>ルルアンタは本当に元気ですね。<end>"
EN: "Good morning, <value:$28>, Lulluantha. You certainly are full of energy, Lulluantha.<end>"
KO: "좋은 아침, <value:$28>, 루루안타. 루루안타는 정말 활기차네요.<end>"

## 1590079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うふふ。<line-break>ありがとうございます！<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. Thank you!<end>"
KO: "후후. 감사합니다!<end>"

## 1590080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "用意してあった朝ごはんは<line-break>食べていただけましたか。<end>"
EN: "Did you have the breakfast I prepared?<end>"
KO: "준비해 둔 아침 식사는 드셨나요?<end>"

## 1590081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とーってもおいしかったです。<end>"
EN: "It was really, really good!<end>"
KO: "정말 맛있었어요.<end>"

## 1590082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オルファウスさんって<line-break>料理上手なんですねぇ。　　　<end>"
EN: "You're a wonderful cook, Orphaus!<end>"
KO: "오르파우스 씨는 요리를 잘하시네요.<end>"

## 1590083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、ありがとうございます。　<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. Thank you.<end>"
KO: "후훗, 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1590084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、昨日の話の続きをしても<line-break>かまいませんか？<end>"
EN: "Now then, may I continue our discussion from yesterday?<end>"
KO: "그럼 어제 이야기의 계속을 해도 괜찮겠습니까?<end>"

## 1590085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さっそくですが、先日、ミイスの村が<line-break>消失したことをご存じですか？<end>"
EN: "To come straight to the matter, are you aware that Miis Village vanished recently?<end>"
KO: "바로 본론으로 들어가죠. 며칠 전 미이스 마을이 소멸한 사실을 알고 계십니까?<end>"

## 1590086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それって、まさか…。<end>"
EN: "You don't mean...<end>"
KO: "그건 설마….<end>"

## 1590087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そう。ことはミイスを焼き払った魔人<line-break>アーギルシャイアに関わります。<end>"
EN: "Yes. This concerns Argyleshire, the demon who burned Miis to the ground.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니다. 미이스를 불태운 마인 아르길샤이어와 관련된 일입니다.<end>"

## 1590088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アーギルシャイア…？<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire...?<end>"
KO: "아르길샤이어…?<end>"

## 1590089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アスティアやヴァシュタールと同じ、<line-break>破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士の１人です。<line-break>　<line-break>妖艶な美女で、残酷で気まぐれです。<line-break>闇の神器をひとつ手に入れるために<line-break>ミイスを焼き払ってしまいました。<end>"
EN: "Like Astia and Vashtar, she is one of the Knights of the Round Table who serve Ulugh, the God of Destruction. She is a bewitching beauty, cruel and capricious. She burned Miis to the ground merely to obtain one of the Artifacts of Darkness.<end>"
KO: "아스티아나 바슈타르와 같은 파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 한 명입니다. 요염한 미녀이며 잔혹하고 변덕스럽습니다. 어둠의 신기 하나를 얻기 위해 미이스를 불태워 버렸습니다.<end>"

## 1590090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "闇の神器？<end>"
EN: "An Artifact of Darkness?<end>"
KO: "어둠의 신기?<end>"

## 1590091
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "強大で凶々しい力を持つ　　　　　<line-break>１２の魔道器のことです。<line-break>　<line-break>かつて、破壊神ウルグの<line-break>復活に使われたと言われています。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "They are twelve magical artifacts endowed with immense and sinister power. It is said they were once used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction.<end>"
KO: "강대하고 불길한 힘을 지닌 12개의 마도기입니다. 과거 파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰였다고 합니다.<end>"

## 1590092
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、彼女は<line-break>その本来の使い方以外のことを　　<line-break>しようとしています。<line-break>彼女は究極の合成怪物を作り<line-break>その力でおもしろ半分に世界を<line-break>破壊しようと考えているのです。<end>"
EN: "But she intends to use one for something other than its original purpose. She means to create the ultimate chimeric monster and use its power to destroy the world for her own amusement.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그녀는 본래의 용도와 다른 일을 하려 합니다. 궁극의 합성 괴물을 만들어 그 힘으로 재미 삼아 세계를 파괴하려고 생각하고 있습니다.<end>"

## 1590093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その合成怪物を作るための知識を　<line-break>闇の神器のひとつ、禁断の聖杯から<line-break>得ようとしているのです。<line-break>あなた方には、アーギルシャイアの<line-break>手に渡る前に、その聖杯を<line-break>取り戻してもらいたいのです。<end>"
EN: "She hopes to obtain the knowledge needed to create that monster from the Forbidden Grail, one of the Artifacts of Darkness. I want you to recover the Grail before it falls into Argyleshire's hands.<end>"
KO: "그 합성괴물을 만들 지식을 어둠의 신기 중 하나인 금단의 성배에서 얻으려는 겁니다. 여러분에게는 아르길샤이어 손에 넘어가기 전에 그 성배를 되찾아 주셨으면 합니다.<end>"

## 1590094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "取り戻す？<line-break>聖杯は誰かに盗まれちゃったのぉ？<end>"
EN: "Recover it? Did someone steal the Grail?<end>"
KO: "되찾는다고? 성배를 누가 훔쳐 간 거야?<end>"

## 1590096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、誰なのぉ？<end>"
EN: "Then who was it?<end>"
KO: "그럼, 누구야아?<end>"

## 1590097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うちにいるネモですよ。<line-break>もともと聖杯は彼のものなのです。　　<line-break>　<line-break>アーギルシャイアの企みを知った彼は<line-break>自分の神器を奪われたくないと考え、<line-break>この際、神器の力でもとの姿に戻ろうと<end>"
EN: "Nemo, who lives here. The Grail belonged to him in the first place. When he learned of Argyleshire's scheme, he decided he would not allow his artifact to be taken. He also saw a chance to use its power to regain his original form, so...<end>"
KO: "우리 집의 네모입니다. 원래 성배는 그의 것이었어요. 아르길샤이어의 계략을 안 그는 자신의 신기를 빼앗기기 싫어서 이참에 신기의 힘으로 원래 모습으로 돌아가려고….<end>"

## 1590098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "３匹のゴブリンたちを使って<line-break>ロストールから神器を盗み出させ、<line-break>自分の所に持ってこさせたのです。　　<end>"
EN: "He had three Goblins steal the artifact from Rostorl and bring it to him.<end>"
KO: "세 마리 고블린을 이용해 로스톨에서 신기를 훔쳐 자기에게 가져오게 한 겁니다.<end>"

## 1590099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ネモは猫のままだったよぉ？<end>"
EN: "But Nemo was still a cat!<end>"
KO: "그런데 네모는 고양이 그대로였어?<end>"

## 1590100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯は持ち主に英知を与えます。<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail grants wisdom to whoever possesses it.<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배는 소유자에게 지혜를 줍니다.<end>"

## 1590101
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "知恵を身につけたゴブリンたちは　　　<line-break>ネモを見限ってしまったのです。<end>"
EN: "Once the Goblins had gained wisdom, they abandoned Nemo.<end>"
KO: "지혜를 얻은 고블린들은 네모를 버리고 말았습니다.<end>"

## 1590102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "かわいそぉ！<end>"
EN: "Poor Nemo!<end>"
KO: "불쌍해애!<end>"

## 1590103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、おかげで、ロストールは<line-break>焼き払われずにすみましたがね。<end>"
EN: "Well, at least it spared Rostorl from being burned to the ground.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 덕분에 로스톨은 불타 없어지지 않았지만요.<end>"

## 1590104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、アーギルシャイアは、<line-break>聖杯を手に入れようとしてるんでしょ？<line-break>　<line-break>聖杯を持ってるゴブリンがいる町ごと<line-break>ミイスみたいに焼き払っちゃうかも<line-break>しれないよぉ。<end>"
EN: "But Argyleshire is still after the Grail, isn't she? She might burn down the whole town where those Goblins are, just like she did to Miis!<end>"
KO: "하지만 아르길샤이어는 성배를 손에 넣으려는 거잖아? 성배를 가진 고블린이 있는 도시째 미이스처럼 태워 버릴지도 몰라.<end>"

## 1590105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>、あなたは<line-break>魔人の恐ろしさを知っているはずです。<line-break>このまま、アーギルシャイアを<line-break>放っておくと、あなたのように<line-break>大事な人を失う人間がたくさん出ます。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you know how terrifying demons can be. If Argyleshire is left unchecked, many more people will lose someone precious to them, just as you did.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 당신은 마인의 무서움을 알고 있을 겁니다. 이대로 아르길샤이어를 내버려 두면 당신처럼 소중한 사람을 잃는 이들이 많이 생길 겁니다.<end>"

## 1590106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔人と戦えとは言いません。<line-break>彼女の思惑を阻止する、つまり、<line-break>聖杯を先に入手してもらいたいのです。<end>"
EN: "I am not asking you to fight the demon. I want you to thwart her plans by obtaining the Grail before she does.<end>"
KO: "마인과 싸우라는 말씀은 아닙니다. 그녀의 계략을 막는 것, 즉 성배를 먼저 손에 넣어 주셨으면 합니다.<end>"

## 1590107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタも協力するよぉ。これ以上、<line-break>ひどいことはさせないんだから！<end>"
EN: "Lulluantha will help too! I won't let her do anything so horrible again!<end>"
KO: "루루안타도 도울 거야아. 더는 심한 짓 못 하게 할 거니까!<end>"

## 1590108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ええ。よろしく頼みます。<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I am counting on you.<end>"
KO: "예. 잘 부탁드립니다.<end>"

## 1590109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "えっと、そのゴブリンの特徴とか<line-break>行き先の心当たりとかないんですかぁ？<end>"
EN: "Um, do you know what those Goblins look like, or where they might have gone?<end>"
KO: "저기, 그 고블린의 특징이나 행선지로 짐작되는 곳은 없나요?<end>"

## 1590110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴブリンは聖杯の力で人語を話し、　　<line-break>ゴブゴブ団を名乗ってます。<line-break>　<line-break>あと、ネモの話だと<line-break>エンシャントにいるという話でしたが<line-break>急がないと、移動してしまうでしょう。<end>"
EN: "The Grail's power lets the Goblins speak the human tongue, and they call themselves the Gob-Gob Gang. Nemo said they were in Ancient, but unless you hurry, they will probably move on.<end>"
KO: "고블린들은 성배의 힘으로 사람 말을 하며 고브고브단이라고 자칭합니다. 또 네모 말로는 엔샨트에 있다고 했지만 서두르지 않으면 이동해 버릴 겁니다.<end>"

## 1590111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、エンシャントに戻ろうよぉ<line-break><value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Then let's go back to Ancient, <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "그럼 엔샨트로 돌아가자, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1590112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その前に説明したいものがあります。<line-break>こちらに来てください。<end>"
EN: "Before you go, there is something I would like to show you. Come this way.<end>"
KO: "그 전에 설명하고 싶은 게 있습니다. 이쪽으로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1590113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わっ、これ何？<line-break>これ何？<end>"
EN: "Whoa, what's this? What is it?<end>"
KO: "왓, 이게 뭐야? 이게 뭐야?<end>"

## 1590114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、これは転送機っていうんです。<line-break>使ってみましょうか。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh. This is called a teleportation device. Shall we try it?<end>"
KO: "후후, 이건 전송기라고 합니다. 한번 써 볼까요?<end>"

## 1590115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何しやがる！<line-break>俺を実験台にするな！<end>"
EN: "What the hell are you doing? Don't use me as a test subject!<end>"
KO: "뭐 하는 거야! 날 실험대로 쓰지 마!<end>"

## 1590116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、すごいすごーい！！<end>"
EN: "Wow! That's amazing!<end>"
KO: "와, 대단해 대단해ー!!<end>"

## 1590117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こんな感じで、<line-break>これは遠くの人を呼び寄せたり…。<line-break>　<line-break>さて、それでは、もう一度。<end>"
EN: "As you can see, it can summon someone from far away. Now then, once more.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이런 식으로 멀리 있는 사람을 불러오기도 하고…. 자, 그럼 다시 한번.<end>"

## 1590118
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こら！<line-break>俺を実験台に…<end>"
EN: "Hey! Stop using me as a―<end>"
KO: "야! 나를 실험대로 삼아서….<end>"

## 1590119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんな感じで、ここにいる人を<line-break>遠くに送り込んだりできます。<end>"
EN: "And like that, it can send someone who's here to a distant place.<end>"
KO: "이런 식으로 여기 있는 사람을 먼 곳으로 보내기도 할 수 있습니다.<end>"

## 1590120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すごいすごーい！<end>"
EN: "Amazing!<end>"
KO: "대단해, 대단해~!<end>"

## 1590121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたが将来、旅で人と出会い、<line-break>仲間を増やしたとき、　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>一緒に旅する仲間を<line-break>変更をしたいと思ったとき<line-break>ここに来てください。<end>"
EN: "In the future, as you meet people on your travels and gain more companions, come here whenever you wish to change which companions travel with you.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 여행 중 사람을 만나 동료가 늘고, 함께 여행하는 동료를 바꾸고 싶어지면 여기로 오세요.<end>"

## 1590122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、今後は、パーティーメンバーの<line-break>了解を取ってから呼び出しますから<line-break>そのあたりのことはご心配なく。<line-break>他に何か、聞きたいことがあれば、<line-break>私にできる範囲でお答えしますよ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and from now on, I will ask your party members for permission before summoning them, so you need not worry about that. If there is anything else you wish to know, I will answer as best I can.<end>"
KO: "아, 앞으로는 파티 멤버의 동의를 받은 뒤에 불러낼 테니 그 부분은 걱정하지 마세요. 그 밖에 궁금한 게 있다면 제가 답할 수 있는 범위에서 알려 드리겠습니다.<end>"

## 1590123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何か質問ですか？<end>"
EN: "Do you have a question?<end>"
KO: "뭔가 질문 있으신가요?<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Ask about Vashtar<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "No questions<end>"
KO: "질문은 없다<end>"

## 1590132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、それは秘密です。　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ま、おいおいわかりますよ。<line-break>とりあえずはギルドで<line-break>仕事をしていってください。<end>"
EN: "Heh heh, that's a secret. You'll understand in time. For now, take on some work at the Guild.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그건 비밀입니다. 뭐, 차차 알게 될 겁니다. 일단 길드에서 일을 해 주세요.<end>"

## 1590133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "他人のために働くことで、<line-break>魂を成長させていけば<line-break>いずれ、わかってきますよ。<end>"
EN: "As you work for the sake of others and nurture your soul, the answer will come to you.<end>"
KO: "다른 사람을 위해 일하며 영혼을 성장시켜 가면 언젠가 알게 될 겁니다.<end>"

## 1590134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "将来、旅の途中で出会った仲間を<line-break>呼び出すための装置です。　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>一緒に旅する仲間を<line-break>変更したくなったら<line-break>この猫屋敷に来てください。<end>"
EN: "This device will let you summon companions you meet on your travels. If you ever want to change who travels with you, come to Cat Manor.<end>"
KO: "앞으로 여행 중 만난 동료를 불러내기 위한 장치입니다. 함께 여행할 동료를 바꾸고 싶어지면 이 고양이 저택으로 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1590135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "一緒に旅をしたい仲間を呼び出したり、<line-break>別行動を取ることを決めた仲間を<line-break>送り出したりできますよ。<end>"
EN: "You can summon companions you wish to travel with, or send off those who have decided to go their separate ways.<end>"
KO: "함께 여행하고 싶은 동료를 불러오거나 따로 행동하기로 한 동료를 돌려보낼 수 있어요.<end>"

## 1590136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの復活に<line-break>用いられた強力な魔道器です。　<line-break>　<line-break>１２種類あるといいます。<line-break>禁断の聖杯は<line-break>その中のひとつですね。<end>"
EN: "It is a powerful magical artifact used to resurrect Ulugh, the God of Destruction. There are said to be twelve such artifacts, and the Forbidden Grail is one of them.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 부활에 쓰인 강력한 마도기입니다. 12종류가 있다고 하지요. 금단의 성배도 그중 하나입니다.<end>"

## 1590137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "禁断の聖杯については<line-break>今、ゴブゴブ団という<line-break>３匹のゴブリンが持っています。<line-break>とりあえずは、<line-break>エンシャントを探してみると<line-break>彼らに会えると思いますよ。<end>"
EN: "The Forbidden Grail is now in the hands of three Goblins called the Gob-Gob Gang. Try searching Ancient for now. I believe you'll find them there.<end>"
KO: "금단의 성배라면 지금 고브고브단이라는 고블린 셋이 가지고 있습니다. 일단 엔샨트를 찾아보면 그들을 만날 수 있을 거예요.<end>"

## 1590138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここにいるブサイクな猫です。　　　　<line-break>口は悪いですけど、無害ですよ。<line-break>　<line-break>もともとは老人星の精霊で<line-break>天空の賢者とも呼ばれた者でしたが<line-break>地上に堕ちて、魔人になりました。<end>"
EN: "That ugly cat over there. He has a foul mouth, but he's harmless. He was once the spirit of the Old Man Star, known as the Sage of the Heavens, but he fell to earth and became a demon.<end>"
KO: "여기 있는 못생긴 고양이입니다. 입은 험하지만 무해해요. 원래는 노인성의 정령으로 천공의 현자라고도 불렸지만 지상으로 떨어져 마인이 되었습니다.<end>"

## 1590139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の強力な魔人、<line-break>１２人の円卓騎士のひとりで<line-break>名前を知るもの、の異名を持ちます。<line-break>そのあだ名の通り<line-break>金を出せば名前を付けてくれますが<line-break>ま、あんまりおすすめはしません。<end>"
EN: "He is one of the twelve Knights of the Round Table, the God of Destruction's mighty demons, and bears the epithet He Who Knows Names. True to that title, he'll give you a name if you pay him, though I wouldn't particularly recommend it.<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 강력한 마인, 열두 원탁기사 중 한 명으로 '이름을 아는 자'라는 이명이 있습니다. 그 별명 그대로 돈을 내면 이름을 지어 주지만, 뭐, 별로 추천하진 않습니다.<end>"

## 1590140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士には他に、ヴァシュタール、<line-break>アーギルシャイアなどがいますね。　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The other Knights of the Round Table include Vashtar and Argyleshire.<end>"
KO: "원탁기사로는 그 밖에 바슈타르, 아르길샤이어 등이 있지요.<end>"

## 1590141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士の１人です。<line-break>妖艶な美女の姿をしています。<line-break>　<line-break>きわめて、気まぐれで残酷です。<line-break>すでに神器を手に入れるため<line-break>ミイスの村を焼き払っています。<end>"
EN: "She is one of Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table and takes the form of a bewitching beauty. She is exceptionally capricious and cruel. She has already burned Miis Village to the ground to obtain a divine artifact.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 한 명입니다. 요염한 미녀의 모습을 하고 있습니다. 극도로 변덕스럽고 잔혹합니다. 이미 신기를 얻기 위해 미이스 마을을 불태웠습니다.<end>"

## 1590142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "究極生物を作ろうとして、<line-break>その製法を神器、禁断の聖杯から　　<line-break>得ようと、聖杯をねらっています。<line-break>円卓騎士には他に、ヴァシュタールや<line-break>うちのネモなどがいますね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "She seeks the Grail, hoping to learn how to create an Ultimate Life-Form from the divine artifact known as the Forbidden Grail. The other Knights of the Round Table include Vashtar and our own Nemo.<end>"
KO: "궁극생물을 만들기 위해 그 제조법을 신기인 금단의 성배에서 얻으려 성배를 노리고 있습니다. 원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 바슈타르나 우리 네모 같은 자들이 있지요.<end>"

## 1590143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士の１人です。<line-break>魔物を使う能力があると聞きます。<line-break>　<line-break>破壊神復活の時を感知し、その時に<line-break>開け放つものヴァシュタールを<line-break>目覚めさせるのが彼女の役割です。<end>"
EN: "She is one of Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table and is said to possess the power to command monsters. Her role is to sense the time of the God of Destruction's resurrection and awaken Vashtar, He Who Opens the Way, when that time comes.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 한 명입니다. 마물을 부리는 능력이 있다고 합니다. 파괴신 부활의 때를 감지하고, 그때 '열어젖히는 자' 바슈타르를 깨우는 것이 그녀의 역할입니다.<end>"

## 1590144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ですから、彼女は告げるものと　　　<line-break>呼ばれています。<line-break>　<line-break>ですが、あなたとの十数年の暮らしが<line-break>彼女を変えてしまったようですね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "That is why she is called She Who Heralds. But it seems the more than ten years she spent with you changed her.<end>"
KO: "그래서 그녀는 '고하는 자'라고 불립니다. 하지만 당신과 십수 년을 함께 살아온 시간이 그녀를 바꿔버린 모양이군요.<end>"

## 1590145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼女にとって、あなたとの短い年月は<line-break>自らの使命を忘れさせるくらい<line-break>大切なものだったのかもしれません。<end>"
EN: "Perhaps the brief years she spent with you meant enough to make her forget her own calling.<end>"
KO: "그녀에게 당신과 함께한 짧은 세월은 자신의 사명조차 잊게 할 만큼 소중했을지도 모릅니다.<end>"

## 1590146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士の１人です。　<line-break>開け放つものと呼ばれています。<line-break>　<line-break>告げるものアスティアにより<line-break>破壊神復活の時を告げられたとき、<line-break>破壊神を目覚めさせるのが彼です。<end>"
EN: "He is one of Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table, known as He Who Opens the Way. When Astia, She Who Heralds, announces that the time has come for the God of Destruction's resurrection, his role is to awaken the god.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사 중 한 명입니다. '열어젖히는 자'라고 불립니다. 알리는 자 아스티아에게 파괴신 부활의 때를 전해 들으면 파괴신을 깨우는 게 그입니다.<end>"

## 1590147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その実力は、円卓騎士の中でも<line-break>トップクラスです。アーギルシャイア、<line-break>ネモらは彼に率いられていました。<end>"
EN: "He is one of the strongest of the Knights of the Round Table. Argyleshire, Nemo, and the others once served under his command.<end>"
KO: "그 실력은 원탁기사 중에서도 최상급입니다. 아르길샤이어와 네모 등은 그의 지휘를 받았죠.<end>"

## 1590148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。<end>"
EN: "Is that so?<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까.<end>"

## 1590149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こんな感じですかね。<line-break>わかっていただけましたか？<end>"
EN: "Well, that about covers it. Does that make sense?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 이런 느낌입니다. 이해하셨나요?<end>"

## 1590150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "聖杯をねらってる悪い魔人は　<line-break>アーギルシャイア。<line-break>　<line-break>聖杯を持ってるゴブリンは<line-break>ゴブゴブ団っていって<line-break>今はエンシャントにいる…と。<end>"
EN: "So, the evil demon after the Grail is Argyleshire. The Goblins who have it are called the Gob-Gob Gang, and they're in Ancient right now... Got it.<end>"
KO: "성배를 노리는 사악한 마인은 아르길샤이어. 성배를 가진 고블린들은 고브고브단이라고 하고, 지금은 엔샨트에 있다… 이거군.<end>"

## 1590151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "忘れないように<line-break>覚えておかなくちゃ。　　　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I'd better remember that.<end>"
KO: "잊지 않도록 기억해 둬야겠어.<end>"

## 1590152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、<line-break>くれぐれも気をつけてください。<line-break>　<line-break>決して、<line-break>無理をしてはいけませんよ。<end>"
EN: "Then please, take great care. Whatever happens, don't push yourself too hard.<end>"
KO: "그럼 부디 조심하세요. 절대로 무리하시면 안 됩니다.<end>"

## 1590153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "네~!<end>"

## 1590156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Well, well. A girl coming here at this hour?<end>"

## 1590157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1590158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちは。<end>"
EN: "Hello.<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요.<end>"

## 1590159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちは。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>それにしても、このような時分に<line-break>この猫屋敷に、２人もお客さんが<line-break>見えるとはね。<end>"
EN: "Hello. I must say, I never expected two visitors at Cat Manor at such an hour.<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요. 이런 때에 이 고양이 저택에 손님이 두 분이나 오시다니.<end>"

## 1590160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まったく手間をとらせやがって！<end>"
EN: "You sure made me go to a lot of trouble, damn it!<end>"
KO: "정말 손이 많이 가게 하는군!<end>"

## 1590161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誘拐の現場を見られちまった以上、<line-break>生かしておくわけにはいかねぇ。<line-break>森の方で始末しちまおう。<end>"
EN: "Now that you've seen the kidnapping, we can't let you live. We'll take you into the woods and finish you off.<end>"
KO: "유괴 현장을 들킨 이상 살려 둘 수는 없어. 숲 쪽에서 처리해 버리자.<end>"

## 1590162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Who the hell are you?!<end>"

## 1590163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml

## 1590166

## 1590168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0それじゃあ、<line-break>お兄ちゃんが助けてくれたの？<end>それじゃあ、<line-break>お姉ちゃんが助けてくれたの？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0So you were the one who saved me, mister?<end>So you were the one who saved me, miss?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0그럼, 오빠가 도와준 거야?<end>그럼, 언니가 도와준 거야?<end>"

## 1590169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだったんだ。<line-break>ありがとう。<line-break>それに、ごめんなさい。<end>"
EN: "Oh, that's what happened. Thank you. And... I'm sorry.<end>"
KO: "그랬구나. 고마워. 그리고 미안해.<end>"

## 1590170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ふぅん。じゃ、<line-break>お兄ちゃんもあたしとおんなじだ。　<line-break>　<line-break>ルルアンタもね、<line-break>お父さんとお母さんいないよ。<end>ふぅん。じゃ、<line-break>お姉ちゃんもあたしとおんなじだ。　<line-break>　<line-break>ルルアンタもね、<line-break>お父さんとお母さんいないよ。<line-break>ずっとはやく、死んじゃった。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Oh. Then you're just like me, mister. Lulluantha doesn't have a mother or father either.<end>Oh. Then you're just like me, miss. Lulluantha doesn't have a mother or father either. They died a long, long time ago.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0흐음. 그럼 오빠도 나랑 똑같네. 루루안타도 아빠랑 엄마 없어.<end>흐음. 그럼 언니도 나랑 똑같네. 루루안타도 아빠랑 엄마 없어. 아주 오래전에 돌아가셨어.<end>"

## 1590171
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お世話になってたフリントさんも、　<line-break>旅の途中で盗賊に襲われて、<line-break>……………死んじゃった。<line-break>そのあとで、旅芸人の一座に<line-break>拾われたんだけど、その一座が、最近<line-break>起きている誘拐事件の犯人だったの。<end>"
EN: "Flint, who'd been looking after me, was attacked by bandits on the road, and... he died. After that, a troupe of traveling performers took me in. But they were the ones behind the recent kidnappings.<end>"
KO: "신세졌던 플린트 씨도 여행 중 도적에게 습격당해서… 죽었어. 그 뒤 유랑극단에 거둬졌는데, 그 극단이 최근 일어나는 유괴 사건의 범인이었던 거야.<end>"

## 1590172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0それを知っちゃって<line-break>ルルアンタも捕まっちゃったところを<line-break>お兄ちゃんに助けてもらったの。<end>それを知っちゃって<line-break>ルルアンタも捕まっちゃったところを<line-break>お姉ちゃんに助けてもらったの。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0When Lulluantha found out, they caught her too. Then you saved her, mister.<end>When Lulluantha found out, they caught her too. Then you saved her, miss.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0그걸 알아버려서 룰루안타도 붙잡혔는데 오빠가 구해 줬어.<end>그걸 알아버려서 룰루안타도 붙잡혔는데 언니가 구해 줬어.<end>"

## 1590173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うん、ルルアンタ、元気だよ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あのね、<line-break>フリントさんが言ってくれたんだ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "Mm-hm! Lulluantha's doing fine. You know, Flint once told me something.<end>"
KO: "응, 루루안타는 잘 지내. 있잖아, 플린트 씨가 말해 줬어.<end>"

## 1590174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ルルアンタは元気だね、<line-break>ルルアンタが元気だと私も<line-break>うれしくなって元気が出るって。<line-break>だから、ルルアンタ、<line-break>天国のフリントさんが<line-break>喜んでくれるように元気なんだ。<end>"
EN: "Flint said, “Lulluantha, you're always so cheerful. When you're cheerful, it makes me happy and cheers me up too.” So Lulluantha stays cheerful to make Flint happy in heaven.<end>"
KO: "루루안타는 건강하구나. 루루안타가 건강하면 나도 기뻐져서 힘이 난대. 그래서 루루안타는 천국의 플린트 씨가 기뻐하도록 건강한 거야.<end>"

## 1590176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0うふふ、じゃあ、ルルアンタ、<line-break>お兄ちゃんと一緒にいてあげるね。<line-break>　<line-break>お兄ちゃん、お名前は？<end>うふふ、じゃあ、ルルアンタ、<line-break>お姉ちゃんと一緒にいてあげるね。<line-break>　<line-break>お姉ちゃん、お名前は？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hee hee! Then Lulluantha will stay with you, mister. What's your name?<end>Hee hee! Then Lulluantha will stay with you, miss. What's your name?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0후후, 그럼 루루안타가 오빠랑 같이 있어 줄게. 오빠, 이름이 뭐야?<end>후후, 그럼 루루안타가 언니랑 같이 있어 줄게. 언니, 이름이 뭐야?<end>"

JP: "じゃ、あらためてぇ…。　　　　<end>"

## 1590181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml

## 1590183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、あなたたちは冒険者として　　<line-break>これから何をすればいいのかを、<line-break>　<line-break>探しているようですが、<line-break>その前に説明したいものがあります。<line-break>こちらに来てください。<end>"
EN: "Now, you seem to be wondering what you should do next as adventurers. Before that, though, there's something I'd like to explain. Come this way.<end>"
KO: "자, 여러분은 모험자로서 이제 무엇을 해야 할지 찾고 있는 것 같지만 그 전에 설명할 게 있습니다. 이쪽으로 오세요.<end>"

## 1590184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I'm not a cat! I'm Nemo―Nemo, one of the God of Destruction's Knights of the Round Table!<end>"
KO: "나는 고양이가 아니다, 네모다. 파괴신의 원탁기사 네모!<end>"

## 1590185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、すごいすごーい！！<end>"
