KO: "와, 대단해 대단해ー!!<end>"

## 1590186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、さすがは破壊神の円卓騎士　<line-break>ですよね。猫の姿になってなお<line-break>人語を操るんですから。<line-break>ま、こんな感じで、<line-break>これは遠くの人を呼び寄せたり…。<line-break>さて、それでは、もう一度。<end>"
EN: "But what else would you expect from one of the God of Destruction's Knights of the Round Table? Even as a cat, he can still speak the human tongue. Anyway, as you just saw, this device can summon people from far away... Now then, let's try that again.<end>"
KO: "하지만 역시 파괴신의 원탁기사답네요. 고양이 모습이 되어도 인간 말을 하니까요. 뭐, 이런 식으로 이건 멀리 있는 사람을 불러오거나…. 자, 그럼 다시 한번.<end>"

## 1590187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、今後は、パーティーメンバーの<line-break>了解を取ってから呼び出しますから<line-break>そのあたりのことはご心配なく。<line-break>それより、<line-break>用意してあった朝ごはんは<line-break>食べていただけましたか。<end>"
EN: "Oh, from now on I'll get your party members' permission before summoning them, so don't worry about that. Anyway, did you have breakfast?<end>"
KO: "아, 앞으로는 파티 멤버의 동의를 받은 뒤에 불러낼 테니 그 부분은 걱정하지 마세요. 그보다 준비해 둔 아침 식사는 드셨습니까?<end>"

## 1590188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、ありがとうございます。<line-break>何よりのほめ言葉です。<line-break>　<line-break>それでは、さっそくですが…<line-break>先日、ミイスの村が<line-break>消失したことをご存じですか？<end>"
EN: "Heh, thank you. I couldn't ask for higher praise. Now, to get straight to the matter... Have you heard that Miis Village vanished the other day?<end>"
KO: "후후, 감사합니다. 최고의 칭찬이네요. 그럼 바로… 얼마 전 미이스 마을이 사라진 것을 알고 계십니까?<end>"

## 1590189
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ことはミイスを焼き払った魔人<line-break>アーギルシャイアに関わります。<end>"
EN: "This concerns Argyleshire, the demon who burned Miis to the ground.<end>"
KO: "이 일은 미이스를 불태운 마인 아르길샤이어와 관련되어 있습니다.<end>"

## 1590190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神ウルグの円卓騎士と呼ばれる<line-break>強力な魔人の１人です。<line-break>　<line-break>妖艶な美女で、残酷で気まぐれです。<line-break>闇の神器をひとつ手に入れるために<line-break>ミイスを焼き払ってしまいました。<end>"
EN: "Argyleshire is one of the powerful demons known as Ulugh's Knights of the Round Table. She is a bewitching beauty, cruel and capricious. She burned Miis to the ground to obtain one of the dark divine artifacts.<end>"
KO: "파괴신 울그의 원탁기사라 불리는 강력한 마인 중 한 명입니다. 요염한 미녀이며 잔혹하고 변덕스럽습니다. 어둠의 신기 하나를 얻기 위해 미이스를 불태웠습니다.<end>"

## 1590191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ただ、彼女は<line-break>その本来の使い方以外のことを　　<line-break>しようとしています。<line-break>彼女は究極の合成怪物を作り<line-break>その力でおもしろ半分に世界を<line-break>破壊しようと考えています。<end>"
EN: "But she intends to use it for something other than its true purpose. She plans to create the ultimate composite monster and use its power to destroy the world merely for amusement.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그녀는 본래 용도 이외의 일을 하려 하고 있습니다. 궁극의 합성괴물을 만들어 그 힘으로 재미 삼아 세계를 파괴하려는 겁니다.<end>"

## 1590192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "破壊神の円卓騎士ネモですよ。<line-break>もともと聖杯は彼のものなのです。　　<line-break>　<line-break>アーギルシャイアの企みを知った彼は<line-break>自分の神器を奪われたくないと考え、<line-break>この際、神器の力でもとの姿に戻ろうと<end>"
EN: "Nemo, the God of Destruction's Knight of the Round Table. The Grail originally belonged to him. When he learned of Argyleshire's scheme, he decided he wouldn't let her steal his divine artifact and would use this opportunity to regain his original form with its power...<end>"
KO: "파괴신의 원탁기사 네모입니다. 원래 성배는 그의 것이었습니다. 아르길샤이어의 음모를 알게 된 그는 자신의 신기를 빼앗기고 싶지 않았고, 이참에 신기의 힘으로 원래 모습으로 돌아가려 한 것입니다.<end>"

## 1590193
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "急がないと、　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "We have to hurry, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "서두르지 않으면 안 돼, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1590194
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "円卓騎士には他に<line-break>アーギルシャイアなどがいますね。　　<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "The other Knights of the Round Table include Argyleshire.<end>"
KO: "원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 아르길샤이어 등이 있습니다.<end>"

## 1590195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "究極生物を作ろうとして、<line-break>その製法を神器、禁断の聖杯から　　<line-break>得ようと、聖杯をねらっています。<line-break>円卓騎士には他に<line-break>うちのネモなどがいますね。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "She seeks the Grail, hoping to learn how to create an Ultimate Life-Form from the divine artifact known as the Forbidden Grail. The other Knights of the Round Table include our own Nemo.<end>"
KO: "궁극 생물을 만들려고, 그 제조법을 신기, 금단의 성배에서 얻기 위해 성배를 노리고 있습니다. 원탁기사에는 그 밖에도 저희 네모 같은 이들이 있죠.<end>"

## 1590196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待ってよぉ！<end>"
EN: "Wait!<end>"
KO: "기다려어!<end>"

## 1590197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec159-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "もう、ちゃんとオルファウスさんに<line-break>ごあいさつしてよぉ。<line-break>　<line-break>そういうことって、いっつも<line-break>ルルアンタしか<line-break>気づかないんだからぁ！<end>"
EN: "Come on, say hello to Orphaus properly! Lulluantha's always the only one who notices things like that!<end>"
KO: "정말, 오르파우스 씨께 제대로 인사해 줘. 그런 건 늘 루루안타만 알아챈다니까!<end>"

## 1600000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>どうだい、私が君の父親になってあげようか？<line-break>お父さんと呼んでくれたまえ…ひっく。<end>"
EN: "Hello, <value:$28>. How would you like me to be your father? Go on, call me Papa... hic!<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>. 어때, 내가 네 아버지가 되어 줄까? 아빠라고 불러 보게… 딸꾹.<end>"

## 1600001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "大人になったら都会に行ってやるって<line-break>思ってたのに…。<line-break>なぜか離れられないんだよな。<end>"
EN: "I thought I'd go off to the city once I grew up... But for some reason, I just can't seem to leave.<end>"
KO: "어른이 되면 도시로 나가 버릴 생각이었는데…. 이상하게 떠날 수가 없네.<end>"

## 1600002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う～ん、どう見てもアスティアさんと<line-break>お前が親子だなんて思えないな。<line-break>雰囲気がぜ～んぜん違うんだよ。<end>"
EN: "Hmm... Looking at the two of you, I'd never think you and Astia were parent and child. You're nothing alike.<end>"
KO: "음~ 아무리 봐도 아스티아 씨와 네가 부모자식이라고는 생각 안 되네. 분위기가 전혀 다르잖아.<end>"

## 1600003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ねえ、<value:$28>。<line-break>また、一緒に森に行こうね。<line-break>約束だよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Let's go to the forest together again sometime. Promise?<end>"
KO: "있잖아, <value:$28>. 또 같이 숲에 가자. 약속이야.<end>"

## 1600004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、<value:$28>。<line-break>このあいだ魔物が町に迷い込んだときは、<line-break>大変だったわね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. It was awful when that monster wandered into town the other day, wasn't it?<end>"
KO: "어머, <value:$28>. 지난번에 마물이 마을에 들어왔을 때는 큰일이었지.<end>"

## 1600005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "村の周りを魔物がうろうろしていて、<line-break>外との交流がほとんどないだろう？<line-break>世界で何がおこってるのかわかりゃしないよ。<end>"
EN: "With monsters prowling around the village, we hardly have any contact with the outside world. We haven't a clue what's going on out there.<end>"
KO: "마을 주변을 마물이 어슬렁거려서 외부와 거의 교류가 없잖아? 세상에서 무슨 일이 일어나는지 알 수가 없어.<end>"

## 1600006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "外との交流がないんで、<line-break>たいしたもんはないけどな。<end>"
EN: "We don't have much contact with the outside, so there isn't much here.<end>"
KO: "밖과 교류가 없어서 대단한 건 없지만 말이야.<end>"

## 1600007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キャー！<end>"
EN: "Aaaah!<end>"
KO: "꺄악!<end>"

## 1600008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰か助けてくれーー！<end>"
EN: "Somebody, help me!<end>"
KO: "누가 좀 도와줘ーー!<end>"

## 1600009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勘弁してくれーーー！<line-break>わしは死にたくない！<end>"
EN: "Spare me! I don't want to die!<end>"
KO: "살려 줘어어! 나는 죽고 싶지 않아!<end>"

## 1600010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec160-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オズワルド＆ミイスメッセージここまで<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "오즈왈드&미이스 메시지 여기까지<end>"

## 1610000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おう、<value:$28>か。<line-break>ちょうどいい所に来てくれた。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>お前さんの腕を見込んで、ひとつ<line-break>頼みたいことがあるんだが…。<line-break>聞いてくれるか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. You came at just the right time. I know what you're capable of, and there's something I'd like to ask of you. Will you hear me out?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>인가. 마침 잘 왔다. 네 솜씨를 믿고 하나 부탁하고 싶은 게 있는데…. 들어 줄래?<end>"

## 1610002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "話してみて<end>"
EN: "Let's hear it.<end>"
KO: "말해 봐<end>"

## 1610003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今忙しいんで<end>"
EN: "I'm busy right now.<end>"
KO: "지금 바빠서<end>"

## 1610004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "実はな、最近、風泣きの洞窟に　　　　<line-break>ものすごい怪物が出るらしい。<line-break>　<line-break>あそこは島唯一のコロル石の産地だから<line-break>町の連中も割と足を踏み入れるんだが、<line-break>このままではとてつもなく危険だ。<end>"
EN: "It seems a fearsome monster has recently appeared in Wind-Wailing Cave. That's the island's only source of Korol Stone, so people from town go in there fairly often. It's far too dangerous to leave things as they are.<end>"
KO: "실은 최근 바람울음 동굴에 엄청난 괴물이 나타난다는군. 거긴 섬에서 유일한 코롤석 산지라 마을 사람들도 자주 들어가는데, 이대로면 너무 위험해.<end>"

## 1610005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そこで、その怪物を倒してもらいたい。<line-break>報酬は、町のみんなで出し合って払う。<line-break>どうだい？　やってくれないか？<end>"
EN: "So I'd like you to defeat the monster. Everyone in town will contribute toward your reward. What do you say? Will you do it?<end>"
KO: "그래서 그 괴물을 쓰러뜨려줬으면 한다. 보수는 마을 사람들이 함께 돈을 모아 낼 거야. 어때? 해주지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1610006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "任せて<end>"
EN: "Leave it to me.<end>"
KO: "맡겨<end>"

## 1610007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "勘弁して<end>"
EN: "Count me out.<end>"
KO: "봐줘<end>"

## 1610008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか！　引き受けてくれるか！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ありがとよ。<line-break>さすがは<value:$2B>だ。<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ。<end>"
EN: "You will? You'll take the job? Thank you. Just what I'd expect from <value:$2B>. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "그래! 맡아 주는 거냐! 고맙다. 역시 <value:$2B>군. 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1610009
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか！　引き受けてくれるか！　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ありがとよ。<line-break>さすがは<value:$28>だ。<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ。<end>"
EN: "You will? You'll take the job? Thank you. Just what I'd expect from <value:$28>. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "그래! 맡아 주는 거냐! 고맙다. 역시 <value:$28>이군. 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1610010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっ！　<line-break>そ、そうか…、勘弁してくれ、か…。　<line-break>　<line-break>いや、こっちとしても仕事を無理強い<line-break>するわけにはいかないからな…。<line-break>じゃあ、気が向いたらまた来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "Urk! R-right... You'd rather not, then. Well, I can't force a job on you. Come back if you ever feel like it.<end>"
KO: "윽! 그, 그런가… 봐 달라는 건가…. 아니, 우리도 일을 억지로 시킬 순 없으니까…. 마음 내키면 다시 와 줘.<end>"

## 1610011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうなのか…。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>悪かったな。<line-break>　<line-break>ちょっと、困った事になっててな。<line-break>そうか、忙しいなら仕方ないな…。<line-break>また暇な時にでも頼むよ。<end>"
EN: "O-oh, right... Sorry. It's just that we're in a bit of a bind. Still, if you're busy, there's no helping it. Maybe I can ask you again when you have some time.<end>"
KO: "그, 그런가…. 미안하군. 좀 곤란한 일이 있어서 말이야. 그래, 바쁘다면 어쩔 수 없지…. 또 한가할 때 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1610012
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그럼 조심해서 모험을 계속해. 뭐, 너라면 걱정 없겠지만.<end>"

## 1610016
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ何も言ってないのに<line-break>ずいぶんと安請け合いなのね。<end>"

## 1610017
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、いいわ。<line-break>きっとそれも自信があるからなのよね。<line-break>　<line-break>実は、この街から少し行った所にある<line-break>乙女の鏡という湖に、すごく強そうな<line-break>魔物が住み着いたの。<end>"
EN: "Well, never mind. I suppose that means you're confident in your abilities. The truth is, there's a lake called Maiden's Mirror not far outside town, and a very formidable-looking monster has taken up residence there.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 좋아. 분명 그만큼 자신이 있다는 거겠지. 사실 이 도시에서 조금 떨어진 소녀의 거울이라는 호수에 아주 강해 보이는 마물이 눌러앉았어.<end>"

## 1610018
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あそこ、私のお気に入りなのよね。<line-break>だから、お願い。私のために<line-break>あの魔物を倒してきてくれないかしら？<end>"
EN: "It's one of my favorite places. So please, would you go and defeat that monster for me?<end>"
KO: "거기, 내가 좋아하는 곳이거든. 그러니까 부탁해. 나를 위해 그 마물을 쓰러뜨려 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1610019

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Leave it to me.<end>"
KO: "맡겨<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610021
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、頼もしいのね。<line-break>それじゃ、お願いしようかしら。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>報酬は仕事が終わったらお渡しするわ。<line-break>頑張って魔物を追い出してね。<end>"
EN: "Oh, how reassuring. Then I'll leave it to you. I'll give you your reward once the job is done. Good luck driving that monster out.<end>"
KO: "어머, 든든하네. 그럼 부탁할게. 보수는 일이 끝나면 줄게. 힘내서 마물을 쫓아내 줘.<end>"

## 1610022
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "報酬は仕事が終わったらお渡しするわ。<line-break>頑張って魔物を追い出してね。<end>"
EN: "You'll get your reward once the job is done. Do your best to drive the monsters out.<end>"
KO: "보수는 일이 끝나면 줄게. 힘내서 마물들을 쫓아내 줘.<end>"

## 1610023
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、それができるようなら　　　　　<line-break>とっくにそうしてるわ。<line-break>　<line-break>だからあなたのような<line-break>有名人に頼んだのに…。<line-break>仕方ないわね。しばらく他を当たるわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, if I could do that, I'd have done it long ago. That's why I asked someone famous like you... Very well. I'll try elsewhere for the time being.<end>"
KO: "어머, 그게 가능했으면 진작 했지. 그래서 당신 같은 유명인에게 부탁한 건데…. 어쩔 수 없네. 당분간 다른 사람을 알아볼게.<end>"

## 1610024
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、まだ何も言ってないのに　　<line-break>もうお断りなの？<line-break>　<line-break>案外、噂ほどでもないのかしら？<line-break>まあ、いいわ。それじゃあ失礼。<line-break>また今度頼みに来ることにするわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh, I haven't even said what I want, and you're already refusing? Perhaps you're not quite as impressive as the rumors suggest. No matter. I'll be on my way, then. I'll come ask you again another time.<end>"
KO: "어머, 아직 아무 말도 안 했는데 벌써 거절하는 거야? 의외로 소문만큼은 아닌가 보네. 뭐, 됐어. 그럼 실례할게. 다음에 다시 부탁하러 오지.<end>"

## 1610029
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、いや、恥ずかしい話なんだが…。　<line-break>この町の先に、幻惑の洞窟があるだろ？<line-break>　<line-break>俺、あそこでコロル石を採取する仕事を<line-break>受けたんだ。でも、中にものすごい獣が<line-break>いて、とてもじゃないが無理なんだ。<end>"
EN: "I-it's embarrassing to admit, but... You know the Bewildering Cave just beyond this town? I took a job collecting Korol Stone there. But there's a terrifying beast inside, and there's no way I can handle it.<end>"
KO: "아, 아니, 부끄러운 얘긴데…. 이 마을 너머에 환혹의 동굴 있잖아? 거기서 코롤석을 채취하는 일을 맡았거든. 그런데 안에 엄청난 짐승이 있어서 도저히 무리야.<end>"

## 1610030
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頼む！　仕事をキャンセルするのも<line-break>恥ずかしいんだ。どうかあの獣を倒して<line-break>俺が入れるようにしてくれぇ！<end>"
EN: "I'm begging you! It'd be embarrassing to cancel the job. Please defeat that beast so I can get inside!<end>"
KO: "부탁이야! 일을 취소하는 것도 창피해. 제발 그 짐승을 쓰러뜨려서 내가 들어갈 수 있게 해 줘!<end>"

## 1610031

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方ないなあ<end>"
EN: "All right, I guess.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없네<end>"

## 1610032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "情けないなあ<end>"
EN: "That's pathetic.<end>"
KO: "한심하네<end>"

## 1610033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうか！　やってくれるか！！<line-break>ありがとう。本当にありがとう！<line-break>　<line-break>あの獣を倒してきてくれたら、<line-break>俺の宝をあんたにやるよ。<line-break>じゃ、倒したらまた来てくれよ。<end>"
EN: "Y-you'll do it? Thank you! Thank you so much! If you defeat that beast for me, I'll give you my treasure. Come back once you've defeated it, all right?<end>"
KO: "그, 그래! 해 주는 거냐!! 고마워. 정말 고마워! 그 짐승을 쓰러뜨리고 오면 내 보물을 줄게. 쓰러뜨리면 다시 와 줘.<end>"

## 1610034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕事、よろしく頼んだぜ。<line-break>あんたにも俺にもいい話だろ？<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you for the job. It's a good deal for both of us, right?<end>"
KO: "일은 잘 부탁한다. 너한테도 나한테도 좋은 이야기잖아?<end>"

## 1610035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、仕方ないじゃないかよぉぉ！　　　<line-break>俺だって、俺だってなあぁ…！<line-break>　<line-break>もういい　あんたになんか頼まん！<line-break>見てろよ、俺はやりゃあできるんだよ！<end>"
EN: "I-I can't help it! Even I... even I...! Forget it! I won't ask you for anything! Just you watch! I can do it if I try!<end>"
KO: "어, 어쩔 수 없잖아아아! 나도, 나도 말이야…! 됐어, 당신한테는 안 부탁해! 두고 봐, 나도 하면 할 수 있다고!<end>"

## 1610036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何ぃっ！！<line-break>まさか、そんな言われ方するとは！　　<line-break>　<line-break>有名な<value:$28>も、<line-break>案外ひどい奴なんだな！<line-break>もういい！　あんたとはもう口きかん！<end>"
EN: "W-what?! I never thought you'd say something like that to me! For all your fame, <value:$28>, you're actually pretty awful! Forget it! I'm never speaking to you again!<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐라고!! 설마 그런 말을 들을 줄이야! 유명한 <value:$28>도, 의외로 심한 녀석이구나! 됐어! 이제 당신하고 말 안 해!<end>"

## 1610037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それでは、お願いしましたよ。<line-break>冒険の子らに神の導きがあらんことを。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll leave it in your hands. May divine guidance be with the children of adventure.<end>"
KO: "그럼 부탁드리겠습니다. 모험의 아이들에게 신의 인도가 함께하기를.<end>"

## 1610038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "失礼。<end>"
EN: "If you'll excuse me.<end>"
KO: "실례.<end>"

## 1610039
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "え、ああ、今の神官様かい？　　　　　<line-break>仕事を依頼しに来たんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>ちょうどいい。<line-break><value:$28>、この仕事<line-break>やってみるかい？<end>"
EN: "Hm? Oh, the priest who just left? Here to commission a job. Good timing. <value:$28>, care to take it on?<end>"
KO: "아, 지금 신관님 말이야? 일을 의뢰하러 왔어. 마침 잘됐네. <value:$28>, 이 일 해 볼래?<end>"

## 1610042
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "魔物退治だ。<line-break>しかし、普通の魔物ならいいんだが…。<line-break>　<line-break>あの神官様が言うには、その魔物からは<line-break>とてつもない闇の力を感じるらしい。<line-break>だから、退治を依頼するんだそうだ。<end>"
EN: "It's a monster hunt. An ordinary monster would be one thing, but... The priest says there's an immense dark power coming from it. That's why the hunt was commissioned.<end>"
KO: "마물 퇴치야. 보통 마물이면 좋겠는데…. 그 신관님 말로는 그 마물에게서 엄청난 어둠의 힘이 느껴진대. 그래서 퇴치를 의뢰한 거래.<end>"

## 1610043
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そいつは、ウルカーンへの街道沿いから<line-break>見える、火山岩地帯にいるらしい。<line-break>どうだ？　行ってきてみるか？<end>"
EN: "Apparently it's in the Volcanic Rock Zone, visible from the road to Ulkarne. What do you say? Want to take the job?<end>"
KO: "그 녀석은 울칸으로 가는 가도에서 보이는 화산암 지대에 있다는군. 어때? 한번 가 볼래?<end>"

## 1610044
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退治に行く<end>"
EN: "Go defeat it.<end>"
KO: "퇴치하러 간다<end>"

## 1610045
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめておく<end>"
EN: "Better not.<end>"
KO: "그만둔다<end>"

## 1610046
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。いや、あんたのことだ。<line-break>絶対に引き受けてくれると思ってたよ。<line-break>　<line-break>あ、そうそう。言い忘れてたけど、<line-break>報酬はもう神官様からもらってる。<line-break>３０００ギアだ。結構なもんだろ？<end>"
EN: "Right. Well, knowing you, I was sure you'd take it. Oh, and I forgot to mention: I've already received the reward from the priest. Three thousand Gea. Not bad, eh?<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 아니, 너라면 반드시 맡아 줄 줄 알았어. 아, 맞다. 말하는 걸 잊었는데 보수는 이미 신관님에게 받았어. 3000기어야. 꽤 되지?<end>"

## 1610047
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、頼んだぜ。　　　　　　　<line-break>終わったらまた来てくれよ。<line-break>　<end>"
EN: "I'll leave it to you, then. Come back when it's done.<end>"
KO: "그럼 부탁한다. 끝나면 다시 와 줘.<end>"

## 1610048
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、ま、お前さんも　<line-break>色々と忙しいだろうからな。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>無理に頼みはしないよ。<line-break>そのうち気が向いたら<line-break>また仕事を探しに来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "All right. I suppose you've got plenty keeping you busy. I won't press you. If you feel like it sometime, come back and look for work again.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 뭐, 너도 여러모로 바쁘겠지. 억지로 부탁하진 않을게. 나중에 마음이 내키면 또 일을 찾으러 와 줘.<end>"

## 1610049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、ま、お前さんも　<line-break>色々と忙しいだろうからな。　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>無理に頼みはしないよ。<line-break>まあ、気が向いたら<line-break>話だけでも聞きに来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "All right. I suppose you've got plenty keeping you busy. I won't press you. If you feel like it, come back sometime and at least hear about the job.<end>"
KO: "그래, 뭐 너도 여러모로 바쁘겠지. 억지로 부탁하진 않을게. 마음이 내키면 이야기만이라도 들으러 와줘.<end>"

## 1610050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아, 당신… 슬럼에서 타르튜바를 되받아친 사람이군…<end>"

## 1610051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml

## 1610052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺たち、冒険者になっての初仕事で<line-break>城塞都市跡まで行ったんだ…。<line-break>　<line-break>そこで恐ろしい魔獣に出会って、<line-break>みんな…。俺ひとり、命からがら<line-break>ここまで逃げ延びたってわけさ。<end>"
EN: "It was our first job as adventurers. We went to the Fortress City Ruins... That's where we ran into a terrifying beast, and everyone... I was the only one who escaped. I barely made it back here alive.<end>"
KO: "…우리, 모험가가 되고 첫 일로 성채도시 터까지 갔어…. 거기서 무시무시한 마수를 만나서 모두가…. 나 혼자 간신히 여기까지 도망쳐 온 거야.<end>"

## 1610056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "頼む。あいつらの仇を討ってくれ…。<line-break>ここに２０００ギアある。少ないが<line-break>報酬として前渡ししよう…。<end>"
EN: "Please. Avenge them. I've got 2,000 Gea here. It isn't much, but you can have it up front as payment...<end>"
KO: "부탁한다. 녀석들의 원수를 갚아 줘…. 여기 2000기어가 있어. 적지만 보수로 선불하겠다….<end>"

## 1610059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "I see... That's too bad. See you...<end>"

## 1610061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "……미안…하다…. 조금… 쉬게… 해 주지 않겠나….<end>"

## 1610063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お前、<value:$28>か…？<line-break>これはおどろいた。みちがえたな。　<line-break>　<line-break>噂は聞いていたんだがな。あの有名な<line-break><value:$2B>がお前だってのは。<line-break>しかし、こうも立派になるとは…。<end>"
EN: "Y-you're <value:$28>...? Well, I'll be. I hardly recognized you. I'd heard the rumors―that you were the famous <value:$2B>. But I never imagined you'd become so impressive...<end>"
KO: "너, 너 <value:$28>냐…? 이거 놀랍군. 못 알아보겠어. 소문은 들었지만 말이야. 그 유명한 <value:$2B>가 너라니. 그래도 이렇게 훌륭해질 줄은….<end>"

## 1610064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お、お前、<value:$28>か…？<line-break>これはおどろいた。みちがえたな。　<line-break>　<line-break>噂は聞いていたんだがな。お前が<line-break>大陸屈指の冒険者になったってのは。<line-break>しかし、こうも立派になるとは…。<end>"
EN: "Y-you're <value:$28>...? Well, I'll be. I hardly recognized you. I'd heard the rumors―that you'd become one of the finest adventurers on the continent. But I never imagined you'd become so impressive...<end>"
KO: "너, <value:$28>인가…? 놀랍군. 못 알아볼 뻔했어. 소문은 들었다. 네가 대륙 굴지의 모험자가 됐다더군. 하지만 이렇게 훌륭해질 줄이야….<end>"

## 1610065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前があの<value:$28>だって？<line-break>こりゃおどろいた。まだ若いんだな。　<line-break>　<line-break>人生ってのは人それぞれだと思うが…、<line-break>その若さで、ものすごい冒険の数々を<line-break>してきたっていうのか…。<end>"
EN: "You're the famous <value:$28>? Well, I'll be. You're still so young. Everyone takes a different path through life, I suppose, but... have you really had all those incredible adventures at your age?<end>"
KO: "네가 그 <value:$28>라고? 이거 놀랍군. 아직 젊잖아. 인생은 사람마다 다르다지만… 그 나이에 엄청난 모험들을 해 왔다는 건가….<end>"

## 1610066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし！　俺もギルドの親父として　　　<line-break>お前に負けない仕事をしよう！<line-break>　<line-break>そうだ！　ちょうどいい大物の仕事が<line-break>入ってきてるんだ！　どうだい？<line-break><value:$28>、やってみないか？<end>"
EN: "Right! As Guildmaster, I'd better find you work worthy of your talents! In fact, a major job has just come in. What do you say, <value:$28>? Want to give it a try?<end>"
KO: "좋아! 나도 길드 주인으로서 너한테 지지 않을 일을 해 보지! 그래! 마침 큰 건 하나가 들어왔어! 어때, <value:$28>? 해 보지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1610067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕事内容を聞く<end>"
EN: "Ask about the job<end>"
KO: "일의 내용을 묻는다<end>"

## 1610068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "지금은 그만둔다<end>"

## 1610069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ここから北にある紺碧の洞窟で　　　　<line-break>怪物退治をするっていう仕事さ。<line-break>　<line-break>帝国のコロル石採掘部門からの依頼で、<line-break>報酬は５０００ギアだ。<line-break>腕試しがてら、引き受けてみるかい？<end>"
EN: "It's a monster-hunting job in Azure Cave, north of here. The request comes from the Empire's Korol Stone Mining Division, and it pays 5,000 Gea. How about taking it on as a test of your skills?<end>"
KO: "여기서 북쪽에 있는 푸른 동굴에서 괴물을 퇴치하는 일이야. 제국의 코롤석 채굴 부문 의뢰고 보수는 5000기어다. 실력 시험 삼아 맡아 볼래?<end>"

## 1610070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 1610071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "그만둔다<end>"

## 1610072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだろうな。お前ほどの冒険者なら<line-break>断る理由が見つからないからな。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃ、片付いたらまたここに来てくれ。<line-break>偉大な冒険者に報酬を渡すのを<line-break>楽しみにしてるよ。<end>"
EN: "I figured you would. An adventurer like you has no reason to turn it down. Once it's taken care of, come back here. It'll be a pleasure to hand a great adventurer the reward.<end>"
KO: "그렇겠지. 너 정도 모험자라면 거절할 이유가 없으니까. 그럼 일이 끝나면 다시 여기로 와 줘. 위대한 모험자에게 보수를 건네는 걸 기대하고 있겠어.<end>"

## 1610073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けないのか！？　…まあ、<line-break>お前にも都合があるだろうからな。<line-break>　<line-break>またいつか、気が向いたら<line-break>引き受けてくれ。たぶんこんな仕事は<line-break>お前くらいにしかできないだろう。<end>"
EN: "You won't take it!? Well... I'm sure you have your reasons. Maybe you'll take it on another time, if you feel like it. It's probably a job only someone of your caliber can handle.<end>"
KO: "안 맡는 거야!? …뭐, 너도 사정이 있겠지. 다음에 마음이 내키면 맡아 줘. 아마 이런 일은 너 정도밖에 못 할 거야.<end>"

## 1610074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。じゃあ、この話は他の奴に<line-break>声をかけてみることにするよ。<end>"
EN: "I see... In that case, I'll offer the job to someone else.<end>"
KO: "그런가… 그럼 이 이야기는 다른 사람에게 부탁해 보기로 하지.<end>"

## 1610075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当なんだよ！<line-break>本当に、鳳凰がいたんだよ！<end>"
EN: "It's true! There really was a phoenix!<end>"
KO: "진짜라니까! 정말로 봉황이 있었어!<end>"

## 1610076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "봉황산에 봉황이 있다…라…. 뭔가 너무 단순한데…….<end>"

## 1610077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hey, mister, you're an adventurer, right? Go find the phoenix on Phoenix Mountain for me!<end>Hey, miss, you're an adventurer, right? Go find the phoenix on Phoenix Mountain for me!<end>"

## 1610080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最近の子供たちの間での話題さ。　　<line-break>鳳凰山の頂上に鳳凰がいるってね。<line-break>　<line-break>まったく、どこの子が言い出したのか<line-break>わからんが、もし鳳凰山に登って<line-break>ケガでもしたら一大事だよ。<end>"
EN: "All the children are talking about it lately. They say there's a phoenix at the top of Phoenix Mountain. I don't know who started the rumor, but if one of them climbs the mountain and gets hurt, we'll have a real problem on our hands.<end>"
KO: "요즘 아이들 사이의 화제야. 봉황산 정상에 봉황이 있다는 거지. 대체 어느 아이가 시작한 말인지 모르겠지만 봉황산에 올라갔다가 다치기라도 하면 큰일이야.<end>"

## 1610081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だーかーらー！<line-break>絶っ対にいるんだってば！！<end>"
EN: "That's what I've been telling you! It's definitely there!<end>"
KO: "그러니까! 절대로 있다니까!!<end>"

## 1610082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、頼むよ。鳳凰を見つけてきてよ。<line-break>本当にいるんだよ。ね、ね！<end>"
EN: "Please, I'm begging you. Go find the phoenix for me. It really is there. Please, please!<end>"
KO: "부탁이야. 봉황을 찾아와 줘. 정말 있다니까. 응, 응!<end>"

## 1610086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それ、ボクの今月のおこづかい全部。<line-break>ほうしゅう、いるんでしょ？<line-break>　<line-break>じゃ、お願いしたからね。<line-break>絶対、見つけてきてね！！<end>"
EN: "That's my whole allowance for this month. You need a reward, right? Okay, I'm counting on you. You have to find it!<end>"
KO: "그거, 내 이번 달 용돈 전부야. 보수 필요하지? 그럼 부탁했어. 반드시 찾아와야 해!!<end>"

## 1610087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그럴 리 없어!! 반드시, 반드시 봉황은 있어!!<end>"

## 1610088

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610089

## 1610090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>。<line-break>この前話した風泣きの洞窟の件だが…、<line-break>　<line-break>いまだに怪物は退治されてないんだ。<line-break>頼む。お前さんがやってくれないか？<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>. About that business with Wind-Wailing Cave I mentioned before... The monster still hasn't been dealt with. Please, could you take care of it?<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 전에 말한 바람우는 동굴 건인데 아직 괴물은 퇴치되지 않았어. 부탁한다. 네가 해 주지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1610093
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか！　引き受けてくれるか！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>ありがとよ。<line-break>さすがは<value:$2B>だ。<line-break>よろしく頼むぜ。<end>"

## 1610094
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그래! 맡아 주는 거냐! 고맙다. 역시 <value:$28>이군. 잘 부탁한다.<end>"

## 1610095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うっ！　<line-break>そ、そうか…、勘弁してくれ、か…。　<line-break>　<line-break>いや、こっちとしても仕事を無理強い<line-break>するわけにはいかないからな…。<line-break>じゃあ、気が向いたらまた来てくれ。<end>"

## 1610096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、気をつけて冒険を続けてくれ。<line-break>まあ、お前さんなら心配いらんか。<end>"
EN: "Take care as you continue your adventures. Though I suppose there's no need to worry about someone like you.<end>"
KO: "그럼 조심해서 모험을 계속해. 뭐, 너라면 걱정 없겠지만.<end>"

## 1610097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、また会ったわね。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>先日話した、乙女の鏡にいる魔物の話、<line-break>引き受けてくれる気になったのかしら？<end>"
EN: "Oh, we meet again. Have you decided to take care of that monster in Maiden's Mirror I told you about the other day?<end>"
KO: "어머, 또 만났네. 전에 말한 소녀의 거울에 있는 마물 이야기, 맡아 줄 마음이 생겼어?<end>"

## 1610100
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、頼もしいのね。　　　　　　　　<line-break>それじゃ、お願いしようかしら。<line-break>　<line-break>報酬は仕事が終わったらお渡しするわ。<line-break>がんばって魔物を追い出してね。<end>"

## 1610101

## 1610102
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、それができるようなら　　　　<line-break>とっくにそうしてるわ。<line-break>　<line-break>だから先日から、評判の高い冒険者の<line-break>あなたに頼んでいるのに…。<line-break>仕方ないわね。また出なおすわ。<end>"
EN: "Oh? If I could do that, I'd have done it long ago. That's why I've kept asking a well-regarded adventurer like you for help... Oh well. I'll come back another time.<end>"
KO: "어머, 그게 가능했다면 벌써 했지. 그래서 며칠 전부터 평판 좋은 모험자인 당신에게 부탁하는 거잖아…. 어쩔 수 없네. 다음에 다시 올게.<end>"

## 1610103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>っ！！<line-break>た、頼む！　助けてくれぇぇ！！　　<line-break>　<line-break>やっぱり俺には幻惑の洞窟の獣なんて<line-break>倒せっこない！　ダメだ！　ムリだ！<line-break>頼む、あんたが倒してきてくれぇぇ！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>!! P-please! Help me! I should've known―there's no way I can beat the beast in the Bewildering Cave! I can't do it! It's impossible! Please, you've got to go defeat it for me!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>!! 부, 부탁이야! 살려 줘어어!! 역시 나한텐 환혹의 동굴 괴물 따윈 못 쓰러뜨려! 안 돼! 무리야! 부탁이야, 네가 쓰러뜨려 줘어어!<end>"

## 1610104
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕方ないなあ<end>"
EN: "Oh, all right.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없네<end>"

## 1610105
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "情けないなあ<end>"
EN: "Pathetic.<end>"
KO: "한심하네<end>"

## 1610106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうか！　やってくれるか！！<line-break>ありがとう。本当にありがとう！<line-break>　<line-break>あの獣を倒してきてくれたら、<line-break>俺の宝をあんたにやるよ。<line-break>じゃ、倒したらまた来てくれよ。<end>"
EN: "S-so you will? You'll do it?! Thank you. Thank you so much! If you defeat that beast for me, I'll give you my treasure. Come back once you've defeated it, all right?<end>"
KO: "그, 그래! 해 주는 거냐!! 고마워. 정말 고마워! 그 짐승을 쓰러뜨리고 오면 내 보물을 줄게. 쓰러뜨리면 다시 와 줘.<end>"

## 1610107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "仕事、よろしく頼んだぜ。<line-break>あんたにも俺にもいい話だろ？<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you for this job. It's a good deal for both of us, right?<end>"
KO: "일은 잘 부탁한다. 너한테도 나한테도 좋은 이야기잖아?<end>"

## 1610108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "し、仕方ないじゃないかよぉぉ！　　　<line-break>俺だって、俺だってなあぁ…！<line-break>　<line-break>もういい！　あんたになんか頼まん！<line-break>見てろよ、俺はやりゃあできるんだよ！<line-break>絶対、あいつを倒してきてやるからな！<end>"
EN: "I-it's not like I can help it! Even I... Even I could...! Fine! I won't ask you for help! Just you watch! I can do it if I put my mind to it! I'll defeat that thing myself, you'll see!<end>"
KO: "어, 어쩔 수 없잖아아아! 나도, 나도 말이야아…! 이제 됐어! 당신한테는 부탁 안 해! 두고 봐, 난 하면 할 수 있어! 반드시 그 녀석을 쓰러뜨리고 올 거야!<end>"

## 1610109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よう、<value:$28>。<line-break>こないだの神官様の話、覚えてるか？<line-break>　<line-break>ほら、火山岩地帯にいる魔物の話さ。<line-break>こんな仕事を任せられるのはあんた<line-break>くらいだ。やってみないかい？<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. Remember what that priest was saying the other day? About the monster in the Volcanic Rock Zone. You're about the only one I'd trust with a job like this. How about it?<end>"
KO: "어이, <value:$28>. 전에 그 신관님 이야기 기억나? 화산암 지대에 있는 마물 얘기 말이야. 이런 일을 맡길 수 있는 건 너 정도야. 해 볼래?<end>"

## 1610110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "退治に行く<end>"
EN: "Go defeat the monster<end>"
KO: "퇴치하러 간다<end>"

## 1610111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やめておく<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "그만둔다<end>"

## 1610112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。いや、あんたのことだ。　　　<line-break>絶対に引き受けてくれると思ってたよ。<line-break>　<line-break>あ、そうそう。言い忘れてたけど、<line-break>報酬はもう神官様からもらってる。<line-break>３０００ギアだ。結構なもんだろ？<end>"
EN: "Good. I knew I could count on you to take it. Oh, that's right―I forgot to mention, the priest already gave me the reward. It's 3,000 Gea. Not bad, eh?<end>"
KO: "그렇군. 아니, 너라면 반드시 맡아 줄 줄 알았어. 아, 맞다. 말하는 걸 잊었는데 보수는 이미 신관님에게 받았어. 3000기어야. 꽤 되지?<end>"

## 1610113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃあ、頼んだぜ。<line-break>終わったらまた来てくれよ。<end>"
EN: "All right, I'm counting on you. Come back when it's done.<end>"
KO: "그럼 부탁한다. 끝나면 다시 와 줘.<end>"

## 1610114
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、ま、お前さんも<line-break>色々と忙しいだろうからな。<line-break>　<line-break>無理に頼みはしないよ。<line-break>そのうち気が向いたら<line-break>また仕事を探しに来てくれ。<end>"
EN: "Fair enough. I suppose you've got plenty keeping you busy. I won't push you into it. If you feel like it sometime, come back and look for work again.<end>"
KO: "그런가. 뭐, 너도 여러모로 바쁘겠지. 억지로 부탁하진 않을게. 나중에 마음이 내키면 또 일을 찾으러 와 줘.<end>"

## 1610115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よお、<value:$28>…。この前<line-break>ここに座ってた男を憶えてるか？　　　<line-break>　<line-break>あいつ…死んじまったよ…。<line-break>仲間のことを最後までくやんでな…。<line-break>切なそうな顔で息を引き取ったよ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>... Remember the man who was sitting here the other day? He... died. He grieved for his companions right to the end. He looked so full of sorrow when he passed.<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>…. 전에 여기 앉아 있던 남자 기억나? 그 녀석… 죽었어…. 끝까지 동료들을 후회하며… 애처로운 얼굴로 숨을 거뒀지.<end>"

## 1610116
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なあ、俺からも頼む。あいつの供養だ。<line-break>城塞都市跡の魔獣を倒してやってくれ。<line-break>　<line-break>ここに２０００ギアある。<line-break>あいつが最期の時、俺に託したものだ。<line-break>これで、仕事を受けてくれないか？<end>"
EN: "Listen, I'm asking you as well. Do it in his memory. Kill the beast at the Fortress City Ruins. Here's 2,000 Gea. He entrusted it to me when he was dying. Will you take the job for that amount?<end>"
KO: "있잖아, 나도 부탁할게. 그 녀석을 위한 공양이야. 성새도시 터의 마수를 쓰러뜨려 줘. 여기 2000기어가 있어. 그 녀석이 마지막 순간 내게 맡긴 거야. 이걸로 일을 맡아 주지 않을래?<end>"

## 1610117

KO: "알았어<end>"

## 1610119

EN: "All right. Thanks. I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "그래, 고맙다. 그럼 부탁하지.<end>"

## 1610120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。残念だ。<line-break>意外と淡白な性格なんだな…。<end>"
EN: "I see... That's a shame. I didn't think you'd be so cold about it.<end>"
KO: "그런가…. 아쉽군. 의외로 담백한 성격이네….<end>"

## 1610121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>。この前話した<line-break>紺碧の洞窟の怪物退治の仕事だが…、　<line-break>　<line-break>あのあとでこれを引き受けた奴らが、<line-break>昨日尻尾巻いてキャンセルしに<line-break>来やがった。怪物が強すぎるんだと。<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Remember that job I mentioned, clearing the monster out of the Azure Cave? The lot who took it came crawling back yesterday to cancel. Said the monster was too strong for them.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>. 전에 말한 감청의 동굴 괴물 퇴치 일 말인데…. 그 뒤 이걸 맡은 녀석들이 어제 꼬리를 말고 취소하러 왔어. 괴물이 너무 강하다더군.<end>"

## 1610122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうだ？　無理にとは言わんが、<line-break>この仕事、お前がやってみないか？<end>"
EN: "What do you say? No pressure, but would you be willing to take the job?<end>"
KO: "어때? 억지로 권하진 않겠지만 이 일, 네가 해보지 않겠나?<end>"

## 1610125
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだろうな。お前ほどの冒険者なら<line-break>この仕事は問題無くこなせるはずだ。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃ、片付いたらまたここに来てくれ。<line-break>偉大な冒険者に報酬を渡すのを<line-break>楽しみにしてるよ。<end>"
EN: "I thought you might. An adventurer of your caliber should have no trouble with this job. Come back here once it's taken care of. I'll look forward to handing a great adventurer the reward.<end>"
KO: "그렇겠지. 너 정도의 모험자라면 이 일은 문제없이 해낼 거야. 그럼 끝나면 다시 여기로 와. 위대한 모험자에게 보수를 건네는 걸 기대하고 있을게.<end>"

## 1610126
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱり引き受けないのか…。まあ、<line-break>お前にも都合があるだろうからな。<line-break>　<line-break>なら、またしばらく他を当たるさ。<line-break>しかし、お前くらいなんだがな。<line-break>この仕事をこなせる冒険者は。<end>"
EN: "So you won't take it after all... Well, I'm sure you have your reasons. I'll ask around a while longer, then. Though you're about the only adventurer I know who could actually pull this off.<end>"
KO: "역시 안 맡는 건가…. 뭐, 너도 사정이 있겠지. 그럼 한동안 다른 사람을 알아보지. 하지만 이 일을 해낼 모험가는 너 정도밖에 없는데.<end>"

## 1610127

## 1610128
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ねえ、お願いだよ。鳳凰山で　　<line-break>鳳凰を見つけてきてよ！！<line-break>　<line-break>ぼくこのまんまじゃ、みんなに<line-break>うそつきって言われちゃうよ…。<line-break>頼むよ、ねえ！　おにいちゃん！<end>ねえ、お願いだよ。鳳凰山で　　<line-break>鳳凰を見つけてきてよ！！<line-break>　<line-break>ぼくこのまんまじゃ、みんなに<line-break>うそつきって言われちゃうよ…。<line-break>頼むよ、ねえ！　おねえちゃん！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Please, mister! Go find the phoenix on Phoenix Mountain! If this keeps up, everyone's going to call me a liar... Please! I'm begging you!<end>Please, miss! Go find the phoenix on Phoenix Mountain! If this keeps up, everyone's going to call me a liar... Please! I'm begging you!<end>"

## 1610132
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それ、ボクの今月のおこづかい全部。<line-break>ほうしゅう、いるんでしょ？<line-break>　<line-break>じゃ、お願いしたからね。<line-break>絶対、見つけてきてね！！<end>"

KO: "그거, 내 이번 달 용돈 전부야. 보수 필요하지? 그럼 부탁했어. 반드시 찾아와야 해!!<end>"

## 1610133
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "아니야! 거짓말 같은 게 아니라고! 봉황은 분명, 분명히 있어!!<end>"

## 1610134
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おお、<value:$28>！<line-break>風泣きの洞窟の怪物を倒したのか！？　<line-break>　<line-break>いやー、大したもんだよ。<line-break>さすが、俺が腕を見込んだだけのことは<line-break>あるってもんだな。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>! You defeated the monster in Wind-Wailing Cave?! That's quite a feat. I knew I was right about you.<end>"
KO: "오, <value:$28>! 바람우는 동굴의 괴물을 쓰러뜨린 거야!? 이야, 대단한데. 역시 내가 실력을 알아본 보람이 있군.<end>"

## 1610135
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが町のみんなからの報酬だ。<line-break>少ないかもしれんが、勘弁してくれよ。<end>"
EN: "Here's your reward from everyone in town. It may not be much, but I hope you'll understand.<end>"
KO: "이게 마을 사람 모두가 주는 보수다. 적을지도 모르지만 이해해 줘.<end>"

## 1610136
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それじゃ、ありがとうよ！<line-break>また仕事の方、よろしく頼むぜ。<end>"

KO: "그럼 고맙다! 또 일거리도 잘 부탁할게.<end>"

## 1610137
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あら、お疲れさま。その顔は<line-break>乙女の鏡の魔物を倒したって顔ね。<end>"

KO: "어머, 수고했어. 그 얼굴을 보니 소녀의 거울에 있던 마물을 쓰러뜨렸나 보네.<end>"

## 1610138
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありがとう、うれしいわ。それじゃあ<line-break>お礼、これくらいでいいかしら？<end>"
EN: "Thank you. I'm delighted. Now, will this do as a reward?<end>"
KO: "고마워, 기쁘네. 그럼 답례는 이 정도면 괜찮을까?<end>"

## 1610139
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그럼 난 이만 실례할게. 또 그 샘에 갈 수 있다고 생각하니 기대되네.<end>"

## 1610140

## 1610141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "す、すげえぇ！！　さすがはあの有名な<line-break><value:$28>だ！<line-break>　<line-break>助かったぁ！　これで俺も自分の<line-break>仕事を達成できる。<line-break>ありがとう。じゃあ！<end>"
EN: "Th-that's amazing!! No wonder <value:$28> is so famous! What a relief! Now I can get my own job done. Thanks! Bye!<end>"
KO: "대, 대단해!! 역시 그 유명한 <value:$28>다! 살았다! 이걸로 나도 내 일을 달성할 수 있어. 고마워. 그럼!<end>"

## 1610142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あわわ、お礼の話だよな？<line-break>わ、忘れてないぜ。大丈夫だ。<line-break>　<line-break>じゃあ、これ。俺の宝物だ。<end>"
EN: "O-oh! Your reward, right? I-I haven't forgotten. Don't worry. Here. It's my treasure.<end>"
KO: "아, 아앗, 보답 이야기지? 이, 잊지 않았어. 괜찮아. 자, 이거. 내 보물이야.<end>"

## 1610143
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "この宝玉は気絶した時に助けてくれる<line-break>ありがたいアクセサリらしい。<line-break>　<line-break>俺は気絶したことないから、実際には<line-break>どうなのかわからないけど、とにかく<line-break>身につけてみてくれ。俺の宝物だ。<end>"
EN: "Apparently, this orb is a handy accessory that helps if you get knocked out. I've never been knocked out myself, so I don't know if it actually works. Anyway, try wearing it. It's my treasure.<end>"
KO: "이 보옥은 기절했을 때 도와주는 고마운 액세서리래. 나는 기절한 적이 없어서 실제로 어떤지는 모르지만 일단 착용해 봐. 내 보물이야.<end>"

## 1610144
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、じゃあ！<end>"
EN: "W-well, bye!<end>"
KO: "그, 그럼!<end>"

## 1610145
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お疲れさま、<value:$28>。<line-break>無事に火山岩地帯の魔物を倒したって？<end>"
EN: "Nice work, <value:$28>. I hear you took care of the monster in the Volcanic Rock Zone?<end>"
KO: "수고했어, <value:$28>. 무사히 화산암 지대의 마물을 쓰러뜨렸다며?<end>"

## 1610146
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いや、なに、さっきこの前の神官様が<line-break>来てな、お礼を言って行ったんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>魔を打ち払ってくれた勇者によろしく、<line-break>だとさ。まったく、神官様ってのは<line-break>なんでもお見通しらしい。すごいね。<end>"
EN: "Well, that priest from the other day stopped by earlier to offer thanks. “Give my regards to the hero who drove back evil,” was the message. Honestly, priests seem to know everything. Amazing, isn't it?<end>"
KO: "아니, 조금 전에 전에 그 신관님이 와서 감사 인사를 하고 갔어. 마를 몰아내 준 용사에게 안부 전해 달라더군. 정말 신관님은 뭐든 다 아는 것 같아. 대단해.<end>"

## 1610147
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、その勇者様に報酬をお渡しせねば<line-break>なりませんな。<end>"
EN: "Now then, we must present our Hero with a reward.<end>"
KO: "자, 그 용사님께 보수를 드려야겠군요.<end>"

## 1610148
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当にお疲れさまだったな。<line-break>これからもよろしく頼むぜ、勇者様。<end>"
EN: "You really did a fine job. I'm counting on you from here on out, Hero.<end>"
KO: "정말 수고 많았어. 앞으로도 잘 부탁한다, 용자님.<end>"

## 1610149
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。そうか、<line-break>城塞都市跡の魔獣を倒したか…。　<line-break>　<line-break>これで、あいつも安心してあの世で<line-break>仲間たちと眠れるってもんだな…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... So you defeated the beast at the Fortress City Ruins. Now he can rest easy in the next world, alongside his companions.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 그렇군, 성채도시 유적의 마수를 쓰러뜨렸나…. 이제 그 녀석도 저승에서 동료들과 편히 잠들 수 있겠지….<end>"

## 1610150
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、お前は知らなかったんだな。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>あの男はお前に仕事を頼んだあと、<line-break>静かに息を引き取ったんだ。<line-break>すまなそうな、つらそうな顔で…。<end>"
EN: "You didn't know, then. After asking you to take the job, he quietly passed away. He looked so pained... so apologetic.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나, 넌 몰랐던 거군. 그 남자는 네게 일을 부탁한 뒤 조용히 숨을 거뒀어. 미안하고 괴로운 듯한 얼굴로…<end>"

## 1610151
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まあ、お前のおかげであいつも<line-break>浮かばれるだろうよ、きっとな。<end>"
EN: "Well, thanks to you, he can rest in peace now. I'm sure of it.<end>"
KO: "뭐, 네 덕분에 그 녀석도 분명 편히 눈을 감겠지.<end>"

## 1610152
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺からも礼を言わせてくれ。<line-break>ありがとう。<value:$28>。<line-break>　<line-break>これからもお前のその力で<line-break>人々の力になってやってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Let me thank you as well. Thank you, <value:$28>. Keep using that strength of yours to help people.<end>"
KO: "나도 감사하게 해 줘. 고맙다, <value:$28>. 앞으로도 그 힘으로 사람들에게 힘이 되어 줘.<end>"

## 1610153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>！<line-break>紺碧の洞窟の怪物を倒したのか！？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! You defeated the monster in the Azure Cave?!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>! 감청색 동굴의 괴물을 쓰러뜨린 거야!?<end>"

## 1610154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50そうか！！　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>さすがは<value:$2B>だ！<line-break>　<line-break>これで帝国のコロル石採掘作業も<line-break>もとどおりの軌道に乗れるだろう。<line-break>本当によくやってくれた。<end>そうか！！　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>さすがは<value:$28>だ！<line-break>　<line-break>これで帝国のコロル石採掘作業も<line-break>もとどおりの軌道に乗れるだろう。<line-break>本当によくやってくれた。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50So you did! Just what I'd expect from <value:$2B>! Now the Empire's Korol Stone mining operation can get back on track. You've done a fine job.<end>So you did! Just what I'd expect from <value:$28>! Now the Empire's Korol Stone mining operation can get back on track. You've done a fine job.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$2A><greater-equal>50그런가!! 역시 <value:$2B>답군! 이제 제국의 코롤석 채굴 작업도 원래 궤도로 돌아갈 수 있겠어. 정말 잘해 줬다.<end>그런가!! 역시 <value:$28>답군! 이제 제국의 코롤석 채굴 작업도 원래 궤도로 돌아갈 수 있겠어. 정말 잘해 줬다.<end>"

## 1610155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しかし、あの町外れの小僧が　　　　<line-break>ここまで立派になるとはな。<line-break>　<line-break>正直言って意外だが、それ以上に<line-break>お前の勇姿を見ることは嬉しいぞ。<line-break>いや、本当に大したもんだよ。<end>"
EN: "To think that kid from the outskirts of town would grow into someone this impressive. Honestly, I'm surprised, but more than that, I'm glad to see how far you've come. Truly, you're something else.<end>"
KO: "하지만 그 변두리 꼬마가 여기까지 훌륭해질 줄이야. 솔직히 의외지만, 그보다 네 늠름한 모습을 보는 게 더 기쁘군. 정말 대단해.<end>"

## 1610156
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、これが帝国からの報酬　　　　<line-break>５０００ギアだ。<line-break>　<line-break>いい仕事をしたな。<line-break>胸を張って受け取ってくれ。<end>"
EN: "Here, your reward from the Empire: 5,000 Gea. Fine work. Take it with pride.<end>"
KO: "자, 이게 제국에서 주는 보수 5000기어다. 좋은 일을 했어. 가슴 펴고 받아.<end>"

## 1610157
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これからもお前に期待してるぜ。　　　<line-break>がんばれよ、大冒険者<value:$28>！<end>"
EN: "I'm counting on you from here on out. Keep it up, great adventurer <value:$28>!<end>"
KO: "앞으로도 네게 기대할게. 힘내라, 대모험자 <value:$28>!<end>"

## 1610158
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0あ！　おにいちゃん！！<line-break>どうだった？　鳳凰いたでしょ！？<end>あ！　おねえちゃん！！<line-break>どうだった？　鳳凰いたでしょ！？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hey, mister! How did it go? The phoenix was there, wasn't it?<end>Hey, miss! How did it go? The phoenix was there, wasn't it?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0아! 오빠!! 어땠어? 봉황 있었지!?<end>아! 언니!! 어땠어? 봉황 있었지!?<end>"

## 1610159
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうなんだ…。<line-break>あの鳳凰、悪い鳳凰だったんだ…。<line-break>　<line-break>でも、確かにいたんだよね！？<line-break>うそじゃなかったんだよね？<end>"
EN: "Oh... So it was a bad phoenix... But it really was there, right? It wasn't a lie, was it?<end>"
KO: "그렇구나…. 그 봉황은 나쁜 봉황이었구나…. 그래도 정말 있었던 거지!? 거짓말이 아니었던 거지?<end>"

## 1610160
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そっか…。<end>"
EN: "Oh... Okay.<end>"
KO: "그렇구나….<end>"

## 1610161
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0自分で見れなかったのは残念だけど…、<line-break>でも、ありがとう！　おにいちゃん。<line-break>　<line-break>ぼくもいつかおにいちゃんみたいに<line-break>自分の力で世界を見てまわれるような<line-break>強い冒険者になるよ！！<end>自分で見れなかったのは残念だけど…、<line-break>でも、ありがとう！　おねえちゃん。<line-break>　<line-break>ぼくもいつかおねえちゃんみたいに<line-break>自分の力で世界を見てまわれるような<line-break>強い冒険者になるよ！！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0It's too bad I didn't get to see it myself... but thank you, mister! Someday I'll be a strong adventurer like you, traveling the world on my own!<end>It's too bad I didn't get to see it myself... but thank you, miss! Someday I'll be a strong adventurer like you, traveling the world on my own!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0직접 보지 못한 건 아쉽지만… 그래도 고마워! 오빠. 나도 언젠가 오빠처럼 내 힘으로 세상을 돌아볼 수 있는 강한 모험자가 될 거야!!<end>직접 보지 못한 건 아쉽지만… 그래도 고마워! 언니. 나도 언젠가 언니처럼 내 힘으로 세상을 돌아볼 수 있는 강한 모험자가 될 거야!!<end>"

## 1610162
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、鳳凰は本当にいたかい。<line-break>お前さん、本当にやさしいんだね。<end>"
EN: "So the phoenix really was there, was it? You really are kind.<end>"
KO: "그렇군, 봉황은 정말 있었나. 자네, 정말 상냥하군.<end>"

## 1610163
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "見るがよい…。人間どもにとって　　<line-break>お前は恐怖以外の何者でもない。<line-break>　<line-break>そこから生まれる、相容れないという<line-break>精神の呪縛…。<line-break>そして奴らは、お前を殺しに来る。<end>"
EN: "Behold... To humans, you are nothing but an object of terror. From that terror comes the curse of believing you can never coexist. And so they will come to kill you.<end>"
KO: "보아라…. 인간들에게 너는 공포 그 자체다. 거기서 생겨나는 서로 받아들일 수 없다는 정신의 속박…. 그리고 놈들은 너를 죽이러 온다.<end>"

## 1610164
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうじゃ。お前が奴らを消せばいい。<line-break>奴らがお前にすることを<line-break>ただ、やり返してやればいい。<line-break>そら、お前を殺そうと<line-break>凶悪な冒険者がやってきおった。<line-break>まずは奴らを血祭りにあげるのだ。<end>"
EN: "Yes. Wipe them out. You need only repay them in kind. Look―some vicious adventurers have come to kill you. Begin by slaughtering them.<end>"
KO: "그래. 네가 놈들을 없애면 된다. 놈들이 너에게 하려는 짓을 그대로 되돌려 주면 돼. 자, 너를 죽이려고 흉악한 모험가들이 왔다. 우선 녀석들을 피바다로 만들어라.<end>"

## 1610165
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ああ、今の祈りですか？<line-break>闇の力を浄化したのです。<line-break>念には念を、というところですか。<line-break>これで、闇がひとつ滅びましたね。<line-break>それでは、急ぐので失礼します。<line-break>またいつか会うこともあるでしょう。<end>"
EN: "Ah, you mean that prayer? I was purifying the power of darkness. One can never be too careful. And now, one more darkness has been destroyed. I'm afraid I must hurry on. Perhaps we'll meet again someday.<end>"
KO: "아, 방금 그 기도 말인가요? 어둠의 힘을 정화한 겁니다. 돌다리도 두드려 본다는 거죠. 이걸로 어둠 하나가 사라졌군요. 그럼 서두르고 있어서 이만 실례하겠습니다. 언젠가 다시 만날 일도 있겠죠.<end>"

## 1610166
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…待て。<line-break>貴様、名を名乗れ。<end>"
EN: "Wait. State your name.<end>"
KO: "…기다려. 네놈, 이름을 대라.<end>"

## 1610167
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の名…ですか？<line-break>私は…、　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>…私は、そう、仮面のしもべ…、<line-break>あのお方は私をサイフォスと<line-break>お呼びくださいます。<end>"
EN: "My name...? I am... I am, yes, a servant of the mask. The exalted one is gracious enough to call me Xiphos.<end>"
KO: "제 이름…입니까? 저는… 저는… 그래요, 가면의 종…. 그분은 저를 사이포스라 불러 주십니다.<end>"

## 1610168
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "サイフォス？<line-break>それが本当に貴様の名なのか？<end>"
EN: "Xiphos? Is that truly your name?<end>"
KO: "사이포스? 그게 정말 네 이름이냐?<end>"

## 1610169
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…では。<end>"
EN: "Well, then...<end>"
KO: "…그럼.<end>"

## 1610170
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て！<end>"
EN: "Wait!<end>"
KO: "기다려!<end>"

## 1610172
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうだな、<line-break>お前も、同じか。<end>"
EN: "Yes... You're the same, then.<end>"
KO: "…그렇군, 너도 같은 건가.<end>"

## 1610173
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よし、ゆくぞ。<end>"
EN: "Good. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "좋아, 가자.<end>"

## 1610174
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の名…ですか？<line-break>私は…　　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>私は…、そう、仮面のしもべ…、<line-break>あのお方は私をサイフォスと<line-break>お呼びくださいます。<end>"

## 1610176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いつもながら見事な手並みです。<line-break><value:$28>さん。<line-break>これも普段の鍛錬の賜物ですか？<end>"
EN: "Impressive work, as always, <value:$28>. Is that the fruit of your daily training?<end>"
KO: "언제 봐도 훌륭한 솜씨군요, <value:$28>님. 이것도 평소 단련의 성과인가요?<end>"

## 1610177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今、なんと…？<line-break>しゃべるゴブリンと戦い、勝利した？<line-break>そうおっしゃいましたか？<line-break>そうですか…。<line-break>フフ…あのお方も、お喜びに…。<end>"
EN: "What did you just say...? You fought a talking Goblin and won? Is that what you said? I see... Heh... The exalted one will be pleased...<end>"
KO: "…지금 뭐라고…? 말하는 고블린과 싸워 이겼다고요? 그렇게 말씀하셨습니까? 그렇군요…. 후후… 그분도 기뻐하시겠군….<end>"

## 1610178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんのつもりです？<line-break>あまり感心できる動きではないですね。<end>"
EN: "What do you think you're doing? I can't say I approve of that move.<end>"

## 1610179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml

## 1610181
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610183
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…今は、特に、<line-break>お前に言うことはない。　　<line-break>　<line-break>…だが、おそらく状況は<line-break>お前の考えているとおりだ。<end>"

## 1610184
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、この祈りが無意味に見えますか？<line-break>念には念…というところです。<line-break>　<line-break>おかしいですか？<line-break>そちらの方はまた不審そうに<line-break>私のことを見ておられるようですが。<end>"
EN: "Heh. Does this prayer seem pointless to you? One can never be too careful. Do you find that strange? That person there seems to be eyeing me suspiciously again.<end>"
KO: "후후, 이 기도가 의미 없어 보이십니까? 만전을 기한다는 거죠. 이상합니까? 그쪽 분은 또 수상하다는 듯 저를 보고 계신 것 같군요.<end>"

## 1610185
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610186
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…またですか。<line-break>ですから、いったい何をです？<line-break>私にはあなたの方こそ理解できません。<end>"

KO: "…또입니까. 그러니까 대체 무엇을 말하는 겁니까? 저로서는 오히려 당신을 이해할 수 없습니다.<end>"

## 1610187
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그럼, <value:$28>님. 다시 만날 날을 기대하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1610188
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610189

## 1610190
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "念には念…ということです。<line-break>フフ、我ながら、律儀なものです…。<line-break>　<line-break>ではまた。またお会いできる日を　　<line-break>楽しみにしています。<end>"
EN: "One can never be too careful. Heh. I must say, I am nothing if not conscientious... Until next time. I look forward to seeing you again.<end>"
KO: "돌다리도 두드려 보라는 거죠. 후후, 저도 참 융통성 없군요…. 그럼 또 뵙죠. 다시 만날 날을 기대하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1610191
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、こいつも、実は<line-break>かわいそうな奴なのかも、な。<line-break>　<line-break>んじゃ、な。<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>また、会おうぜ。<end>"

## 1610192
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610193

## 1610195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml

## 1610198

## 1610199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フッ<line-break>相手を見て突っかかるのだな。　<line-break>　<line-break>無謀な挑戦は、自分だけではなく<line-break>周りの人間にも迷惑がかかる。<line-break>…覚えておけ。<end>"

## 1610200

## 1610202

## 1610203

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610205

## 1610207

## 1610209

## 1610210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ…、自分が何者なのか<line-break>わかっている人間などいない。<line-break>　<line-break>だが、お前が<line-break>何者かは教えてやる。<line-break>役立たずだ。<end>"

KO: "흥…. 자신이 무엇인지 아는 인간은 없어. 하지만 네가 무엇인지는 알려 주지. 쓸모없는 녀석이다.<end>"

## 1610211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml

## 1610213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁーにぃ！？　この下等生物は！？<line-break>生意気よ！　生意気よ！　生意気よお！<end>"

## 1610214
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1610215
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "無知なわがままエルフぅ？　よくって？<line-break>アタクシは１６００年も生きてるの…！<line-break>　<line-break>１００年も生きていない下等生物に<line-break>無知呼ばわりされるようなことなんて<line-break>絶対、絶対、絶対なくってよぉおお！<end>"
EN: "An ignorant, selfish Elf? Listen here: I've lived for 1,600 years! There is absolutely, absolutely, absolutely no way an inferior creature who has not even lived a hundred years can call me ignorant!<end>"
KO: "무지하고 제멋대로인 엘프으? 잘 들어! 나는 1600년이나 살았어…! 100년도 못 산 하등생물에게 무지하다는 소리를 들을 일은 절대, 절대, 절대 없어어어!<end>"

## 1610216
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そ、そうね。　　　　　　　　　　　<line-break>コホン。<line-break>　<line-break>高貴で優雅なエルフであるアタクシが<line-break>こぉんな下等生物に本気で怒るなんて…<line-break>ほんの少し、はしたないことだったわ。<end>"
EN: "...Y-yes, you're right. Ahem. It was just a little undignified for a noble, elegant Elf like me to get seriously angry at such an inferior creature...<end>"
KO: "…그, 그렇지. 흠흠. 고귀하고 우아한 엘프인 내가 이런 하등생물에게 진심으로 화를 내다니… 아주 조금 품위 없는 일이었어.<end>"

## 1610217
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…では、行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "Come. We're leaving.<end>"
KO: "…그럼, 간다.<end>"

## 1610218
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ、<value:$28>、<line-break>また会えてうれしいわ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>でも、とっても残念。<line-break>約束を果たせそうもないわ…。　<end>"
EN: "Hee hee. <value:$28>, I'm so glad to see you again. But what a shame. It seems I won't be able to keep my promise...<end>"
KO: "후후, <value:$28>, 다시 만나서 기뻐. 하지만 정말 아쉽네. 약속을 지키긴 어려울 것 같아….<end>"

## 1610219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私、あなたと一緒にいる人が邪魔なの。<line-break>だから、あなただけを、じっくりと<line-break>殺してあげられないの…。<end>"
EN: "The person with you is in my way. So I can't have you all to myself and take my time killing you...<end>"
KO: "난 당신과 함께 있는 사람들이 거슬려. 그래서 당신만 천천히 죽여 줄 수가 없잖아….<end>"

## 1610220
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "貴様が…やはり…！<end>"
EN: "You... I knew it!<end>"
KO: "네놈이… 역시…!<end>"

## 1610221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "本当に残念だわ。<line-break>でも、これがふたりの運命だったのよ。<line-break>　<line-break>あなたの苦痛の記憶はあきらめるわ。<line-break>…じゃ、これ以上ここにいると<line-break>別れがつらいから、失礼するわね。<end>"
EN: "It truly is a shame. But this was always to be our fate. I'll have to give up on the memory of your suffering. Well, if I stay any longer, parting will only grow harder, so I'll take my leave.<end>"
KO: "정말 아쉽네. 하지만 이게 두 사람의 운명이었던 거야. 당신의 고통스러운 기억은 포기할게. …그럼 더 있으면 헤어지기 힘들 테니 먼저 갈게.<end>"

## 1610222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "待て！<end>"
EN: "Wait!<end>"
KO: "기다려!<end>"

## 1610223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しつこいわ、セラ。<line-break>もういいかげん大人になって…。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>私も、もう大人なの。<line-break>あなたの保護はいらないわ。<line-break>…新しい保護者もできたしね。くすっ。<end>"
EN: "You're so persistent, Sera. It's time you grew up. I'm an adult now, too. I don't need your protection anymore. Besides, I've got a new protector now. Hee hee.<end>"
KO: "끈질기네, 세라. 이제 그만 어른이 돼…. 나도 이제 어른이야. 네 보호는 필요 없어. …새 보호자도 생겼고. 후훗.<end>"

## 1610224

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "네놈이이이!<end>"

## 1610225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "最後にあなたたちを元気にしてあげる。<line-break>そうして、より多くの苦痛を感じながら<line-break>死んで行ってちょうだい。くすすっ。<end>"

JP: "…俺も<line-break>お前と一緒に行かせてもらおう。<line-break>　<line-break>俺の名は、セラだ。<end>"
EN: "I'll come with you. My name is Sera.<end>"
KO: "…나도 너와 함께 가겠다. 내 이름은 세라다.<end>"

## 1610230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前に断る権利はない。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>そしてここにはもう用はない。<line-break>行くぞ。<end>"
EN: "You have no right to refuse. There's nothing left for us here. Let's go.<end>"
KO: "네게 거절할 권리는 없다. 그리고 여긴 이제 볼일 없다. 간다.<end>"

## 1610231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言っておくが<line-break>お前に断る権利はない。<end>"
EN: "Let me make this clear: you have no right to refuse.<end>"
KO: "말해 두지만, 네게 거절할 권리는 없다.<end>"

## 1610232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、猫屋敷…という所で　　　　　<line-break>呼び出すのだな。<line-break>　<line-break>役立たずどもをパーティーから外して、<line-break>さっさと俺を加えることだな。<line-break>俺より優れた者などいない。<end>"
EN: "So Cat Manor is where I can be summoned. Remove those useless fools from the party and add me at once. No one surpasses me.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 고양이 저택이라는 곳에서 부르는 거군. 쓸모없는 녀석들을 파티에서 빼고 얼른 나를 넣어라. 나보다 뛰어난 자는 없어.<end>"

## 1610233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺が、ただひとり認めた<line-break>あの男をのぞいてはな。<end>"
EN: "Except for that man―the only one I have ever acknowledged.<end>"
KO: "…내가 유일하게 인정한 그 남자를 제외하면 말이지.<end>"

## 1610234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ま、腹を立てなさんなって。<line-break>世の中にはああいう奴もいる。　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>腕も結構、立つみたいだしな。<line-break>不器用でかわいらしいじゃないか？<end>"
EN: "Now, don't get so angry. There are people like that in the world. He seems quite capable, too. And isn't that awkwardness of his rather endearing?<end>"
KO: "뭐, 화내지 마. 세상엔 저런 녀석도 있어. 실력도 꽤 되는 모양이고. 서툴러서 귀엽잖아?<end>"

## 1610235
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あれはあれで結構つらい生き方だぜ？<line-break>理解してやれ、とまでは言わないが<line-break>少なくとも拒絶はしないでやるんだな。<end>"
EN: "It's a hard way to live, being like that. I won't ask you to understand him, but at least don't reject him outright.<end>"
KO: "그것도 그것대로 꽤 괴로운 삶이야. 이해해주라고까지는 안 하겠지만, 적어도 거부하지는 말아줘.<end>"

## 1610236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか。<line-break>そんじゃ、俺たちも行こうか。<end>"
EN: "I see. Well then, shall we get going too?<end>"
KO: "그래. 그럼 우리도 갈까.<end>"

## 1610237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぁにぃ？　あの不作法な男は？<line-break>自分がド下等生物だってことを　　　　<line-break>わきまえてるのかしら？<line-break>なんでも強引に、勝手に決めちゃって。<line-break>秘密の臭いだけプンプンさせちゃって。<line-break>ちょっと強いからっていい気になって！<end>"
EN: "What is with that ill-mannered man? Does he even realize he's the lowest of lower life-forms? Always forcing his way and deciding everything on his own! Reeking of secrets! Getting conceited just because he's a little strong!<end>"
KO: "뭐야아? 저 무례한 남자는? 자기가 최하등 생물이라는 걸 알고나 있는 거야? 뭐든 억지로 자기 멋대로 정해 버리고. 비밀 냄새만 잔뜩 풍기고. 좀 강하다고 잘난 척은!<end>"

## 1610238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "絶対、絶対、絶対、絶対、絶対、絶対、<line-break>気に入らないわぁー！<end>"
EN: "I absolutely, absolutely, absolutely, absolutely, absolutely, absolutely cannot stand him!<end>"
KO: "절대, 절대, 절대, 절대, 절대, 절대 마음에 안 들어어어!<end>"

## 1610239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェティをなだめる<end>"
EN: "Calm Fety down<end>"
KO: "페티를 달랜다<end>"

## 1610240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フェティをしかる<end>"
EN: "Scold Fety<end>"
KO: "페티를 꾸짖는다<end>"

## 1610241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "甘いわ！　あなた、同じ人間族だから<line-break>あの無礼者をかばってるんじゃなくて？<end>"
EN: "You're too soft! Aren't you defending that insolent wretch just because you're both human?<end>"
KO: "무르네! 당신, 같은 인간족이라고 그 무례한 자를 감싸는 거 아냐?<end>"

## 1610242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そうね。確かに<line-break>あなたは種族で差別はしないわ。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>それで、高貴なエルフと<line-break>他のド下等生物と同じ扱いにするのには<line-break>ほんと、アタクシも閉口しているもの。<end>"
EN: "Well... yes. You certainly don't discriminate by race. In fact, I'm thoroughly exasperated by the way you treat noble Elves exactly the same as all those other utterly inferior creatures.<end>"
KO: "…그렇네. 확실히 당신은 종족으로 차별하진 않아. 그래서 고귀한 엘프와 다른 하등생물을 똑같이 취급하는 건 정말 나도 질색이지만.<end>"

## 1610243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…わかったわ。あなたがそう言うなら、<line-break>５６７０００００００歩譲って<line-break>一応アタクシも納得してさしあげるわ。<end>"
EN: "Very well. If you insist, I'll concede 5,670,000,000 steps and deign to accept your argument―for the moment.<end>"
KO: "…알았어. 네가 그렇게 말한다면 56억 걸음 양보해서 일단 나도 납득해 주겠어.<end>"

## 1610244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28><line-break>どうして、アタクシをしかるわけ？<line-break>この場合、おかしいのはあの男よー！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, why are you scolding me? He's the one in the wrong here!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 왜 나를 혼내는 거야? 이 경우 이상한 건 그 남자잖아ー!<end>"

## 1610245
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちゃんとした事情も語らないくせに　　<line-break>こっちが何か尋ねようとすると怒る。<line-break>　<line-break>強引に、勝手にすべてのことを決定して<line-break>アタクシたちのことをよく知らないのに<line-break>侮辱するようなことばかり言うのよー？<end>"
EN: "He won't even give us a proper explanation, yet he gets angry whenever we try to ask him anything. He forces through every decision entirely on his own, and even though he barely knows us, all he does is insult us!<end>"
KO: "제대로 된 사정도 말하지 않으면서 이쪽이 뭔가 물어보면 화를 내. 억지로 제멋대로 모든 걸 결정하고 우리를 잘 알지도 못하면서 모욕적인 말만 한다고오!<end>"

## 1610246
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたのアタクシへの非難は不当だわ。<line-break>納得いかない、絶対、絶対、絶対、<line-break>絶対に納得いかないわー！<end>"
EN: "Your criticism of me is unjustified. I won't accept it. Absolutely, absolutely, absolutely, absolutely not!<end>"
KO: "당신이 이 몸을 비난하는 건 부당해! 납득 못 해, 절대, 절대, 절대, 절대로 납득 못 해애!<end>"

## 1610247
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姉ちゃん、姉ちゃん。<line-break>あのセラって奴さ、どお？　シュミ？<end>"
EN: "Sis, hey, Sis. What do you think of Sera? Your type?<end>"
KO: "누나, 누나. 그 세라라는 녀석 말이야, 어때? 취향이야?<end>"

## 1610248
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いったあ！　すぐ殴るのやめろよ！<line-break>頭、悪くなるだろ！　もう！<end>"
EN: "Ow! Quit being so quick to hit me! You'll make me stupid! Come on!<end>"
KO: "아파아! 바로 때리는 것 좀 그만해! 머리 나빠지잖아! 정말!<end>"

## 1610249
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "チャカは少し頭が悪くなった。　<line-break>ＩＮＴが１下がった。<line-break>　<line-break>チャカは少し打たれ強くなった。<line-break>ＶＩＴが１上がった。<end>"
EN: "Chaka got a little dumber. ＩＮＴ decreased by 1. Chaka got a little better at taking hits. ＶＩＴ increased by 1.<end>"
KO: "차카는 조금 머리가 나빠졌다. INT가 1 내려갔다. 차카는 조금 더 맷집이 좋아졌다. VIT가 1 올랐다.<end>"

## 1610250
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "オッシ先生が言ってたわ。<line-break>実力があっても、かえってそれで<line-break>目をふさがれて、自滅するって。<line-break>彼ってそんな感じかしら？<line-break>なんにせよ、すごい自信よね。<end>"
EN: "Master Ossi said that even real ability can blind you and lead you to your own ruin. Do you think he's like that? In any case, that's quite some confidence.<end>"
KO: "오시 선생님이 그러셨어. 실력이 있어도 오히려 그것 때문에 눈이 가려져 자멸한다고. 그 사람도 그런 느낌일까? 어쨌든 대단한 자신감이네.<end>"

## 1610251
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なんか、かっこいいよね。セラってさ。<line-break>ちょっと、気になるかな。<line-break>　<line-break>…なーんてボクが言ったら<line-break><value:$28>は気になる？<end>"
EN: "Sera's kind of cool, don't you think? I might be a little interested. If I said that, would it bother you, <value:$28>?<end>"
KO: "세라, 좀 멋있지 않아? 조금 신경 쓰이네. …내가 이렇게 말하면 <value:$28>은 신경 쓰여?<end>"

## 1610252
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ウソウソ、冗談だよ！<line-break>ね、ね、こっち向いてよ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, no, I was kidding! Come on, look at me!<end>"
KO: "아, 거짓말 거짓말, 농담이야! 자, 자, 이쪽 좀 봐!<end>"

## 1610253
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、ボクのことからかってたんだ！ <line-break>ひどいよ、<value:$28>。<line-break>ボク…本気で嫌われたと思ったんだよ！<line-break>もう、知らないよー！<line-break>べーだ！<end>"
EN: "Oh, you were teasing me! That's mean, <value:$28>. I really thought you hated me! I don't care anymore! Bleh!<end>"
KO: "아, 날 놀린 거였구나! 너무해, <value:$28>. 나… 진짜 미움받은 줄 알았잖아! 이제 몰라! 메롱이다!<end>"

## 1610254
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ボク、やっぱり役立たずなのかな。<end>"
EN: "Maybe I really am useless.<end>"
KO: "…나, 역시 쓸모없는 걸까.<end>"

## 1610255
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "セラは…純粋だね。<line-break>何も知らないってことは幸せなんだ。<line-break>それに、そう、いいことだよ。<end>"
EN: "Sera is... innocent. There's happiness in knowing nothing. And... yes. That's a good thing.<end>"
KO: "세라는… 순수하네. 아무것도 모른다는 건 행복한 일이야. 그리고 그래, 좋은 일이기도 하지.<end>"

## 1610256
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人は何かを追い求めるとき　　　　　　<line-break>他のものを見失う。<line-break>…あの男はそういう男だ。<line-break>また、他の物が見える男より<line-break>他の物が見えなくなる男の方が<line-break>求めるべき物だけを見ることができる。<end>"
EN: "When people pursue something, they lose sight of everything else. That's the kind of man he is. A man who can no longer see anything else can focus solely on what he ought to seek, unlike one who still sees other things.<end>"
KO: "사람은 무언가를 좇을 때 다른 것을 놓친다. …그 남자는 그런 사람이야. 그리고 다른 것도 보이는 남자보다 다른 것이 보이지 않게 되는 남자가 자신이 추구해야 할 것만 바라볼 수 있지.<end>"

## 1610258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうなさったのですか？　　　　　<line-break>闇の怪物を放っておくのですか？<line-break>　<line-break>すみませんが、私も急いでいます。<line-break>回復してさしあげますので<line-break>闇の者のもとへと急ぎましょう。<end>"
EN: "What is it? Are you going to leave that dark monster unchecked? I'm sorry, but I'm in a hurry too. I'll restore your strength, so let's make haste to the creature of darkness.<end>"
KO: "무슨 일이십니까? 어둠의 괴물을 내버려 두실 겁니까? 죄송하지만 저도 서두르고 있습니다. 회복시켜 드릴 테니 어둠의 자가 있는 곳으로 서두르죠.<end>"

## 1610259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、行きましょう。<end>"
EN: "Then let's be on our way.<end>"
KO: "그럼 가죠.<end>"

## 1610260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？　どした？<line-break>体勢立て直すために出直すのかい？<line-break>　<line-break>んじゃ、俺は俺で、またしばらく<line-break>ブラブラ冒険してるとするかな。<line-break>じゃな。<end>"
EN: "Hm? What's up? Heading back to regroup? Well, I guess I'll carry on roaming around and adventuring for a while, then. See you.<end>"
KO: "응? 왜 그래? 태세를 정비하려고 다시 나가는 거야? 그럼 난 또 한동안 여기저기 모험이나 하러 다닐까. 그럼.<end>"

## 1610261
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec161-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やっぱりそうなのね。噂は聞いてるわ。<line-break>ねえ、ちょっと仕事をお願いしたいの。<line-break>頼めて？<end>"
EN: "I thought so. I've heard the rumors. Say, I have a little job I'd like you to do. Will you take it?<end>"
KO: "역시 그렇구나. 소문은 들었어. 있잖아, 일 하나 부탁하고 싶은데. 맡아 줄래?<end>"

## 1610271

## 1620000

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: ""
